"0","There were some crows who flocked together in a court-yard upon a tree and cawed stretching their necks.","yǒu yā jí tíng shù。 yǐn jǐng ér míng。","有鴉集庭樹。引頸而鳴。"
"1","A boy hooted at them.","ér chì zhī。","兒叱之。"
"2","His father asked, ""What is the harm in this?","fù yuē。 shì hé hài。","父曰。是何害。"
"3","The boy said, ""I have often heard people say that when a magpie chatters, it brings good luck, and when a crow caws, it brings bad luck. Today the crows have been cawing, therefore I have hooted at them.","ér yuē。 cháng wén rén yán。 què míng jí。 yā míng xiōng。 jīn míng zhě yā yě。 gù chì zhī。","兒曰。常聞人言。鵲鳴吉。鴉鳴凶。今鳴者鴉也。故叱之。"
"4","The father said, ""Man's knowledge and experience are much higher than those of birds, yet he cannot foresee good luck and ill luck. Still more the birds are not able to do it.","fù yuē。 rén zhī zhì zhì。 yuǎn shèng yú niǎo。 shàng bù néng yù zhī jí xiōng。 ér kuàng niǎo hū。","父曰。人之智識。遠勝於鳥。尚不能預知吉凶。而況鳥乎。"
"5","Flocked together upon a tree in a court-yard.","jí tíng shù","集庭樹"
"6","to act contrary to right.","bèi lǐ ér xíng","背理而行"
"7","to get without thinking of it.","bù sī ér dé","不思而得"
"8","to govern without severity.","bù yán ér zhì","不嚴而治"
"9","those who know do not speak; those who speak do not know.","zhī zhě bù yán， yán zhě bù zhī yě","知者不言，言者不知也"
"10","those who know it are not equal to those who love it.","zhī zhī zhě， bù rú hào zhī zhě","知之者，不如好之者"
"11","those who have virtue are certain to be able to speak; those who can speak are not certain to have virtue.","yǒu dé zhě， bì yǒu yán， yǒu yán zhě， bù bì yǒu dé","有德者，必有言，有言者，不必有德"

"22","A boy was passing under a veranda, and it seemed to him that somebody was following him.","ér xíng láng xià。 sì yǒu yī rén suí qí hòu。","兒行廊下。似有一人隨其後。"
"23","The boy did not dare to look back.","bù gǎn huí gù。","不敢回顧。"
"24","Hurriedly he entered the house, and told his elder sister.","jí rù gào jiě。","急入告姊。"
"25","His sister said, ""It is the shadow of your body.","zǐ yuē。 cǐ rǔ shēn zhī yǐng yě。","姊曰。此汝身之影也。"
"26","When you are standing before a lamp, or going under the sun, there always is a shadow.","rǔ lì dèng qián。 xíng rì xià。 jiē yǒu yǐng。","汝立燈前。行日下。皆有影。"

"51","There was a tiger in the mountains who devoured men and cattle.","shān yǒu hǔ hài rén chù。","山有虎害人畜。"
"52","Hunters planned to catch him, and laid a pit-fall in order to waylay him.","liè rén móu bǔ hǔ。 shè jǐng yǐ sì zhī。","獵人謀捕虎。設阱以伺之。"
"53","Upon a wall near by they wrote in big characters as follows, ""Here a pit-fall for a tiger is laid. Let passers-by be careful"".","dà shū qiáng shàng yuē。 xià yǒu hǔ jǐng。 xíng rén zhǐ bù。","大書牆上曰。下有虎阱。行人止步。"
"54","A villager who was an illiterate man was passing under that wall.","xiāng rén bù shí zì zhě。 guò qiáng xià。","鄉人不識字者。過牆下。"
"55","By mistake he trod on the pit-fall, fell into it and hurt his leg.","wù cù zhī。 zhuì jǐng shāng zú。","誤蹴之。墜阱傷足。"
"56","He shouted loudly for help.","dà hū qiú yuán。","大呼求援。"
"57","When he was dragged out, somebody pointed to the inscription on the wall and told him about it.","jì chū。 rén zhǐ qiáng shàng shū gào zhī。","既出。人指牆上書告之。"
"58","The villager then said with a sigh, ""If I had been able to read, how could this have happened to me!","nǎi tàn yuē。 wú gǒu zhī shū。 qǐ zhì cǐ zāi。","乃嘆曰。吾苟知書。豈至此哉。"

"71","A crow was very thirsty.","lǎo yā kě shèn。","老鴉渴甚。"
"72","She saw in front of a house a pot containing some water and wished to drink it.","jiàn tíng qián yǒu shuǐ hú。 yù yǐn zhī。","見庭前有水壺。欲飲之。"
"73","But the pot was deep, and the water in it was shallow.","hú shēn shuǐ qiǎn。","壺深水淺。"
"74","The crow tried to stretch her beak as much as she could, but still could not get the water.","yā jié lì shēn huì。 zú bù dé shuǐ。","鴉竭力伸喙。卒不得水。"
"75","Then she raised her head as if she were thinking.","yǎng shǒu ruò yǒu sī。","仰首若有思。"
"76","Suddenly she flew away and came back carrying a stone in her beak which she dropped into the pot.","hū fēi qù。 xián shí zhì。 tóu hú zhōng。","忽飛去。銜石至。投壺中。"
"77","Thus she went and came back more than ten times.","wǎng fǎn shí yú cì。","往返十餘次。"

"90","Suddenly his son ran up in haste and cried, ""Our house has been robbed by somebody, and the household effects are all gone!","hū qí zǐ bēn gào yuē。 wú jiā wéi rén suǒ jié。 jiā jù jǐn sàng。","忽其子奔告曰。吾家為人所劫。家具盡喪。"

"173","(One of his) advisers said, admonishing him, ""Have you not heard about the sea-monster, sir?","kè jiàn yuē。 jūn bù wén hǎi dà yú hū。","客諫曰。君不聞海大魚乎。"
"174","A net cannot stop it, and it is impossible to haul it with a hook.","wǎng bù néng zhǐ。 gōu bù néng qiān。","網不能止。鉤不能牽。"
"175","(But if) unfortunately it is out of the water, mole-crickets and ants would then (easily) master (it).","bù xìng shī shuǐ。 zé lóu yǐ zhì yān。","不幸失水。則螻蟻制焉。"
"176","Your presence in Ch'i is similar to the abode of the sea-monster in water.","jūn zhī zài qí。 yóu yú zhī zài shuǐ yě。","君之在齊。猶魚之在水也。"
"177","If you govern Ch'i in the proper way, Ch'i will become strong, and what sorrow could you have?","jūn gǒu shàn zhì qí。 qí qiáng。 jūn yòu hé yōu yān。","君苟善治齊。齊強。君又何憂焉。"
"178","(But) if one day Ch'i perishes, (then) even if you should build the walls of Hsüeh higher than the sky, still it would not be of any use to you.","ruò yī dàn qí wáng。 suī zhù xuē zhī chéng。 shǐ gāo yú tiān。 yóu zhī wú yì yě。","若一旦齊亡。雖築薛之城。使高於天。猶之無益也。"
"179","Good,"" said Ching Kuo-chün, and he did not build the walls in Hsüeh.","jìng guō jūn yuē。 shàn。 nǎi bù chéng xuē。","靖郭君曰。善。乃不城薛。"
"180","A traveller hired a donkey and went on a long journey.","xíng rén lìn lǘ ér xíng yuǎn。","行人賃驢而行遠。"
"181","The day just happened to be (very) hot.","tiān fāng shǔ。","天方暑。"
"182","(The traveller and the donkey-boy) looked for shade, (but) could not find (it).","mì yīn mò dé。","覓蔭莫得。"
"183","Then (they) crept under the donkey's belly in order to get shelter from the sun.","nǎi fú yú lǘ fù zhī xià yǐ bì rì。","乃伏於驢腹之下以避日。"
"184","But the donkey's belly could shelter only one man, and (therefore) the donkey-boy and the traveller (began to) quarrel about it.","rán lǘ fù jǐn bì yī rén。 lǘ fū yǔ xíng rén zhēng zhī。","然驢腹僅蔽一人。驢夫與行人爭之。"
"185","The donkey-boy said, ""I have hired out my donkey to you, but did not hire his shadow.","lǘ fū yuē。 wú lìn jūn lǘ。 bù lìn jūn yǐng。","驢夫曰。吾賃君驢。不賃君影。"
"186","The traveller said, ""I have hired your donkey with money, and therefore his shadow also belongs to me.","xíng rén yuē。 wú yǐ qián lìn lǘ。 zé yǐng yì shǔ wǒ。","行人曰。吾以錢賃驢。則影亦屬我。"
"187","(They both) quarreled without yielding to each other, and came to blows under the donkey.","yǔ bù xiāng ràng。 dòu yú lǘ xià。","語不相讓。鬥於驢下。"
"188","The donkey took fright and ran away.","lǘ jīng ér yì。","驢驚而逸。"
"189","The traveller and the donkey-boy rushed after him but could not catch him, and returned in low spirits.","xíng rén yǔ lǘ fū gòng zhuī zhī。 bù huò。 ào sàng ér guī。","行人與驢夫共追之。不獲。懊喪而歸。"
"190","For this reason it is said: ""He who quarrels about nothing may lose something substantial.","gù yuē。 zhēng xū zhě sàng qí shí。","故曰。爭虛者喪其實。"

"223","Plants appeared upon the earth earlier than movable beings.","wù lèi zhī shēng yě。 zhí wù xiān yú dòng wù。","物類之生也。植物先於動物。"
"224","Hence in remotest antiquity there were only thick forests everywhere.","gù shàng gǔ zhī shí。 sēn lín dú duō。","故上古之時。森林獨多。"
"225","In the course of time after transformations of the surface of the earth, those forests were submerged and buried within the earth, and there, under the continuous pressure they changed into coal, lying between layers of sand, mud and earth.","hòu jīng líng gǔ biàn qiān。 yān mò qí zhōng。 jī yā jì jiǔ。 suì chéng wéi méi。 yǔ shā ní tǔ xiāng jiān。","後經陵谷變遷。湮沒其中。積壓既久。遂成為煤。與沙泥土相間。"

"228","It is brittle, easily splits and possesses the quality of durable combustion out of comparison with fire-wood.","zhì cuì yì suì。 xìng nài jiǔ rán。 fēi chái xīn suǒ kě bǐ。","質脆易碎。性耐久燃。非柴薪所可比。"

"232","Magnificent natural resources we do not exploit ourselves, but wait till others do it for us, and thus we allow the source of immense profit to be monopolized by foreigners.","fú tiān rán zhī měi lì。 wǒ bù zì kāi。 ér dài rén wéi wǒ kāi zhī。 suì shǐ jué dà lì yuán。 wéi wài guó suǒ lǒng duàn。","夫天然之美利。我不自開。而待人為我開之。遂使絕大利源。為外國所壟斷。"
"233","It is deplorable indeed!","chéng kě tòng yě。","誠可痛也。"
"234","A peasant of the Sung State when ploughing found a piece of jade. He offered it to protector of the town Tzu-han.","sòng zhī yě rén。 gēng ér dé yù。 xiàn zhī sī chéng zǐ hǎn。","宋之野人。耕而得玉。獻之司城子罕。"
"235","But Tzu-han refused to accept it.","zǐ hǎn bù shòu。","子罕不受。"
"236","The peasant said, ""I showed it to a jeweller and he acknowledged it a gem. Therefore I dared to offer it to you"".","yě rén yuē。 yǐ shì yù rén。 yù rén yǐ wéi bǎo yě。 gù gǎn xiàn zhī。","野人曰。以示玉人。玉人以為寶也。故敢獻之。"
"237","You take jade for a gem,"" replied Tzu-han, ""but I take integrity for a gem. If you give this jade to me, we both would lose our gems. Better if each of us keeps his gem for himself.","zǐ hǎn yuē。 zǐ yǐ yù wéi bǎo。 wǒ yǐ bù tān wéi bǎo。 ruò yǔ wǒ。 jiē sàng bǎo yě。 bù ruò gè yǒu qí bǎo。","子罕曰。子以玉為寶。我以不貪為寶。若與我。皆喪寶也。不若各有其寶。"
"238","Therefore the elders of the Sung State used to say, ""Tzu-han knows what thing the gem is; only his gem is different.","gù sòng guó zhī zhǎng zhě yuē。 zǐ hǎn fēi bù zhī bǎo yě。 suǒ bǎo zhě yì yě。","故宋國之長者曰。子罕非不知寶也。所寶者異也。"
"239","If we take a hundred ounces of silver and a millet cake and offer to a child, he certainly will take the cake.","jīn yǐ bǎi jīn yǔ tuán shǔ yǐ shì xiǎo ér。 xiǎo ér bì qǔ tuán shǔ yǐ。","今以百金與摶黍以示小兒。小兒必取摶黍矣。"
"240","If we offer a Ho-shih jade and a hundred ounces of silver to an uncultured man, he will surely take silver.","yǐ hé shì zhī bì yǔ bǎi jīn。 yǐ shì bǐ rén。 bǐ rén bì qǔ bǎi jīn yǐ。","以龢氏之璧與百金。以示鄙人。鄙人必取百金矣。"

"242","Those whose tastes are coarse ask for primitive things, and those whose tastes are refined ask for refined things.","qí zhī mí jīng。 qí suǒ qǔ mí jīng。 qí zhī mí cū。 qí suǒ qǔ mí cū。","其知彌精。其所取彌精。其知彌粗。其所取彌粗。"

"290","The father said, ""When water vapors, about to be transformed into rain drops, are suddenly exposed to a cold wind in the high zones of the atmosphere, they instantly congeal into pieces of ice which fall down in disorder.","fù yuē。 kōng zhōng shuǐ qì jiāng chéng yǔ diǎn。 hū wéi gāo chù lěng fēng suǒ chuī。 zhòu jié bīng kuài。 cuò zá xià jiàng。","父曰。空中水汽將成雨點。忽為高處冷風所吹。驟結冰塊。錯雜下降。"

"447","When Duke Po of the Ch'u State raised a rebellion, Chuang-shan bade farewell to his mother intending to go to the place of the rebellion and to die because of it.","chǔ bái gōng zuò luàn。 zhuāng shàn cí qí mǔ。 jiāng wǎng sǐ zhī。","楚白公作亂。莊善辭其母。將往死之。"

"565","I avail myself of this opportunity to express to you my best wishes.","cǐ dá。 shùn sòng rì qí。","此達。順頌日祺。"
"566","I beg to inform Your Excellency that a member of our Legation formerly asked for a passport to be issued to him for the purpose of travel in Shansi.","jìng qǐ zhě。 qián yīn běn guǎn mǒu guān。 qǐng lǐng hù zhào。 qián fù shān xī dì fāng yóu lì。","逕啟者。前因本館某官。請領護照。前赴山西地方游歷。"
"567","On the . . . day of the current month he returned to Peking and handed back his passport.","xiàn yú běn yuè mǒu rì huí jīng。 chéng jiǎo hù zhào。","現於本月某日回京。呈繳護照。"
"568","According to his own verbal report, he received most satisfactory attention from the local authorities in every place he passed through, for which he is deeply grateful.","bìng jù miàn chēng。 suǒ yǒu jīng guò gè chù。 jūn méng gè dì fāng guān。 tuǒ wéi zhào liào。 shí shēn míng gǎn。 děng qíng qián lái。","並據面稱。所有經過各處。均蒙各地方官。妥為照料。實深銘感。等情前來。"
"569","I beg to return the passport originally received and hope that Your Excellency will take note thereof and cancel the same.","zī jiāng yuán lǐng hù zhào yī zhǐ sòng shàng。 jí xī guì zǒng zhǎng chá zhào hé xiāo kě yě。","茲將原領護照一紙送上。即希貴總長查照核銷可也。"
"570","moreover he told him this matter point by point.","yòu jiāng cǐ shì cì dì gào zhī","又將此事次第告之"
"571","he immediately released the men who had been seized.","jiāng bèi ná zhī rén lì xíng fàng huí","將被拏之人立行放回"
"572","It being thus.","shì ruò cǐ","事若此"
"573","To regard a difficult matter as an easy one.","shì nán ruò yì","視難若易"
"574","Thinking that no one is as pretty as herself.","yǐ wéi měi。 mò jǐ ruò yě","以為美。莫己若也"
"575","Like an innocent person going to the place of death.","ruò wú zuì ér jiù sǐ dì","若無罪而就死地"
"576","To do what you do and to seek for what you desire is like climbing a tree to seek for fish.","yǐ ruò suǒ wéi。 qiú ruò suǒ yù。 yóu yuán mù ér qiú yú yě。","以若所為。求若所欲。猶緣木而求魚也。"
"577","The people's longing for him was like their longing for rain in a time of great drought.","mín zhī wàng zhī。 ruò dà hàn zhī wàng yǔ yě。","民之望之。若大旱之望雨也。"
"578","To look upon virtue as something to which one cannot attain.","jiàn shàn rú bù jí","見善如不及"
"579","Like a fish that finds the water, like a bird that finds a grove.","rú yú dé shuǐ。 rú niǎo dé lín。","如魚得水。如鳥得林。"
"580","The faults of the superior man are like the eclipse of the sun and moon. He has his faults, and all men see them.","jūn zǐ zhī guò yě。 rú rì yuè zhī shí yān。 guò yě， rén jiē jiàn zhī","君子之過也。如日月之食焉。過也，人皆見之"
"581","The affection of husband and wife is deep as the sea.","fū qī zhī qíng。 shēn rú hǎi","夫妻之情。深如海"
"582","When the Master was not occupied with business, he looked comfortable and happy.","zǐ zhī yān jū。 shēn shēn rú yě。 yāo yāo rú yě","子之燕居。申申如也。夭夭如也"
"583","As if he saw his very lungs and liver.","rú jiàn qí fèi gān rán","如見其肺肝然"
"584","Holy men and sages love other men as themselves.","shèng xián ài rén zhī xīn。 rú ài jǐ rán","聖賢愛人之心。如愛己然"
"585","Humanity conquers inhumanity like water conquers fire.","rén zhī shèng bù rén yě。 yóu shuǐ shèng huǒ","仁之勝不仁也。猶水勝火"
"586","Breath is like wind, blood is like water.","qì yóu fēng yě。 xuè yóu shuǐ yě","氣猶風也。血猶水也"
"587","The Master said, ""To go beyond is as wrong as not to come up.","zǐ yuē guò yóu bù jí","子曰過猶不及"
"588","Our Master cannot be attained in the same way as the heaven cannot be reached by the steps of a stair.","fū zǐ zhī bù kě jí yě， yóu tiān zhī bù kě jiē ér shēng yě。","夫子之不可及也，猶天之不可階而升也。"
"589","The people turn to benevolence as water flows downwards.","mín zhī guī rén yě。 yóu shuǐ zhī jiù xià","民之歸仁也。猶水之就下"
"590","The music of the present day is like the music of antiquity.","jīn zhī yuè。 yóu gǔ zhī yuè yě。","今之樂。由古之樂也。"
"591","Like the insensibility of wood or stone.","shì mù shí zhī wú zhī yě","似木石之無知也"
"592","The ancients in their form were like beasts, but in their hearts they had the virtue of the great sages.","gǔ rén xíng shì shòu。 xīn yǒu dà shèng dé","古人形似獸。心有大聖德"
"593","King Wang looked on the people as on a wounded man, and he looked towards the right path as if he could not see it.","wén wáng shì mín rú shāng。 wàng dào ér wèi zhī jiàn","文王視民如傷。望道而未之見"
"594","His virtue is like that of holy men and sages.","qí dào dé， yǎn rú shèng xián","其道德，儼如聖賢"
"595","His merits are like those of heroes.","qí gōng yè， yǎn rán háo jié","其功業，儼然豪傑"
"596","As though he had already known it long before.","yǎn ruò sù zhī","儼若宿知"
"597","The way of virtue is like a great road. Is it difficult to know it?","fū dào ruò dà lù rán， qǐ nán zhī zāi","夫道若大路然，豈難知哉"
"598","In the time of the Sung emperor, Chen-tsung, the Kitans attacked China.","sòng zhēn zōng shí。 qì dān rù kòu。","宋真宗時。契丹入寇。"
"599","The commander in chief, Kao-ch'iung, urged the emperor to cross the river in order to oppose the enemies.","zhǐ huī shǐ gāo qióng。 qǐng dì dù hé yù kòu。","指揮使高瓊。請帝渡河禦寇。"
"600","The emperor would not assent.","dì bù yǔn。","帝不允。"
"601","Kao-ch'iung began to argue with him very hotly.","qióng zhēng zhī shèn lì。","瓊爭之甚力。"
"602","The prime-minister Feng-cheng, who was standing beside him, shouted at him: ""Kao-ch'iung, how dare you disregard all etiquette!","shí zǎi chén féng zhěng。 zài páng hē zhī yuē。 gāo qióng hé dé wú lǐ。","時宰臣馮拯。在旁呵之曰。高瓊何得無理。"
"603","You have secured the office of minister by your essays"", replied Kao-ch'iung angrily, ""now, when there are so many enemies, you blame me for lack of etiquette. Why then do you not compose some verses in order to rout them?","qióng nù yuē。 jūn yǐ wén zhāng wéi dà chén。 jīn lǔ qí chōng chì rú cǐ。 yóu zé qióng wú lǐ。 jūn hé bù fù shī yī zhāng。 yǐ tuì lǔ yē。","瓊怒曰。君以文章為大臣。今虜騎充斥如此。猶責瓊無禮。君何不賦詩一章。以退虜耶。"
"604","Feng-cheng was struck speechless with that reply.","féng zhěng yǔ sè。","馮拯語塞。"
"605","The emperor followed Kao-ch'iung's advice, proceeded to the northern side of the river, ascended the wall, and ordered the yellow imperial banners to be displayed.","zhēn zōng nǎi cóng qióng yán。 zhì hé běi dēng chéng。 zhāng huáng lóng qí。","真宗乃從瓊言。至河北登城。張黃龍旗。"
"606","Officers and soldiers who were under the wall saw the emperor and greeted him enthusiastically.","chéng xià jiàng shì wàng jiàn zhī。 jiē hū wàn suì。","城下將士望見之。皆呼萬歲。"
"607","Their courage increased a hundred fold; they attacked the Kitans and drove them away.","qì shì bǎi bèi。 suì jī qì dān zǒu zhī。","氣勢百倍。遂擊契丹走之。"
"608","As an official of our Legation was intending to go into Shensi for travel, I sent a letter asking Your Excellency to have a passport prepared for him.","jìng qǐ zhě。 qián yīn běn shǔ mǒu guān。 nǐ fù shǎn xī yóu lì。 dāng yóu běn gōng shǐ hán qǐng guì zǒng zhǎng。 shàn gěi hù zhào yī zhāng。","逕啟者。前因本署某官。擬赴陝西遊歷。當由本公使函請貴總長。繕給護照一張。"
"609","But before he started, a telegram was received from our Foreign Office ordering him to return home at once.","shàng wèi dòng shēn。 xiàn jiē běn guó wài jiāo bù diàn kāi。 chì lìng gāi yuán。 xùn jí huí guó。 děng yīn qián lái。 zhǔn cǐ。","尚未動身。現接本國外交部電開。飭令該員。迅即回國。等因前來。准此。"
"610","Naturally the matter of the trip of the said official into Shensi must be dropped.","suǒ yǒu gāi yuán fù shǎn yī jǔ。 zì yīng zhōng zhǐ。","所有該員赴陝一舉。自應中止。"
"611","Now, I beg to return the originally received passport which I hope Your Excellency will note and duly cancel.","zī jiāng yuán lǐng hù zhào yī zhǐ sòng shàng。 jí xī guì zǒng zhǎng chá zhào zhù xiāo kě yě。","茲將原領護照一紙送上。即希貴總長查照註銷可也。"
"612","At an earlier date I sent you by letter the newly fixed trade regulations.","jìng qǐ zhě。 rì qián běn gōng shǐ hán sòng nǐ dìng tōng shāng xīn zhāng。","逕啟者。日前本公使函送擬定通商新章。"
"613","Although up to this time several days have elapsed, I have not yet been honored with your reply and do not know whether any decision has been made concerning the said regulations.","qì jīn shù rì。 wèi méng jiàn fù。 wèi zhī céng fǒu yì dìng。","迄今數日。未蒙見覆。未知曾否議定。"
"614","Being very anxious about this matter I beg to especially request Your Excellency to let me know at your earliest convenience what are the conditions of your decision, in order that I may transmit your communication to our Foreign Office for their information.","pàn niàn qí qiè。 wèi cǐ tè qǐng guì zǒng zhǎng。 jiāng jìn rì yì dìng rú hé qíng xíng。 sù jí cì fù。 yǐ biàn zhuǎn fù běn guó wài jiāo bù chá zhào。 shì hé。","盼念綦切。為此特請貴總長。將近日議定如何情形。速即賜覆。以便轉覆本國外交部查照。是荷。"
"615","Yen-tzu was sent as envoy to the Ch'u State. He was very short.","yàn zǐ shǐ chǔ。 yàn zǐ duǎn。","晏子使楚。晏子短。"
"616","The people of Ch'u had erected a small door beside the big gate, and asked Yen-tzu to pass through it.","chǔ rén wéi xiǎo mén yú dà mén zhī cè。 ér yán yàn zǐ。","楚人為小門於大門之側。而延晏子。"
"617","But Yen-tzu refused to do it.","yàn zǐ bù rù。","晏子不入。"
"618","If I were sent as envoy to a dog country"", he said, ""I would have entered through the dog-gate. But I am sent to the Ch'u State, and it is not proper for me to pass through that gate.","yuē。 shǐ zhì gǒu guó zhě。 cóng gǒu mén rù。 jīn chén shǐ chǔ。 bù dāng cóng cǐ mén。","曰。使至狗國者。從狗門入。今臣使楚。不當從此門。"
"619","Thereupon the master of the ceremonies changed his mind, and let him enter through the big gate. When Yen-tzu saw the prince of Ch'u the latter asked him, ""Has Ch'i no men?","bīn zhě gèng cóng dà mén。 rù jiàn chǔ wáng。 chǔ wáng yuē。 qí wú rén yē。","儐者更從大門。入見楚王。楚王曰。齊無人耶。"
"620","Our Ch'i"", replied Yen-tzu, ""in the locality of Lin-tzu has three hundred villages. If our people were to open their sleeves, a big curtain could be made of them. If they were to wipe off their sweat, it would be like rain. They are living very close to each other. How should one say, that Ch'i has no men!","yàn zǐ duì yuē。 qí zhī lín zī sān bǎi lǘ。 zhāng mèi chéng wéi， huī hàn chéng yǔ。 bǐ jiān jiē zhǒng ér zài。 hé wéi wú rén。","晏子對曰。齊之臨淄三百閭。張袂成帷，揮汗成雨。比肩接踵而在。何為無人。"
"621","Why then did they send you?"" asked the prince.","wáng yuē。 rán zé hé wéi shǐ zi。","王曰。然則何為使子。"
"622","My sovereign"", replied Yen-tzu, ""when sending his envoys considers the worth of the princes. Virtuous men he sends to virtuous princes, and worthless ones to worthless princes.","yàn zǐ duì yuē。 qí mìng shǐ gè yǒu suǒ zhǔ。 qí xián zhě shǐ xián zhǔ。 bù xiào zhě shǐ bù xiào zhǔ。","晏子對曰。齊命使各有所主。其賢者使賢主。不肖者使不肖主。"
"623","I am a very bad fellow, therefore I had to be sent to the Ch'u State.","yīng zuì bù xiào。 gù yí shǐ chǔ ěr。","嬰最不肖。故宜使楚耳。"
"624","this house is mine.","cǐ fáng wéi wú suǒ yǒu","此房為吾所有"
"625","the five articles of the present treaty.","suǒ yǒu xiàn yuē wǔ tiáo","所有現約五條"

"630","he has not cleared off his debts.","suǒ qiàn wèi céng jié qīng","所欠未曾結清"
"631","The envoys sent by Ch'i all have princes who are worthy of them.","qí mìng shǐ gè yǒu suǒ zhǔ","齊命使各有所主"
"632","everybody acts conformably to his abilities.","rén zuò shì gè yǒu suǒ cháng","人作事各有所長"
"633","The Pole Star keeps its place, and all the stars bow to it.","běi chén jū qí suǒ。 ér zhòng xīng gòng zhī","北辰居其所。而眾星共之"
"634","The Master said, ""When I walk along with two others, they may serve me as my teachers. I will select their good qualities and follow them, their bad qualities and avoid them.","zǐ yuē， sān rén xíng。 bì yǒu wǒ shī yān， zé qí shàn zhě ér cóng zhī， qí bù shàn zhě ér gǎi zhī","子曰，三人行。必有我師焉，擇其善者而從之，其不善者而改之"
"635","The Master said, ""To give one's self earnestly to duties due to men, to respect spiritual beings and to keep aloof from them may be called wisdom.","zǐ yuē wù mín zhī yì， jìng guǐ shén ér yuǎn zhī， kě wèi zhī yǐ","子曰務民之義，敬鬼神而遠之，可謂知矣"
"636","With a bow in hand and some arrows fastened at the waist.","shǒu gōng ér yāo shǐ","手弓而腰矢"
"637","Poverty and meanness are what men dislike.","pín yǔ jiàn， shì rén zhī suǒ wù yě","貧與賤，是人之所惡也"
"638","More than tongue and pen can express.","fēi yán yǔ bǐ suǒ néng jìn","非言與筆所能盡"
"639","The Three-Character Classic and the Thousand-Character Essay are what children study.","sān zì jīng yǔ qiān zì wén， wéi yòu zǐ suǒ niàn","三字經與千字文，為幼子所念"
"640","It was only you and I who attained this.","wéi wǒ yǔ ěr。 yǒu shì fú","惟我與爾。有是夫"
"641","During the winter of last year and the spring of this.","shàng nián dōng jiān jí běn nián chūn jiān","上年冬間及本年春間"
"642","The south wind blows in spring and in autumn.","nán fēng fā yú chūn jiān jí qiū jiān","南風發於春間及秋間"
"643","Gentle and docile.","wēn qiě huì","溫且惠"
"644","The Master said, ""Though a man have abilities as admirable as those of the duke of Chou, yet if he be proud and niggardly, those other things are not worth being looked at.","zǐ yuē rú yǒu zhōu gōng zhī cái zhī měi。 shǐ jiāo qiě lìn。 qí yú bù zú guān yě yǐ","子曰如有周公之才之美。使驕且吝。其餘不足觀也已"
"645","When a country is well-governed, poverty and a mean condition are things to be ashamed of. When a country is ill-governed, riches and honor are things to be ashamed of.","bāng yǒu dào， pín qiě jiàn yān， chǐ yě。 bāng wú dào， fù qiě guì yān， chǐ yě","邦有道，貧且賤焉，恥也。邦無道，富且貴焉，恥也"
"646","In ancient times our country was much troubled in the north by the Tartar hordes.","wǒ guó gǔ shí。 běi yǒu xiōng nú zhī huàn。","我國古時。北有匈奴之患。"
"647","The States of Yen and Chao had built walls in order to protect themselves against them.","yān zhào zhū guó。 zhù chéng fáng zhī。","燕趙諸國。築城防之。"
"648","But each State only protected its own frontiers, and their walls had not been united.","gè bǎo jiāng tǔ。 bù xiāng lián zhǔ。","各保疆土。不相連屬。"
"649","When emperor Ch'in-shih-huang conquered the six kingdoms and united China, he despatched his general Meng-t'ien at the head of an army of 300,000 men against the Hsiung-nu.","qín shǐ huáng jì miè liù guó。 yī tǒng zhōng xià。 nǎi qiǎn jiāng jūn méng tián。 fā bīng sān shí wàn fá xiōng nú。","秦始皇既滅六國。一統中夏。乃遣將軍蒙恬。發兵三十萬伐匈奴。"
"650","He completely routed them, rebuilt the old walls and united them into one.","dà bài zhī。 xiū zhù jiù chéng。 hé ér wéi yī。","大敗之。修築舊城。合而為一。"
"651","The Wall begins on the east at Shan-hai-kuan and ends on the west at Chia-yü-kuan, being more than 5000 ""li"" in length.","dōng qǐ shān hǎi guān。 xī zhì jiā yù guān。 cháng fán wǔ qiān yú lǐ。","東起山海關。西至嘉峪關。長凡五千餘里。"
"652","This wall is one of the famous constructions of the world, and is called the Great Wall.","wéi quán qiú yǒu míng zhī jù gōng。 shì rén chēng yuē。 wàn lǐ cháng chéng。","為全球有名之鉅工。世人稱曰。萬里長城。"
"653","Outside of the wall are Sheng-ching and Mongolia. Inside the wall are the provinces of Chihli, Shansi, Shensi and Kansu.","cháng chéng zhī wài。 wéi shèng jīng měng gǔ。 qí nèi。 zé zhí lǐ。 shān xī。 shǎn xī。 gān sù děng shěng。","長城之外。為盛京蒙古。其內。則直隸。山西。陝西。甘肅等省。"
"654","The wall is built of bricks and stone.","chéng yuán yǐ zhuān shí wéi zhī。","城垣以甎石為之。"
"655","It is 40 feet high and more than 20 feet thick, and has a very imposing aspect.","gāo sì shí chǐ。 hòu èr shí yú chǐ。 xíng shì xióng jùn。","高四十尺。厚二十餘尺。形勢雄峻。"
"656","At all sharp pointed peaks there are high towers, from which one can get a distant view.","fán fēng luán ruì chù。 yǒu gāo lóu kě yǐ yuǎn tiào。","凡峰巒銳處。有高樓可以遠眺。"
"657","These were just watch-towers used by the ancients.","jí gǔ rén shǒu wàng chù yě。","即古人守望處也。"
"658","The various subjects we discussed together recently, all concerned matters of great importance.","jìng qǐ zhě， qián jīng miàn tán gè jié， jù xì jǐn yào shì jiàn。","逕啟者，前經面談各節，俱係緊要事件。"
"659","I feared lest there might be some misunderstanding in our conversation, and therefore have composed a memorandum of the subjects about which we talked. I beg to send it herewith to Your Excellency and to ask you to examine it and to see if there be any mistakes therein.","běn gōng shǐ kǒng yán yǔ zhī jiān， huò yǒu wù huì zhī chù， zī jiāng qián rì hù tán gè shì， lìng shàn jié lüè yī zhǐ sòng shàng， jí qǐng guì zǒng zhǎng chá yuè qí jiān， yǒu wú chuǎn wù zhī chù","本公使恐言語之間，或有誤會之處，茲將前日互談各事，另繕節略一紙送上，即請貴總長查閱其間，有無舛誤之處"
"660","Hoping that you will favor me with your reply.","jí xī jiàn fù kě yě","即希見覆可也"
"661","I received yesterday your letter, with a memorandum enclosed, with which I have fully acquainted myself.","zuó zhǔn lái hán bìng jié lüè yī zhāng。 jūn yǐ yuè xī。","昨准來函並節略一張。均已閱悉。"
"662","From this it can be seen how zealously Your Excellency performs your business. I cannot find words to express my respect to you.","jù jiàn guì gōng shǐ rèn zhēn gōng shì， mò míng qīn pèi","具見貴公使認真公事，莫名欽佩"
"663","I have examined the memorandum. It contains the questions we discussed several days ago, and I find no mistakes therein.","běn zǒng zhǎng jiǎn chá jié lüè suǒ kāi， jūn xì qián rì hù tán gè shì。 qí jiān bìng wú chuǎn wù zhī chù。","本總長檢查節略所開，均係前日互談各事。其間並無舛誤之處。"
"664","I beg to return the memorandum herewith.","xiāng yīng jiāng jié lüè yī zhāng fù hái","相應將節略一張附還"
"665","The prince of the Wu State wished to attack the Ch'u State and said to those who were round him, ""If anybody dares to admonish me, that man shall die"".","wú wáng yù fá jīng。 gào qí zuǒ yòu yuē。 yǒu gǎn jiàn zhě sǐ。","吳王欲伐荊。告其左右曰。有敢諫者死。"
"666","Among the courtiers there was one named Shao-ju-tzu. He wished to admonish the prince, but did not dare.","shè rén yǒu shào rú zǐ zhě。 yù jiàn bù gǎn。","舍人有少孺子者。欲諫不敢。"
"667","Therefore he took a crossbow and some pellets and went to take a walk in the back garden, where he got his dress all moistened with dew.","zé huái wán cāo dàn， yóu yú hòu yuán。 lù zhān qí yī。","則懷丸操彈，游於後園。露沾其衣。"
"668","He did thus three days.","rú shì zhě sān dàn。","如是者三旦。"
"669","The prince asked him, ""Why do you come with your dress so moistened?","wú wáng yuē。 zǐ lái hé kǔ zhān yī rú cǐ。","吳王曰。子來何苦沾衣如此。"
"670","There is a tree in the garden"", he replied, ""with a cicada upon it.","duì yuē。 yuán zhōng yǒu shù。 qí shàng yǒu chán。","對曰。園中有樹。其上有蟬。"
"671","The cicada is sitting very high up. It chirps mournfully and drinks dew not knowing that there is a mantis behind it.","chán gāo jū。 bēi míng yǐn lù。 bù zhī táng láng zài qí hòu yě。","蟬高居。悲鳴飲露。不知螳螂在其後也。"
"672","The mantis lies in ambush wishing to catch the cicada, and not knowing that an oriole is beside it.","táng láng wěi shēn qū fù yù qǔ chán， ér bù zhī huáng què zài qí páng yě","螳螂委身曲附欲取蟬，而不知黃雀在其旁也"
"673","The oriole stretches out its neck trying to peck the mantis not knowing that a crossbow and pellets are below.","huáng què yán jǐng yù zhuó táng láng。 ér bù zhī dàn wán zài qí xià yě","黃雀延頸欲啄螳螂。而不知彈丸在其下也"
"674","Those three earnestly wish to take advantage of what is before them and do not realize the evil that is behind.","cǐ sān zhě。 jiē wù yù dé qí qián lì。 ér bù gù qí hòu zhī yǒu huàn yě。","此三者。皆務欲得其前利。而不顧其後之有患也。"
"675","Good"", said the prince, and stopped his troops.","wú wáng yuē。 shàn zāi。 nǎi bà qí bīng。","吳王曰。善哉。乃罷其兵。"

"696","Heterodox doctrines are like robbers and brigands or fire and water. "699","I thereupon wrote officially to the said consul, and at the same time sent a deputy to inform him of the matter personally.","běn dà chén dāng jīng zhào huì gāi lǐng shì guān。 bìng pài wěi yuán xiàng qí dāng miàn gào zhī。","本大臣當經照會該領事官。並派委員向其當面告知。"

"703","One day prince Hsüan of Ch'u said to his ministers, ""I have heard that the northerners are afraid of Chao-hsi-hsü. What is the reason for it?","jīng xuān wáng wèn qún chén yuē。 wú wén běi fāng zhī wèi zhāo xī xù yě。 guǒ chéng hé rú。","荊宣王問群臣曰。吾聞北方之畏昭奚恤也。果誠何如。"
"704","The ministers could not reply.","qún chén mò duì。","群臣莫對。"
"705","Then Chiang-i said, ""Once upon a time a tiger went out in quest of prey and caught a fox.","jiāng yī duì yuē。 hǔ qiú bǎi shòu ér shí zhī。 dé hú。","江一對曰。虎求百獸而食之。得狐。"
"706","You will not dare to eat me"", said the fox, ""because God has made me lord of all the beasts. Now if you eat me, you will disobey the order of God.","hú yuē。 zǐ wú gǎn shí wǒ yě。 tiān dì shǐ wǒ zhǎng bǎi shòu。 jīn zǐ shí wǒ。 shì nì tiān dì mìng yě。","狐曰。子無敢食我也。天帝使我長百獸。今子食我。是逆天帝命也。"
"707","If you do not believe me, I will walk on ahead of you, and you will follow me, and then you shall see whether the beasts dare do other than run away as soon as they see me.","zǐ yǐ wǒ wéi bù xìn。 wǒ wèi zǐ xiān xíng。 zǐ suí wǒ hòu。 guān bǎi shòu zhī jiàn wǒ。 ér gǎn bù zǒu hū。","子以我為不信。我為子先行。子隨我後。觀百獸之見我。而敢不走乎。"
"708","The tiger agreed and went with the fox.","hǔ yǐ wéi rán。 gù suì yǔ zhī xíng。","虎以為然。故遂與之行。"
"709","All the beasts, when they saw them, ran away in terror.","shòu jiàn zhī jiē zǒu。","獸見之皆走。"
"710","But the tiger did not know that the beasts were afraid of him, and thought that it was the fox they feared.","hǔ bù zhī shòu wèi jǐ ér zǒu yě。 yǐ wéi wèi hú yě。","虎不知獸畏己而走也。以為畏狐也。"
"711","Your Majesty's kingdom occupies an area of five thousand square ""li"", and there are one million soldiers exclusively subordinated to Chao-hsi-hsü.","jīn wáng zhī dì wǔ qiān lǐ。 dài jiǎ bǎi wàn。 ér zhuān zhǔ zhī zhāo xī xù。","今王之地五千里。帶甲百萬。而專屬之昭奚恤。"
"712","Therefore although the northerners are afraid of Chao-hsi-hsü, in reality they are afraid of Your Majesty's troops, just the same as the beasts were afraid of the tiger.","gù běi fāng zhī wèi zhāo xī xù yě。 qí shí wèi wáng zhī jiǎ bīng yě。 yóu bǎi shòu zhī wèi hǔ yě。","故北方之畏昭奚恤也。其實畏王之甲兵也。猶百獸之畏虎也。"
"713","I received your letter several days ago with the eight clauses of the newly fixed customs regulations.","qián zhǔn lái hán。 bìng xīn dìng shuì wù zhāng chéng bā tiáo。","前准來函。並新訂稅務章程八條。"
"714","I then sent a copy of these regulations to my Foreign Office for their approval.","dāng jīng běn gōng shǐ jiāng gāi xīn zhāng chāo sòng běn guó wài jiāo bù hé bàn。","當經本公使將該新章抄送本國外交部核辦。"
"715","I have now received a reply to the effect that as there are in the newly fixed regulations three clauses that are slightly inconvenient for commerce, I am asked to reconsider them carefully with you so that they may be made quite satisfactory.","xiàn jiē zhǔn zī fù。 yǐ suǒ dìng xīn zhāng nèi yǒu sān tiáo。 yú shāng qíng shǎo yǒu wèi biàn。 qǐng zài zhēn zhuó jǐn shàn。 yǐ guī tuǒ dàng。 děng yīn qián lái。","現接准咨覆。以所訂新章內有三條。于商情少有未便。請再斟酌盡善。以歸妥當。等因前來。"
"716","I propose therefore to go to your ministry on the 20th of this month to personally consult with you and decide this matter in order that these regulations may remain for ever, which is a very important thing.","zhǔn cǐ。 běn gōng shǐ nǐ yú běn yuè èr shí rì。 qián wǎng guì shǔ。 miàn yì zhuó bàn。 bǐ chuí jiǔ yuǎn。 shì wéi zhì yào。","准此。本公使擬於本月二十日。前往貴署。面議酌辦。俾垂久遠。是為至要。"

"719","Unexpectedly this morning I find myself indisposed and will not be able to hold the interview.","jù zhī jīn zǎo shēn tǐ ǒu jué bù shuǎng。 bù kè qián wǎng huì wù。","詎知今早身體偶覺不爽。不克前往會晤。"

"722","For this reason I beg to send you this special letter for your information, hoping that Your Excellency will excuse me.","yòng tè zhuān hán fèng bù。 guì zǒng zhǎng chá zhào。 bìng wàng yuán liàng shì xìng。","用特專函奉布。貴總長查照。並望原諒是幸。"
"723","The Chao State was going to attack the Yen State. The oyster closed up its shell and jammed its beak.","zhào qiě fá yān， sū dài wèi yān wèi huì wáng yuē， jīn zhě chén lái， guò yì shuǐ。 bàng fāng chū pù ér yù zhuó qí ròu， bàng hé ér qián qí huì","趙且伐燕，蘇代為燕謂惠王曰，今者臣來，過易水。蚌方出曝而鷸啄其肉，蚌合而箝其喙"

"750","Yü quickly jumped aside to avoid it, and the arrow struck the wall where it stuck quivering.","gōng jí yuè bì。 shǐ guàn yú bì。 zhàn zhàn yǒu shēng。","公急躍避。矢貫於壁。戰戰有聲。"
"751","The demon became furious, pulled out his sword and whirled it like the wind intending to give him a tremendous blow.","guǐ nù shèn。 bá pèi dāo huī rú fēng。 wàng gōng lì pī。","鬼怒甚。拔佩刀揮如風。望公力劈。"
"752","Yü ducked, and the sword struck the stone ledge cutting it in two.","gōng náo jìn。 dāo zhòng tíng shí。 shí lì duàn。","公猱進。刀中庭石。石立斷。"
"753","Yü sprang between the demon's legs and began hacking at his ankles. There was the sound of ringing blows.","gōng chū qí gǔ jiān。 xuē guǐ zhòng huái。 kēng rán yǒu shēng。","公出其股間。削鬼中踝。鏗然有聲。"
"754","The demon, still more furious, roared like thunder and turned round to chop again at Yü.","guǐ yì nù。 hǒu rú léi。 zhuǎn shēn fù duò。","鬼益怒。吼如雷。轉身復剁。"
"755","The latter again crouched and passed between the demon's legs.","gōng yòu fú shēn rù。","公又伏身入。"

"757","Yü reached its side and hacked fiercely with the same ringing sound, and the demon fell down and became rigid.","gōng yǐ jí xié xià。 měng zhuó zhī。 yì kēng rán yǒu shēng。 guǐ pú ér jiāng。","公已及脅下。猛斫之。亦鏗然有聲。鬼仆而僵。"
"758","Yü hit at it wildly, and it sounded hard like a watchman's rattle.","gōng luàn jī zhī。 shēng yìng rú tuò。","公亂擊之。聲硬如柝。"
"759","Turning the light on he found it was a wooden idol as tall as a man, with a bow and arrows attached to the waist, and carved and painted in a hideous fashion.","zhú zhī。 zé yī mù ǒu。 gāo dà rú rén。 gōng shǐ shàng chán yāo jì。 kè huà zhēng níng。","燭之。則一木偶。高大如人。弓矢尚纏腰際。刻畫猙獰。"
"760","Wherever the sword had struck, there was blood.","jiàn jī chù jiē yǒu xuè。","劍擊處皆有血。"
"761","Yü held the candle in his hand till dawn, and then he realized that the demons were all sent by the diviner, intending to bring about his death and so manifest his own magic art.","gōng yīn bǐng zhú dài dàn。 fāng wù guǐ wù jiē bo rén qiǎn zhī。 yù zhì rén yú sǐ。 yǐ shén qí shù yě。","公因秉燭待旦。方悟鬼物皆卜人遣之。欲致人於死。以神其術也。"
"762","The next day Yü told the story to all his friends and went with them to the diviner's house.","cì rì piān gào jiāo zhī。 yǔ gòng yì bo suǒ。","次日偏告交知。與共詣卜所。"
"763","The latter saw him coming from a distance and made himself invisible.","bo rén yáo jiàn gōng。 piē bù kě jiàn。","卜人遙見公。瞥不可見。"
"764","Some one said this was a trick of rendering oneself invisible, and the charm could be dispelled with dog's blood.","huò yuē。 cǐ yì xíng shù yě。 quǎn xuè kě pò。","或曰。此翳形術也。犬血可破。"
"765","Yü accordingly procured some dog's blood and went back to the diviner's house.","gōng rú yán jiè bèi ér wǎng。","公如言戒備而往。"
"766","Again the diviner disappeared as before, but Yü scattered the blood on the place where he had been standing.","bo rén yòu nì rú qián。 jí yǐ quǎn xuè wò lì chù。","卜人又匿如前。急以犬血沃立處。"
"767","The diviner's head and face appeared blurred with blood, his eyes glaring like a demon's.","dàn jiàn bo rén tóu miàn jiē wéi quǎn xuè mó hú。 mù zhuó zhuó rú guǐ lì。","但見卜人頭面皆為犬血模糊。目灼灼如鬼立。"
"768","Thereupon they seized him and turned him over to the civil authorities who put him to death.","nǎi zhí fù yǒu sī ér shā zhī。","乃執付有司而殺之。"
"769","When according to these rules a foreign law is to be applied, its application is nevertheless forbidden if it contravenes the public order or good morals of China.","yī běn tiáo lì shì yòng wài guó fǎ shí。 qí guī dìng yǒu bèi yú zhōng guó gōng gòng zhì xù huò shàn liáng fēng sú zhě。 réng bù shì yòng zhī。","依本條例適用外國法時。其規定有背於中國公共秩序或善良風俗者。仍不適用之。"
"770","When, according to these rules, the national law of the party is to be applied, his national law, if he has had more than one nationality, is determined according to his nationality last acquired.","yī běn tiáo lì shì yòng dāng shì rén běn guó fǎ shí。 qí dāng shì rén yǒu duō shù zhī guó jí zhě。 yī zuì hòu qǔ dé zhī guó jí。 dìng qí běn guó fǎ。","依本條例適用當事人本國法時。其當事人有多數之國籍者。依最後取得之國籍。定其本國法。"
"771","If, according to the laws of nationality, he should be recognised as a Chinese subject, the Chinese law shall be applied.","dàn yī guó jí fǎ yīng rèn wéi zhōng guó rén zhě。 yī zhōng guó zhī fǎ lǜ。","但依國籍法應認為中國人者。依中國之法律。"
"772","If the party has no nationality, the law of his domicile shall be applied, and if his domicile is unknown, the law of his place of residence shall be applied.","dāng shì rén wú guó jí zhě。 yī qí zhù suǒ dì fǎ。 zhù suǒ bù míng shí。 yī qí jū suǒ dì fǎ。","當事人無國籍者。依其住所地法。住所不明時。依其居所地法。"
"773","If, in the country of the party, the laws of different localities are different the law of the locality to which he belongs shall be applied.","dāng shì rén běn guó nèi gè dì fāng fǎ lǜ bù tóng zhě。 yī qí suǒ shǔ dì fāng zhī fǎ。","當事人本國內各地方法律不同者。依其所屬地方之法。"
"774","Living in retirement to study their aims, and practising righteousness to carry out their principles, I have heard these words, but I have not seen such men.","yǐn jū yǐ qiú qí zhì。 xíng yì yǐ dá qí dào。 wú wén qí yǔ yǐ。 wèi jiàn qí rén yě。","隱居以求其志。行義以達其道。吾聞其語矣。未見其人也"
"775","Artisans dwell in their shops in order to accomplish their work. The superior man learns in order to carry out his principles.","bǎi gōng jū sì。 yǐ chéng qí shì。 jūn zǐ xué yǐ zhì qí dào","百工居肆。以成其事。君子學以致其道"
"776","To instruct the young in order to prevent wrong doing.","xùn zǐ dì。 yǐ jìn fēi wéi","訓子弟。以禁非為"
"777","The master said, ""The determined scholar and the man of perfect virtue will not seek to live at the expense of injuring their virtue. They will even sacrifice their lifes in order to preserve their virtue complete.","zǐ yuē。 zhì shì rén rén wú qiú shēng yǐ hài rén。 yǒu shā shēn yǐ chéng rén","子曰。志士仁人無求生以害仁。有殺身以成仁"
"778","The Master said, ""In ancient times men learned for themselves. Now-a-days men learn for other men.","zǐ yuē。 gǔ zhī xué zhě wèi jǐ。 jīn zhī xué zhě wèi rén","子曰。古之學者為己。今之學者為人"
"779","Tzu-lu said to Confucius, ""The ruler of Wei has been waiting for you in order to administer the government.","zǐ lù yuē。 wèi jūn dài zǐ ér wéi zhèng","子路曰。衛君待子而為政"
"780","How shall I know the men of virtue and talent so that I may raise them to office?","yān zhī xián cái ér jǔ zhī","焉知賢才而舉之"

"787","Since the establishment of the Republic fifteen years ago internecine strife has prevailed without a single day of peace.","mín guó chéng lì。 shí yǒu wǔ zǎi。 fēn luàn qì wú níng rì。","民國成立。十有五載。紛亂迄無寧日。"
"788","Since I assumed office as Chief Executive I have been striving my best to lead the country to peace so as to make a new start with the people.","běn zhí zhèng lì shì yǐ lái。 jīng jīng yǐ zhèn dǎo hé píng yǔ mín gēng shǐ wéi niàn。","本執政蒞事以來。兢兢以振導和平與民更始為念。"
"789","Unfortunately my virtues fail to win the respect of others, my ability is not sufficient to stop the rebellion, my strength is not equal to my will, and I have not been able to realize my expectations.","bù tú dé wèi zú yǐ gǎn rén。 cái wèi zú yǐ jì biàn。 lì bù cóng xīn。 shì jù wéi yuàn。","不圖德未足以感人。才未足以濟變。力不從心。事俱違願。"
"790","I have more than once expressed my desire to resign from office, but the reason why I still do not retire in spite of all abuses and insults, is that I have played no small part in the establishment of the Republic sharing all joys and sorrows with it.","dié jīng shēng shù。 qī yú tuì xiū。 rán yóu bù cí bàng yì。 rěn rǔ zhì jīn zhě。 tú yǐ mín guó dì gòu。 běn zhí zhèng xīn lì suǒ cún。 xiū qī yǔ gòng。","迭經聲述。期於退休。然猶不辭謗議。忍辱至今者。徒以民國締構。本執政心力所存。休戚與共。"
"791","When I saw the internal difficulties of the country, the necessity of maintaining the national faith with the foreign Powers and the ""red"" danger to which the metropolitan district was exposed, I could not bear to give up my office suddenly without a feeling of responsibility.","nèi shěn shí jiān。 wài chóng guó xìn。 qiě mù dǔ chì huà zhī huò。 liú yú shǒu dū。 bù gǎn jù wéi wú zé rèn zhī fàng qì ěr。","內審時艱。外崇國信。且目睹赤化之禍。流於首都。不敢遽為無責任之放棄耳。"
"792","The ""coup d'etat"" of the 9th of this month seriously affects the laws of the country and the duty of military men, and my heart is filled with sorrow at this sudden change.","běn yuè jiǔ rì zhī luàn。 suǒ guān yú guó jiā jì gāng jūn rén zhí zé zhě jué jù。 gòu zī qí biàn。 nèi jiù yóu shēn。","本月九日之亂。所關於國家紀綱軍人職責者絕鉅。遘茲奇變。內疚尤深。"
"793","Formerly, when the Provisional Government was first established, I planned a number of affairs to be carried out, but in the course of one year I was hindered in my plans by the force of circumstances.","nǎng zhě lín shí zhèng fǔ kāi shǐ zhī rì。 céng guī dìng yīng bàn zhě ruò gān shì。 yī nián zhī zhōng。 shì shì hàn gé。","曩者臨時政府開始之日。曾規定應辦者若干事。一年之中。事勢扞格。"

"795","It is significant for the future prosperity of the country that recently many elder statesmen and military leaders have begun to talk over among themselves fundamental plans for the salvation of the country.","ěr lái zōng guó yuán gōng。 fāng yú zhū shuài。 lǚ yǐ dà jì xiāng yǔ xún móu。 guó jiā zhī fú。 yǒu mù gòng jiàn。","邇來宗國元功。方隅諸帥。屢以大計相與詢謀。國家之福。有目共見。"
"796","At the present juncture when disorders have reached an extreme point and everybody is longing for order there may be a good opportunity for the improvement of affairs.","dāng cǐ luàn jí sī zhì zhī qiū。 bù wú zhēn xià qǐ yuán zhī huì。","當此亂極思治之秋。不無貞下起元之會。"
"797","Let them quickly decide measures for rehabilitation, so that there will be no interruption of governmental business.","qí sù tuǒ yì shàn hòu。 bǐ guó zhèng bù zhì zhōng zhǎn。","其速妥議善後。俾國政不至中斬。"

"807","The onlookers urged the peasant to get rid of an inferior pear and let him go.","guān zhě quàn zhì liè zhě yī méi lìng qù。","觀者勸置劣者一枚令去。"

"811","Somebody remarked, ""Since you have it, why do you not eat it yourself?","huò yuē。 jì yǒu zhī hé bù zì shí。","或曰。既有之何不自食。"
"812","I particularly wanted the seeds to plant them,"" he replied.","yuē。 wú tè xū cǐ hé zuò zhǒng。","曰。吾特需此核作種。"

"817","The Revised Import Tariff of the twenty-eighth year of Kuang-Hsü has, up to the present, already reached the ten years limit.","àn chá qián qīng guāng xù èr shí bā nián xù xiū tōng shāng jìn kǒu shuì zé。 shī xíng zhì jīn。 yǐ jiè shí nián。","案查前清光緒二十八年續修通商進口稅則。施行至今。已屆十年。"

"819","Again, the rate of this Tariff was raised, in accordance with the International Protocol of 1901, to an effective 5% ""ad valorem"" whose basis of valuation was taken from the mean value of the merchandise at the moment of its debarkation in the three years of 1897, 1898 and 1899.","yòu chá cǐ xiàng shuì zé， xì zhào xīn chǒu gè guó hé yuē zēng zhì qiè shí zhí bǎi chōu wǔ。 qí gū suàn huò jià zhī jī， yǐ yī qiān bā bǎi jiǔ shí qī bā jiǔ sān nián xiè huò shí。 gè huò qiān suàn jià zhí","又查此項稅則，係照辛丑各國和約增至切實值百抽五。其估算貨價之基，以一千八百九十七八九三年卸貨時。各貨牽算價值"
"820","Now ten years have elapsed and in view of the fluctuations in values of the various kinds of merchandise, it should be revised in due time so as to make it conformable to the original agreement of an effective 5% ""ad valorem"".","jīn yǐ yuè shí nián。 gè huò jià zhí duō yǒu zēng jiǎn， zì yīng jí shí xiū gǎi。 yǐ fú qiè shí zhí bǎi chōu wǔ zhī yuán yuē","今已閱十年。各貨價值多有增減，自應及時修改。以符切實值百抽五之原約"

"849","The priest took it and soaked the place which he had dug. Every eye watched attentively.","dào shì jiē jìn kǎn chù， wàn mù cuán shì。","道士接浸坎處，萬目攢視。"
"850","A hooked sprout appeared; quickly became a tree, branches and leaves spread out.","xiàn yǒu jù méng chū。 jiàn dà。 é chéng shù。 zhī yè fú shū。","見有句萌出。漸大。俄成樹。枝葉扶疎。"
"851","Suddenly it flowered, suddenly fruited.","shū ér huā。 shū ér shí。","倏而花。倏而實。"
"852","The fruit grew large and had a fragrant smell. It hung in profusion on the entire tree.","shuò dà fāng fù。 léi léi mǎn shù。","碩大芳馥。纍纍滿樹。"
"853","The priest then picked the fruit from the tree and handed it round to the onlookers.","dào rén nǎi jí shù tóu。 zhāi cì guān zhě。","道人乃即樹頭。摘賜觀者。"
"854","In a moment all the pears were gone.","qǐng kè ér jǐn。","頃刻而盡。"
"855","Then he took his pick and hacked for a long time at the tree.","yǐ nǎi yǐ chán fá shù。 dīng dīng liáng jiǔ。","已乃以鑱伐樹。丁丁良久。"
"856","When it was cut down, he shouldered it together with its leaves and quietly walked away.","nǎi duàn。 dài yè hé jiān tóu。 cóng róng xú bù ér qù。","乃斷。帶葉荷肩頭。從容徐步而去。"
"857","From the beginning, while the priest was performing this trick, the peasant was in the crowd straining his neck and staring in wonder.","chū dào shì zuò fǎ shí。 xiāng rén yì zá zhòng zhōng。 yǐn lǐng zhù mù。","初道士作法時。鄉人亦雜眾中。引領注目。"
"858","His business was entirely forgotten.","jìng wàng qí yè。","竟忘其業。"
"859","As soon as the priest had gone, he then looked at his cart, all his pears were gone.","dào shì jì qù。 shǐ gù chē zhōng zé lí yǐ kōng yǐ。","道士既去。始顧車中則梨已空矣。"
"860","He then knew that the things that had just been distributed were all his things.","fāng wù shì suǒ biāo sàn。 jiē jǐ wù yě。","方悟適所俵散。皆己物也。"
"861","Further, looking more closely at the cart, he discovered that one of the handles was missing having been newly broken off.","yòu xì shì chē shàng yī bǎ wáng。 shì xīn záo duàn zhě。","又細視車上一靶亡。是新鑿斷者。"
"862","He was greatly enraged.","xīn dà fèn hèn。","心大憤恨。"
"863","Quickly he set out in pursuit of the priest, and just as he turned a corner of the wall, he came upon the handle which had been cast away under the wall.","jí jì zhī。 zhuǎn guò qiáng yú。 zé duàn bǎ qì yuán xià。","急迹之。轉過牆隅。則斷靶棄垣下。"
"864","Then he knew that the cut pear-tree was this thing.","shǐ zhī suǒ fá lí běn。 jí shì wù yě。","始知所伐梨本。即是物也。"
"865","The priest was no where to be seen.","dào shì bù zhī suǒ zài。","道士不知所在。"
"866","All in the market-place laughed.","yī shì càn rán。","一市粲然。"
"867","As the present Import Tariff had already exceeded its ten year limit, on August 14th last year this Ministry addressed a note to the Ministers of the signatory Powers to the said Tariff at Peking, signifying China's desire to revise it.","àn chá xiàn xíng yáng huò jìn kǒu shuì zé。 yǐ jiè shí nián qī mǎn。 yè jīng běn bù jiāng xiū gǎi zhī yì。 yú qù nián bā yuè shí sì rì zhào huì。 céng jīng qiān zì。 yú gāi shuì zé zhī gè guó zhù jīng dà chén zài àn。","案查現行洋貨進口稅則。已屆十年期滿。業經本部將修改之意。於去年八月十四日照會。曾經簽字。於該稅則之各國駐京大臣在案。"
"868","As the Government of the Chinese Republic is now maintaining its formal and perfect relations with the friendly Powers, foreign trade with China is bound to flourish.","xiàn mín guó zhèng fǔ。 jì yǔ gè yǒu bāng bǎo chí zhèng shì wán quán zhī guān xì。 zhōng wài shāng wù。 bì jiāng rì zhēn fā dá。","現民國政府。既與各友邦保持正式完全之關係。中外商務。必將日臻發達。"
"869","Therefore the aforesaid Tariff naturally should be thoroughly revised to the advantage of both our revenue collection and foreign commercial interests.","qián xiàng jìn kǒu shuì zé。 zì yīng qiè shí xiū gǎi。 yǐ qī yú shuì kè shāng qíng。 liǎng yǒu bì yì。","前項進口稅則。自應切實修改。以期於稅課商情。兩有裨益。"
"870","I have the honor therefore to communicate it to Your Excellency for your information and to request you to favor me with a reply, so that negotiations over the question may be conducted at the earliest possible moment.","xiāng yīng zhào huì guì gōng shǐ chá zhào jiàn fù。 yǐ biàn zǎo rì shāng bàn cǐ shì。 shì suǒ gǎn pàn。","相應照會貴公使查照見復。以便早日商辦此事。是所感盼。"
"871","In our recent conversation Your Excellency stated, with reference to the revision of the Import Tariff, that although no nations had manifested any disinclination in connection with the communications of August 14, 1912 and October 14, 1913 from the Ministry of Foreign Affairs, several nations had as yet made no reply.","xiū gǎi jìn kǒu shuì zé zhī shì。 rì qián huì wù shí。 guì zǒng zhǎng miàn chēng。 xiàn zài gè guó zhèng fǔ duì yú shàng nián bā yuè shí sì rì jí běn nián shí yuè shí sì rì běn bù zhào huì。 suī wú biàn bó zhī chù。 rán shàng yǒu duō shù guó jiā wèi céng dá fù děng yǔ。","修改進口稅則之事。日前會晤時。貴總長面稱。現在各國政府對於上年八月十四日及本年十月十四日本部照會。雖無辯駁之處。然尚有多數國家未曾答復等語。"
"872","I have the honor to inform Your Excellency that the proposal of the Government of the Republic of China to revise the existing Tariff in order that an effective five per cent duty may be collected meets with the approval of the American Government.","chá guì zhèng fǔ nǐ dìng xiū gǎi xiàn xíng shuì zé。 bǐ jiāng zhí bǎi chōu wǔ bàn fǎ qiè shí zhēng shōu。 běn guó zhèng fǔ shēn yuàn biǎo shì tóng qíng。 běn gōng shǐ yòng tè shēng míng。 xiāng yīng zhào fù guì zǒng zhǎng chá zhào kě yě。","查貴政府擬定修改現行稅則。俾將值百抽五辦法切實征收。本國政府深願表示同情。本公使用特聲明。相應照復貴總長查照可也。"
"873","Why can one not deal with it according to the old plan?","yīn hé bù kě zhào jiù bàn lǐ","因何不可照舊辦理"

"882","The Master said, ""The superior man does not promote a man simply on account of his words; nor does he put aside good words because of the man.","zǐ yuē。 jūn zǐ bù yǐ yán jǔ rén， bù yǐ rén fèi yán","子曰。君子不以言舉人，不以人廢言"
"883","Among the members of the body there are some honorable, some ignoble; there are some small, some great. One must not injure the great for the small, nor injure the honorable for the ignoble.","tǐ yǒu guì jiàn。 yǒu xiǎo dà， wú yǐ xiǎo hài dà。 wú yǐ jiàn hài guì","體有貴賤。有小大，無以小害大。無以賤害貴"
"884","Gold or iron even in small quantities is heavy, because their particles are compact. "885","The king Hui of Liang for the matter of territory destroyed his people.","liáng huì wáng yǐ tǔ dì zhī gù， mí làn qí mín","梁惠王以土地之故，糜爛其民"

"896","During the reign of Ch'ung-Chen he was in the capital for the palace examinations.","chóng zhēn jiān。 diàn shì zài dōu。","崇禎間。殿試在都。"
"897","His servant became ill and was unable to rise, and Yü was very anxious about him.","pū yì bù qǐ。 huàn zhī。","僕疫不起。患之。"
"898","In the market-place there was a clever diviner who could forecast life and death, and Yü intended to ask him about his servant.","huì shì yǒu shàn bǔ zhě。 néng jué rén shēng sǐ。 jiāng dài wèn zhī。","會市有善卜者。能決人生死。將代問之。"
"899","When he came to his place, before he had uttered a word, the diviner spoke, saying, ""Were you not going to ask about your servant's illness, sir?","jì zhì。 wèi yán。 bǔ zhě yuē。 jūn mò yù wèn pū bìng hū。","既至。未言。卜者曰。君莫欲問僕病乎。"
"900","Yü was startled and acknowledged that he was.","gōng hài yìng zhī。","公駭應之。"
"901","The diviner then said, ""His illness is not serious, but you, sir, are in danger.","yuē。 bìng zhě wú hài。 jūn kě wēi。","曰。病者無害。君可危。"
"902","Whereupon Yü wished to have his fortune told.","gōng nǎi zì bǔ。","公乃自卜。"
"903","The diviner cast his horoscope and in alarm said, ""Sir, in three days you must die!","bǔ zhě qǐ guà。 è rán yuē。 jūn sān rì dāng sǐ。","卜者起卦。愕然曰。君三日當死。"
"904","Yü stood for a long time stunned and frightened, and the diviner quietly said, ""I have a little skill in magic. If you will give me ten ounces of silver, I will break the evil spell.","gōng jīng chà liáng jiǔ。 bǔ zhě cóng róng yuē。 bǐ rén yǒu xiǎo shù。 bào wǒ shí jīn。 dāng dài ráng zhī。","公驚詫良久。卜者從容曰。鄙人有小術。報我十金。當代禳之。"
"905","Yü reflected that life and death were predetermined, how then could any magic change the course of events? So he refused and started to go away.","gōng zì niàn shēng sǐ yǐ dìng。 shù qǐ néng jiě。 bù yìng ér qǐ。 yù chū。","公自念生死已定。術豈能解。不應而起。欲出。"
"906","You find it hard"", said the diviner, ""to part with this small sum, beware you do not repent later"".","bǔ zhě yuē。 xī cǐ xiǎo fèi。 wù huǐ wù huǐ。","卜者曰。惜此小費。勿悔勿悔。"
"907","Yü's friends were all alarmed for him and urged him to empty his purse to beg the diviner's help. But Yü would not listen.","ài gōng zhě jiē wèi gōng jù。 quàn qìng tuó yǐ āi zhī。 gōng bù tīng。","愛公者皆為公懼。勸罄橐以哀之。公不聽。"
"908","The third day came quickly, and Yü was sitting in the inn waiting quietly and keeping close watch.","shū hū zhì sān rì。 gōng duān zuò lǚ shè。 jìng yǐ chān zhī。","倏忽至三日。公端坐旅舍。靜以覘之。"
"909","Nothing happened to him the whole day, and at night-fall he closed the door, trimmed the lamp, and leaning on his sword sat and waited.","zhōng rì wú yàng。 zhì yè hé hù tiāo dēng。 yǐ jiàn wēi zuò。","終日無恙。至夜闔戶挑燈。倚劍危坐。"

"1037","Thereupon they all sought cups and bowls and raced to drain their goblets for fear the wine-pot should be emptied.","suì gè mì àng yú。 jìng yǐn xiān jiào。 wéi kǒng zūn jǐn。","遂各覓盎盂。競飲先釂。惟恐樽盡。"
"1038","But again and again wine was poured out, and to his surprise it never seemed to grow less.","ér wǎng fù yì zhù。 jìng bù shǎo jiǎn。 xīn qí zhī。","而往復挹注。竟不少減。心奇之。"
"1039","Article 3. The national law of a foreign juridical person whose existence is recognized by Chinese law is the law of his domicile.","dì sān tiáo wài guó fǎ rén jīng zhōng guó fǎ rèn xǔ chéng lì zhě。 yǐ qí zhù suǒ dì fǎ。 wéi qí běn guó fǎ。","第三條外國法人經中國法認許成立者。以其住所地法。為其本國法。"
"1040","Article 4. When, according to these rules, the national law of the party is to be applied, and according to his national law Chinese law should be applied, then the Chinese law shall be used.","dì sì tiáo yī běn tiáo lì shì yòng dāng shì rén běn guó fǎ shí。 rú yī qí běn guó fǎ yīng shì yòng zhōng guó fǎ zhě。 yī zhōng guó fǎ。","第四條依本條例適用當事人本國法時。如依其本國法應適用中國法者。依中國法。"
"1041","Article 5. The capacity of a person is governed by his national law.","dì wǔ tiáo rén zhī néng lì。 yī qí běn guó fǎ。","第五條人之能力。依其本國法。"
"1042","A foreigner who has no capacity according to his national law, but has it according to the law of China, is considered to have capacity in regard to his juridical acts in China, excepting in such cases as concern family law and the law of succession and those affecting immovables situate abroad.","wài guó rén yī qí běn guó fǎ wéi wú néng lì。 ér yī zhōng guó fǎ wéi yǒu néng lì zhě。 jiù qí zài zhōng guó zhī fǎ lǜ xíng wéi shì wéi yǒu néng lì。 dàn guān yú yī qīn zú fǎ jì chéng fǎ jí zài wài guó bù dòng chǎn zhī fǎ lǜ xíng wéi。 bù zài cǐ xiàn。","外國人依其本國法為無能力。而依中國法為有能力者。就其在中國之法律行為視為有能力。但關於依親族法繼承法及在外國不動產之法律行為。不在此限。"
"1043","A foreigner who has capacity, but, on acquiring Chinese nationality, according to Chinese law is considered to have no capacity, still retains his original capacity.","yǒu néng lì zhī wài guó rén。 qǔ dé zhōng guó guó jí。 yī zhōng guó fǎ wéi wú néng lì shí。 réng bǎo chí qí gù yǒu zhī néng lì。","有能力之外國人。取得中國國籍。依中國法為無能力時。仍保持其固有之能力。"
"1044","The man of perfect virtue, wishing to be established himself, seeks also to establish others; wishing to be enlarged himself, he seeks also to enlarge others.","fū rén zhě。 jǐ yù lì ér lì rén。 jǐ yù dá ér dá rén。","夫仁者。己欲立而立人。己欲達而達人。"
"1045","When the prince is merciful, the country has no enemies.","fú guó jūn hào rén。 tiān xià wú dí yān","夫國君好仁。天下無敵焉"
"1046","War is like fire,—if not stopped, it will spread itself.","fú bīng yóu huǒ yě。 fú jí jiāng zì fén yě。","夫兵猶火也。弗戢將自焚也。"
"1047","As to Yu and Ch'iu, they may be called ordinary ministers.","jīn yóu yǔ qiú yě。 kě wèi jù chén yǐ","今由與求也。可謂具臣矣"
"1048","In the most ancient times there were some who did not inter their parents.","gài shàng shì cháng yǒu bù zàng qí qīn zhě","蓋上世嘗有不葬其親者"
"1049","For an extraordinary deed an extraordinary man is needed.","gài yǒu fēi cháng zhī gōng。 bì dài yǒu fēi cháng zhī rén","蓋有非常之功。必待有非常之人"
"1050","Among the shepherds of men throughout the empire there is not one who does not find pleasure in killing men.","jīn fú tiān xià zhī rén mù。 wèi yǒu bù shì shā rén zhě","今夫天下之人牧。未有不嗜殺人者"
"1051","By striking water and causing it to leap up, you may make it go over your forehead, and, by damming and leading it, you may force it up a hill;—but are such movements according to the nature of water?","jīn fú shuǐ。 bó ér yuè zhī。 kě shǐ guò sǎng。 jī ér xíng zhī。 kě shǐ zài shān。 shì qǐ shuǐ zhī xìng zāi","今夫水。搏而躍之。可使過顙。激而行之。可使在山。是豈水之性哉"
"1052","If the water is not deep, it cannot carry a large boat.","qiě fú shuǐ zhī jī yě bù hòu。 zé fù dà zhōu wú lì","且夫水之積也不厚。則負大舟無力"
"1053","The policy of a strong state is based on military force.","qiě fú qiáng guó zhī dào。 yǐ bīng wéi běn","且夫強國之道。以兵為本"
"1054","It is the law of nature that the strongest win, and the weakest lose.","qiě fú yōu shèng liè bài zhě。 tiān zhī gōng lì yě","且夫優勝劣敗者。天之公例也"
"1055","Suddenly one of the guests said, ""We sit here enjoying the moonlight. But we drink alone. Why not call up Ch'ang-E?","é yī kè yuē。 méng cì yuè míng zhī zhào。 nǎi ěr jì yǐn。 hé bù hū cháng é lái。","俄一客曰。蒙賜月明之照。乃爾寂飲。何不呼嫦娥來。"
"1056","Whereupon he threw a chopstick into the moon, and they saw a beautiful girl coming forth from the light,—at first not more than a foot high; but on touching the ground she became as tall as a man.","nǎi yǐ zhù zhì yuè zhōng。 jiàn yī měi rén。 zì guāng zhōng chū。 chū bù yíng chǐ。 zhì dì。 suì yǔ rén děng。","乃以箸擲月中。見一美人。自光中出。初不盈尺。至地。遂與人等。"
"1057","She had a slender waist and a beautiful neck and fluttered gracefully in the ""Colored Garment"" dance.","xiān yāo xiù xiàng。 piān piān zuò ní cháng wǔ。","纖腰秀項。翩翩作霓裳舞。"
"1058","When the dance was finished, she sang, ""Fairies! Fairies! Do you return? Do you intend to send me back to my lonely palace of the moon?","yǐ ér gē yuē。 xiān xiān hū。 ér hái hū。 ér yōu wǒ yú guǎng hán hū。","已而歌曰。仙仙乎。而還乎。而幽我於廣寒乎。"
"1059","Her voice was clear sonorous and ringing like a flute.","qí shēng qīng yuè。 liè rú xiāo guǎn。","其聲清越。烈如簫管。"
"1060","At the end of the song she turned round, jumped up on the table and before the astonished eyes of all, became again a chopstick. The three friends laughed loudly.","gē bì。 pán xuán ér qǐ。 yuè dēng jǐ shàng。 jīng gù zhī jiān。 yǐ fù wéi zhù。 sān rén dà xiào。","歌畢。盤旋而起。躍登几上。驚顧之間。已復為箸。三人大笑。"
"1061","Another guest said, ""This evening we are very happy, but we have drunk enough. Let us take a parting drink in the palace of the moon!","yòu yī kè yuē。 jīn xiāo zuì lè。 rán bù shèng jiǔ lì yǐ。 qí jiàn wǒ yú yuè gōng kě hū。","又一客曰。今宵最樂。然不勝酒力矣。其餞我於月宮可乎。"
"1062","So the three moved their feast, and gradually walked into the moon, where all saw them sitting and drinking, their beards and eyebrows plainly seen as though reflected in a mirror.","sān rén yí xí。 jiàn rù yuè zhōng。 zhòng shì sān rén。 zuò yuè zhōng yǐn。 xū méi bì xiàn。 rú yǐng zhī zài jìng zhōng。","三人移席。漸入月中。眾視三人。坐月中飲。鬚眉畢見。如影之在鏡中。"
"1063","After a while the moon darkened.","yí shí yuè jiàn àn。","移時月漸暗。"
"1064","The disciples brought lighted candles, and found the priest sitting alone. The guests had disappeared.","mén rén rán zhú lái。 zé dào shì dú zuò ér kè yǎo yǐ。","門人然燭來。則道士獨坐而客杳矣。"
"1065","Viands were still upon the table.","jǐ shàng yáo hé shàng cún。","几上肴核尚存。"
"1066","The moon on the wall was merely a paper,—round like a mirror.","bì shàng yuè。 zhǐ yuán rú jìng ér yǐ。","壁上月。紙圓如鏡而已。"
"1067","Has everybody had enough to drink?"" asked the priest. ""Quite enough"" replied the disciples.","dào shì wèn zhòng。 yǐn zú hū。 yuē zú yǐ。","道士問眾。飲足乎。曰足矣。"
"1068","In that case"", said the priest, ""you had better retire early so as not to be late for the fuel gathering to-morrow morning"". And so they retired.","zú yí zǎo qǐn。 wù wù qiáo sū。 zhòng nuò ér tuì。","足宜早寢。勿悞樵蘇。眾諾而退。"
"1069","Wang was very much pleased, and all his longing for home was forgotten.","wáng qiè xīn mù。 guī niàn suì xī。","王竊忻慕。歸念遂息。"
"1070","Another month passed, and the hardships became unbearable.","yòu yī yuè。 kǔ bù kě rěn。","又一月。苦不可忍。"
"1071","Moreover the priest had not taught him any magical skill, and he could stand it no longer.","ér dào shì bìng bù chuán jiào yī shù。 xīn bù néng dài。","而道士並不傳教一術。心不能待。"
"1072","Therefore he took his leave saying, ""I have come many miles to receive your instructions.","cí yuē。 dì zǐ shù bǎi lǐ shòu yè xiān shī。","辭曰。弟子數百里受業仙師。"
"1073","Though I cannot obtain the secret of immortality, still if I should receive instruction in some minor art, it also would satisfy my cravings for knowledge.","zòng bù néng dé cháng shēng shù。 huò xiǎo yǒu chuán xí。 yì kě wèi qiú jiào zhī xīn。","縱不能得長生術。或小有傳習。亦可慰求教之心。"
"1074","In these two or three months just passed, there has been nothing but going out early to gather fuel and returning in the evening.","jīn yuè liǎng sān yuè。 bù guò zǎo qiáo ér mù guī。","今閱兩三月。不過早樵而暮歸。"
"1075","At home I have not been accustomed to these hardships.","dì zǐ zài jiā。 wèi ān cǐ kǔ。","弟子在家。未諳此苦。"
"1076","Article 6. A foreigner who has his domicile or residence in China and, according to both his national law and the law of China, has reasons to be interdicted may be declared as interdicted.","dì liù tiáo fán zài zhōng guó yǒu zhù suǒ huò jū suǒ zhī wài guó rén。 yī qí běn guó fǎ。 jí zhōng guó fǎ。 tóng yǒu jìn zhì chǎn zhī yuán yīn zhě。 dé xuān gào jìn zhì chǎn。","第六條凡在中國有住所或居所之外國人。依其本國法。及中國法。同有禁治產之原因者。得宣告禁治產。"
"1077","Article 7. The provision of the last preceding article is applicable to quasi-interdiction.","dì qī tiáo qián tiáo guī dìng。 yú zhǔn jìn zhì chǎn zhě shì yòng zhī。","第七條前條規定。於準禁治產者適用之。"
"1078","Article 8. When a foreigner who has his domicile or residence in China is not known to be alive or dead, a declaration of death may be made according to Chinese law only in respect to his property in China and legal relations based on Chinese law.","dì bā tiáo fán zài zhōng guó yǒu zhù suǒ huò jū suǒ zhī wài guó rén。 shēng sǐ bù míng shí。 qí jiù qí zài zhōng guó zhī cái chǎn。 jí yīng yī zhōng guó fǎ lǜ zhī fǎ lǜ guān xì。 dé yī zhōng guó fǎ wéi sǐ wáng zhī xuān gào。","第八條凡在中國有住所或居所之外國人。生死不明時。祇就其在中國之財產。及應依中國法律之法律關係。得依中國法為死亡之宣告。"
"1079","To see what is right and not to do it is want of courage.","jiàn yì bù wéi。 wú yǒng yě","見義不為。無勇也"

"1119","The Master said, ""The 'Feng' bird does not come; the river sends forth no map:—it is all over with me!","fèng niǎo bù zhì。 hé bù chū tú。 wú yǐ yǐ fú","鳳鳥不至。河不出圖。吾已矣夫"
"1120","The priest laughingly said, ""I certainly told you, you could not endure hardships, and to-day it is proved. To-morrow early I will send you home"".","dào shì xiào yuē。 wǒ gù wèi bù néng zuò kǔ。 jīn guǒ rán。 míng zǎo dāng qiǎn rǔ xíng。","道士笑曰。我固謂不能作苦。今果然。明早當遣汝行。"
"1121","I have worked for you,"" replied Wang, ""many days. Will you not impart a little of your skill, that my coming may not have been in vain"".","wáng yuē。 dì zǐ cāo zuò duō rì。 shī lüè shòu xiǎo jì。 cǐ lái wéi bù fù yě。","王曰。弟子操作多日。師略授小技。此來為不負也。"
"1122","For what do you ask?"" asked the priest. Wang replied, ""I have noticed that wherever you go, walls are no obstacle to you. To learn only that secret would be enough.","dào shì wèn hé shù zhī qiú。 yuē。 měi jiàn shī xíng chù。 qiáng bì suǒ bù néng gé。 dàn dé cǐ fǎ zú yǐ。","道士問何術之求。曰。每見師行處。牆壁所不能隔。但得此法足矣。"
"1123","The priest laughed and assented.","dào shì xiào ér yǔn zhī。","道士笑而允之。"
"1124","Whereupon he taught Wang a formula and bade him recite. When he had done so, the priest shouted, ""Go through it!","nǎi chuán yǐ jué。 lìng zì zhòu bì。 hū yuē。 rù zhī。","乃傳以訣。令自咒畢。呼曰。入之。"
"1125","Wang facing the wall did not dare to move forward.","wáng miàn qiáng bù gǎn rù。","王面牆不敢入。"
"1126","Try to enter it,"" said again the priest.","yòu yuē。 shì rù zhī。","又曰。試入之。"
"1127","Wang slowly advanced, reached the wall and was there stopped.","wáng guǒ cóng róng rù。 jí qiáng ér zǔ。","王果從容入。及牆而阻。"
"1128","The priest said, ""Hang your head down and enter quickly. Do not hesitate!","dào shì yuē。 fǔ shǒu zhòu rù。 wù qūn xún。","道士曰。俛首驟入。勿逡巡。"
"1129","Wang took several steps from the wall and rushed at it. At the wall it seemed to him there was no wall, and looking back he found himself outside.","wáng guǒ qù qiáng shù bù。 bēn ér rù。 jí qiáng。 xū ruò wú wù。 huí shì。 guǒ zài qiáng wài yǐ。","王果去牆數步。奔而入。及牆。虛若無物。回視。果在牆外矣。"
"1130","Overjoyed he returned and expressed his gratitude to the priest.","dà xǐ。 rù xiè。","大喜。入謝。"
"1131","When you return home, you must be clean. Otherwise it will not be effective"", said the priest.","dào shì yuē。 guī yí jié chí。 fǒu zé bù yàn。","道士曰。歸宜潔持。否則不驗。"
"1132","Then he gave Wang travelling expenses and sent him home.","suì zī fǔ qiǎn zhī guī。","遂資斧遣之歸。"
"1133","At home Wang bragged that he had seen the immortals and that no wall was a barrier to him.","dǐ jiā。 zì xǔ yù xiān。 jiān bì suǒ bù néng zǔ。","抵家。自詡遇仙。堅壁所不能阻。"
"1134","His wife did not believe his words.","qī bù xìn。","妻不信。"
"1135","Thereupon Wang repeated his former performance. He stepped back several steps from the wall and rushed at it at full speed.","wáng xiào qí zuò wéi。 qù qiáng shù chǐ。 bēn ér rù。","王傚其作為。去牆數尺。奔而入。"
"1136","His head struck the hard wall and he fell down suddenly.","tóu chù yìng bì。 mò rán ér bó。","頭觸硬壁。驀然而踣。"
"1137","His wife helped him up and looked at him. On his forehead was a lump as big as a large egg.","qī fú shì zhī。 é shàng fén qǐ。 rú jù luǎn yān。","妻扶視之。額上墳起。如巨卵焉。"
"1138","His wife made fun of him, and Wang half ashamed half angry cursed the old priest calling him a wicked fellow.","qī yé yú zhī。 wáng cán fèn。 mà dào shì wú liáng ér yǐ。","妻揶揄之。王慚忿。罵道士無良而已。"
"1139","Article 9. The essentials of a marriage are governed by the respective national laws of the parties.","dì jiǔ tiáo hūn yīn chéng lì zhī yào jiàn。 yī dāng shì rén gè gāi běn guó fǎ。","第九條婚姻成立之要件。依當事人各該本國法。"
"1140","Article 10. The effect of a marriage is governed by the national law of the husband.","dì shí tiáo hūn yīn zhī xiào lì。 yī fū zhī běn guó fǎ。","第十條婚姻之效力。依夫之本國法。"
"1141","The property relations between husband and wife are governed by the national law of the husband in effect at the time of the formation of the marriage.","fū fù cái chǎn zhì。 yī hūn yīn chéng lì shí。 fū zhī běn guó fǎ。","夫婦財產制。依婚姻成立時。夫之本國法。"
"1142","Article 11. A divorce may be decreed upon a ground which, at the time of its occurrence, is by the national law of the husband and the law of China considered as constituting a ground for divorce.","dì shí yī tiáo lí hūn yī qí shì shí fā shēng shí。 fū zhī běn guó fǎ。 jí zhōng guó fǎ jūn rèn qí shì shí wéi lí hūn yuán yīn zhě。 dé xuān gào zhī。","第十一條離婚依其事實發生時。夫之本國法。及中國法均認其事實為離婚原因者。得宣告之。"
"1143","Article 12. The status of a child is governed by the national law of the husband of the mother existing at the time of its birth and, if he died before its birth, governed by the law of the country to which he last belonged.","dì shí èr tiáo zǐ zhī shēn fèn。 yī chū shēng shí qí mǔ zhī fū zhī běn guó fǎ。 rú qí fū yú zǐ chū shēng qián yǐ sǐ shí。 yī qí zuì hòu suǒ shǔ guó zhī fǎ lǜ。","第十二條子之身分。依出生時其母之夫之本國法。如其夫於子出生前已死時。依其最後所屬國之法律。"
"1144","Article 13. The essentials of legitimation of an illegitimate child are governed by the respective national laws of the legitimating parent and the legitimized child.","dì shí sān tiáo sī shēng zǐ rèn lǐng zhī chéng lì yào jiàn。 yī rèn lǐng zhě yǔ bèi rèn lǐng zhě。 gè gāi běn guó fǎ。","第十三條私生子認領之成立要件。依認領者與被認領者。各該本國法。"
"1145","The effect of legitimation is governed by the national law of the legitimating parents.","rèn lǐng zhī xiào lì。 yī rèn lǐng zhě zhī běn guó fǎ。","認領之效力。依認領者之本國法。"
"1146","Filial piety and fraternal submission!—are they not the root of all benevolent actions?","xiào dì yě zhě。 qí wéi rén zhī běn yú","孝弟也者。其為仁之本與"
"1147","The Master's mode of asking information!—is it not different from that of other men?","fū zǐ zhī qiú zhī yě。 qí zhū yì hū rén zhī qiú zhī yú","夫子之求之也。其諸異乎人之求之與"
"1148","The Master said, ""Never flagging when I set forth anything to him;—ah! that is Hui.","zǐ yuē。 yù zhī ér bù duò zhě。 qí huí yě yú","子曰。語之而不惰者。其回也與"
"1149","How greatly filial was Shun!","shùn qí dà xiào yě yú","舜其大孝也與"
"1150","The Master said, ""He who puts on an appearance of stern firmness, while inwardly he is weak, is like small mean people; is he not like the thief who breaks through a wall?","zǐ yuē。 sè lì ér nèi rěn。 pì zhū xiǎo rén。 qí yóu chuān yú zhī dào yě yú","子曰。色厲而內荏。譬諸小人。其猶穿窬之盜也與"
"1151","Is it not delightful to have friends coming from distant quarters?","yǒu péng zì yuǎn fāng lái。 bù yì lè hū","有朋自遠方來。不亦樂乎"
"1152","It is Heaven who knows me!","zhī wǒ zhě。 qí tiān hū","知我者。其天乎"
"1153","How majestic was he in the works which he accomplished!","wēi wēi hū qí yǒu chéng gōng yě","巍巍乎其有成功也"
"1154","The Master said, ""Is virtue a thing remote? I wish to be virtuous,—and virtue is at hand.","zǐ yuē。 rén yuǎn hū zāi。 wǒ yù rén。 sī rén zhì yǐ","子曰。仁遠乎哉。我欲仁。斯仁至矣"
"1155","Though I may not get a great burial, shall I die upon the road?","yǔ zòng bù dé dà zàng。 yǔ sǐ yú dào lù hū","予縱不得大葬。予死於道路乎"
"1156","The Master said, ""It is all over! I have not yet seen one who could perceive his faults, and inwardly accuse himself.","zǐ yuē。 yǐ yǐ hū。 wú wèi jiàn néng jiàn qí guò。 ér nèi zì sòng zhě yě。","子曰。已矣乎。吾未見能見其過。而內自訟者也。"
"1157","The Master said, ""It is all over! I have not seen one who loves virtue as he loves beauty.","zǐ yuē。 yǐ yǐ hū。 wú wèi jiàn hào dé rú hào sè zhě yě","子曰。已矣乎。吾未見好德如好色者也"
"1158","The Master said, ""If a minister makes his own conduct correct, what difficulty will he have in assisting in government?","zǐ yuē。 gǒu zhèng qí shēn yǐ。 yú cóng zhèng hū hé yǒu","子曰。苟正其身矣。於從政乎何有"
"1159","If the words of a ruler be good, is it not also good that no one opposes them? But if they are not good, and no one opposes them, is it not then that a single sentence may bring the ruin of the country?","rú qí shàn。 ér mò zhī wéi yě。 bù yì shàn hū。 rú bù shàn。 ér mò zhī wéi yě。 bù jī hū yī yán ér sàng bāng hū","如其善。而莫之違也。不亦善乎。如不善。而莫之違也。不幾乎一言而喪邦乎"
"1160","I have no pleasure in being a prince.","yǔ wú lè hū wéi jūn","予無樂乎為君"
"1161","Food and raiment thereupon become plentiful.","yī shí yú shì hū zú","衣食於是乎足"
"1162","The Master said, ""Small indeed was the capacity of Kuan-Chung!","zǐ yuē。 guǎn zhòng zhī qì。 xiǎo zāi","子曰。管仲之器。小哉"
"1163","Great indeed was Yao as a sovereign!","dà zāi。 yáo zhī wéi jūn yě","大哉。堯之為君也"
"1164","True indeed is this saying!","chéng zāi。 shì yán yě","誠哉。是言也"
"1165","Beautiful indeed is the garden!","měi zāi。 yuán yě","美哉。園也"
"1166","Can it be without some reason that he has invited me?","fú zhào wǒ zhě。 ér qǐ tú zāi","夫召我者。而豈徒哉"
"1167","How should he not know that when a man is old he must die?","qǐ bù zhī rén lǎo bì sǐ","豈不知人老必死"
"1168","How can one sanction this matter, which will trouble the people and trouble the officials?","qǐ kě zhǔn cǐ lěi mín lěi guān zhī shì","豈可准此累民累官之事"
"1169","How can this be right?","qǐ qí rán hū","豈其然乎"
"1170","Is it not great?","qǐ bù dà yú","豈不大與"
"1171","My eldest sister's husband's grandfather, the late Mr. Sung-Tao, was a salaried licentiate in our city.","yǔ zǐ fū zhī zǔ。 sòng gōng。 huì dào。 yì lǐn shēng。","予姊夫之祖。宋公。諱燾。邑廩生。"
"1172","One day while lying ill, he saw an official servant with a document, leading a horse with a white forehead, approach him with a message that he was summoned to the examination.","yī rì bìng wò。 jiàn lì chí dié。 qiān bái diān mǎ lái yún。 qǐng fù shì。","一日病臥。見吏持牒。牽白顛馬來云。請赴試。"
"1173","Sung said, ""The provincial examiner has not yet arrived. Why should I hurry to be examined?","gōng yán wén zōng wèi lín。 hé jù dé kǎo。","公言文宗未臨。何遽得考。"
"1174","The messenger did not reply, but pressed him to go.","lì bù yán。 dàn dūn cù zhī。","吏不言。但敦促之。"
"1175","Sung overcame his sickness, got on the horse and followed after.","gōng lì jí chéng mǎ cóng qù。","公力疾乘馬從去。"
"1176","The road was quite strange.","lù shén shēng shū。","路甚生疏。"
"1177","They reached a city resembling the capital of a prince.","zhì yī chéng guō。 rú wáng zhě dū。","至一城郭。如王者都。"
"1178","They shortly entered a palace the buildings of which were very beautiful and imposing.","yí shí rù fǔ xiè。 gōng shì zhuàng lì。","移時入府廨。宮室壯麗。"
"1179","Ten or more officials were seated at the upper end, all strangers to him, except Kuan-Chuang-mu whom he recognized.","shàng zuò shí yú guān。 dōu bù zhī hé rén。 wéi guān zhuàng miù kě shí。","上坐十餘官。都不知何人。惟關壯繆可識。"
"1180","Below, under the eaves, were put two tables and two chairs.","yán xià shè jǐ dūn gè èr。","簷下設几墩各二。"
"1181","There was a graduate already seated at the end.","xiān yǒu yī xiù cái zuò qí mò。","先有一秀才坐其末。"
"1182","Sung then sat down shoulder to shoulder with him.","gōng biàn yǔ lián jiān。","公便與連肩。"
"1183","Writing materials were prepared on the tables, and a piece of paper with a theme on it fluttered down from above.","jǐ shàng gè yǒu bǐ zhá。 é tí zhǐ fēi xià。","几上各有筆札。俄題紙飛下。"
"1184","Looking at it, they saw the eight following words, ""One man; two men. With intention; without intention.","shì zhī。 bā zì yún。 yī rén èr rén。 yǒu xīn wú xīn。","視之。八字云。一人二人。有心無心。"
"1185","When they both finished their essays, they passed them up.","èr gōng wén chéng。 chéng diàn shàng。","二公文成。呈殿上。"
"1186","In Sung's essay there was the following passage, ""For intended good, although it is good, there is no reward. For unintended wrong, although it is wrong, there is no punishment.","gōng wén zhōng yǒu yún。 yǒu xīn wéi shàn。 suī shàn bù shǎng。 wú xīn wéi è。 suī è bù fá。","公文中有云。有心為善。雖善不賞。無心為惡。雖惡不罰。"
"1187","It was unanimously praised as it was circulated among the deities, and Sung was summoned into their presence and thus ordered, ""Honan lacks a Guardian God. You are designated to this post.","zhū shén chuán zàn bù yǐ。 zhào gōng shàng。 yù yuē。 hé nán quē yī chéng huáng。 jūn chēng qí zhí。","諸神傳贊不已。召公上。諭曰。河南缺一城隍。君稱其職。"
"1188","The essentials of adoption are governed by the respective laws of the parties.","yǎng zǐ chéng lì zhī yào jiàn。 yī dāng shì rén gè gāi běn guó fǎ。","養子成立之要件。依當事人各該本國法。"
"1189","The effect of adoption is governed by the national law of the adopting parents.","yǎng zǐ zhī xiào lì。 yī yǎng fù mǔ zhī běn guó fǎ。","養子之效力。依養父母之本國法。"
"1190","The legal relations between parents and child are governed by the national law of the father, and, if it has no father, by the national law of the mother.","fù mǔ yǔ zǐ zhī fǎ lǜ guān xì。 xì yī fù zhī běn guó fǎ。 wú fù zhě。 yī mǔ zhī běn guó fǎ。","父母與子之法律關係。係依父之本國法。無父者。依母之本國法。"
"1191","The duty of maintenance is governed by the national law of the party liable, with the exception of the cases when the right of maintenance is denied by Chinese law.","fú yǎng zhī yì wù。 yī fú yǎng yì wù zhě zhī běn guó fǎ。 dàn fú yǎng quán lì zhī qǐng qiú。 wéi zhōng guó fǎ suǒ bù xǔ zhě。 bù zài cǐ xiàn。","扶養之義務。依扶養義務者之本國法。但扶養權利之請求。為中國法所不許者。不在此限。"
"1192","Family relations other than those provided in the last preceding eight articles and the rights and obligations arising out of such relations are governed by the national law of the party.","qián bā tiáo yǐ wài zhī qīn zú guān xì。 jí yīn qí guān xì suǒ shēng zhī quán lì yì wù。 yī dāng shì rén zhī běn guó fǎ。","前八條以外之親族關係。及因其關係所生之權利義務。依當事人之本國法。"
"1193","From what do you know that I am competent?","hé yóu zhī wú kě yě","何由知吾可也"
"1194","Since your Majesty deems them excellent, why do you not practise them?","wáng rú shàn zhī。 zé hé wéi bù xíng","王如善之。則何為不行"
"1195","How shall I know that they have no ability and so avoid employing them?","wú hé yǐ shí qí bù cái。 ér shě zhī","吾何以識其不才。而舍之"
"1196","The Master said, ""Heaven produced the virtue that is in me. Huan-T'ui, what can he do to me?","zǐ yuē。 tiān shēng dé yú yǔ。 huán tuī qí rú yǔ hé","子曰。天生德於予。桓魋其如予何"
"1197","What does he do?","fú hé wéi zāi","夫何為哉"
"1198","Does Heaven speak?","tiān hé yán zāi","天何言哉"
"1199","What was there to choose between an ox and a sheep?","niú yáng hé zé yān","牛羊何擇焉"
"1200","The Master said, ""Am I possessed of knowledge?","zǐ yuē。 wú yǒu zhì hū zāi","子曰。吾有知乎哉"
"1201","Whom should I impose upon? Should I impose upon Heaven?","wú shuí qī。 qī tiān hū","吾誰欺。欺天乎"
"1202","The Master said, ""Do you think, my friends, that I have any secrets?","zǐ yuē。 èr sān zǐ yǐ wǒ wéi yǐn hū","子曰。二三子以我為隱乎"
"1203","When the Master comes to any country, he does not fail to learn all about its government. Does he ask for his information or is it given to him?","fū zǐ zhì yú shì bāng yě。 bì wén qí zhèng。 qiú zhī yú。 yì yǔ zhī yú","夫子至於是邦也。必聞其政。求之與。抑與之與"
"1204","When the superior man practices high principles, is it also his aim to seek for a living in the future thereby?","jūn zǐ zhī wéi dào yě。 qí zhì yì jiāng yǐ qiú shí yú","君子之為道也。其志亦將以求食與"
"1205","May I hear from you what is it that your Majesty greatly desires?","wáng zhī suǒ dà yù。 kě dé wén yú","王之所大欲。可得聞與"
"1206","Is it as bad as that?","ruò shì qí shèn yú","若是其甚與"
"1207","Chi-K'ang-tzu asked about Chung-yu, whether he was fit to be employed as an officer of the government.","jì kāng zǐ wèn zhòng yóu。 kě shǐ cóng zhèng yě yú","季康子問仲由。可使從政也與"
"1208","Tzu-kung said, ""There is a beautiful gem here. Should I lay it up in a case and keep it or should I seek for a good price and sell it?","zǐ gòng yuē。 yǒu měi yù yú sī。 yùn dú ér cáng zhū。 qiú shàn jià ér gū zhū","子貢曰。有美玉於斯。韞匵而藏諸。求善賈而沽諸"
"1209","Raise to office those whom you know. As to those whom you do not know, will others neglect them?","jǔ ěr suǒ zhī。 ěr suǒ bù zhī。 rén qí shě zhū","舉爾所知。爾所不知。人其舍諸"
"1210","Is there a single sentence which can ruin a country?","yī yán ér sàng bāng。 yǒu zhū","一言而喪邦。有諸"
"1211","The prince Hsüan of Ch'i said to Mencius, ""People all tell me to pull down the Brilliant Palace. Shall I put it down, or stop the movement for that object?","qí xuān wáng wèn yuē。 rén jiē wèi wǒ huǐ míng táng。 huǐ zhū yǐ hū","齊宣王問曰。人皆謂我毀明堂。毀諸已乎"
"1212","When you do not know life, how can you know about death?","wèi zhī shēng。 yān zhī sǐ","未知生。焉知死"
"1213","Sir, in carrying on your government, why should you use killing at all?","zǐ wéi zhèng。 yān yòng shā","子為政。焉用殺"
"1214","Why use an ox-knife to kill a fowl?","gē jī yān yòng niú dāo","割雞焉用牛刀"
"1215","The Master said, ""A youth is to be regarded with respect. How do we know that his future will not be equal to our present?","zǐ yuē。 hòu shēng kě wèi。 yān zhī lái zhě zhī bù rú jīn yě","子曰。後生可畏。焉知來者之不如今也"
"1216","The Master said to his disciples, ""Come, let each of you tell his wishes.","zǐ yuē。 hé gè yán ěr zhì","子曰。盍各言爾志"
"1217","The duke Ai inquired of Yu-jo, saying, ""The year is one of scarcity, and the income is not sufficient for expenditure; what is to be done?"" Yu-jo replied to him, ""Why not tithe the people?","āi gōng wèn yú yǒu ruò yuē。 nián jī yòng bù zú。 rú zhī hé。 yǒu ruò duì yuē。 hé chè hū","哀公問於有若曰。年饑用不足。如之何。有若對曰。盍徹乎"
"1218","The bandits are coming; why not leave?","kòu zhì。 hé qù zhū","寇至。盍去諸"
"1219","Sir, why are you not engaged in the government?","zǐ xī bù wéi zhèng","子奚不為政"
"1220","Tzu-lu said, ""The ruler of Wei has been waiting for you, in order to administer the government with you. What will you consider the first thing to be done?","zǐ lù yuē。 wèi jūn dài zǐ ér wéi zhèng。 zǐ jiāng xī xiān","子路曰。衛君待子而為政。子將奚先"
"1221","What do you think of it, sir?","zǐ yǐ wéi xī","子以為奚"
"1222","How can he become my friend?","xī kě yǐ yǔ wǒ yǒu。","奚可以與我友。"
"1223","If he can bear to do this, what may he not bear to do?","shì kě rěn yě。 shú bù kě rěn yě","是可忍也。孰不可忍也"
"1224","If the people have plenty, their prince will not be left to want alone. If the people are in want, their prince cannot enjoy plenty alone.","bǎi xìng zú。 jūn shú yǔ bù zú。 bǎi xìng bù zú。 jūn shú yǔ zú","百姓足。君孰與不足。百姓不足。君孰與足"
"1225","Shun had the empire. Who gave it to him?","shùn yǒu tiān xià。 shú yǔ zhī","舜有天下。孰與之"
"1226","Rank or life, which is more important?","gōng míng yǔ shēn mìng shú zhòng","功名與身命孰重"
"1227","Contrary to all expectations, the matter has been finished.","jù zhī qí shì jìng chéng","詎知其事竟成"

"1229","How could a man without great talent accomplish a great deed?","wú dà cái。 jù néng chéng dà shì","無大才。詎能成大事"
"1230","How can it be thus?","ān néng rú cǐ","安能如此"
"1231","Where is the master?","zhǔ rén ān zài hū","主人安在乎"

"1233","Is it so or not?","shì yé。 fēi yé","是耶。非耶"

"1235","I am extremely delighted.","hé shèng xīn yuè","曷勝欣躍"

"1237","Where is he now?","hé zhì zāi","曷至哉"
