"1244","How dare he withstand me?","bǐ wū gǎn dāng wǒ zāi","彼惡敢當我哉"
"1245","Where is that paternal relation to the people?","wū zài qí wéi mín fù mǔ yě","惡在其為民父母也"
"1246","Benevolence is the dwelling-place of the heart, and righteousness the path of life.","jū wū zài。 rén shì yě。 lù wū zài。 yì shì yě","居惡在。仁是也。路惡在。義是也"
"1247","How can the empire be settled?","tiān xià wū hū dìng","天下惡乎定"
"1248","If a superior man abandon virtue, how can he fulfil the requirements of that name?","jūn zǐ qù rén。 wū hū chéng míng","君子去仁。惡乎成名"
"1249","Sung then realized what had happened, bowed his head and wept, saying, ""I am highly and unworthily honored with your commands. How could I dare to refuse?","gōng fāng wù。 dùn shǒu qì yuē。 rǔ yīng chǒng mìng。 hé gǎn duō cí。","公方悟。頓首泣曰。辱膺寵命。何敢多辭。"
"1250","And yet my old mother is seventy years old, and there is none to care for her. I beg of you to permit me to wait till the end of her life, and then I will be at your disposal.","dàn lǎo mǔ qī xún。 fèng yǎng wú rén。 qǐng dé zhōng qí tiān nián。 wéi tīng lù yòng。","但老母七旬。奉養無人。請得終其天年。惟聽錄用。"
"1251","There was one among them resembling an emperor, who commanded that they search out his mother's term of life.","shàng yī dì wáng xiàng zhě。 jí lìng jī mǔ shòu jí。","上一帝王像者。即令稽母壽籍。"
"1252","A long-haired servant brought in the Book of Life and turning the leaves over examined it and reported that nine years still remained for her to live.","yǒu cháng xū lì。 pěng cè fān yuè yī guò。 bái yǒu yáng suàn jiǔ nián。","有長鬚吏。捧冊翻閱一過。白有陽算九年。"
"1253","They were all in a quandary, but Kuan-Ti said, ""Never mind. Let Chang act in his place, and after nine years he shall be relieved.","gòng chóu chú jiān。 guān dì yuē。 bù fáng lìng zhāng shēng shè zhuàn。 jiǔ nián guā dài kě yě。","共躊躇間。關帝曰。不妨令張生攝篆。九年瓜代可也。"
"1254","And then he said to Sung, ""You ought to proceed at once to your post, but in consideration of your filial affection, a leave of nine years is granted to you, after the end of which term you will be again summoned.","nǎi wèi gōng yīng jí fù rèn。 jīn tuī rén xiào zhī xīn。 gěi jiǎ jiǔ nián。 jí qī dāng fù xiāng zhào。","乃謂公應即赴任。今推仁孝之心。給假九年。及期當復相召。"
"1255","He also addressed words of encouragement to Chang; and then they both made their obeisance and stepped down.","yòu miǎn lì xiù cái shù yǔ。 èr gōng jī shǒu bìng xià。","又勉勵秀才數語。二公稽首並下。"
"1256","The other student took Sung's hand and led him out into the country.","xiù cái wò shǒu。 sòng zhū jiāo yě。","秀才握手。送諸郊野。"
"1257","He told him that his name was Chang and he was from Ch'ang-shan.","zì yán cháng shān zhāng mǒu。","自言長山張某。"
"1258","At parting he presented him with a bit of verse.","yǐ shī zèng bié。","以詩贈別。"
"1259","I have forgotten what was in it, but there were these lines in those verses: ""If we have flowers and wine to drink, We will enjoy eternal spring. Without moon, without light The night itself to us is bright.","dōu wàng qí cí。 zhōng yǒu yǒu huā yǒu jiǔ chūn cháng zài。 wú yuè wú dēng yè zì míng zhī jù。","都忘其詞。中有有花有酒春長在。無月無燈夜自明之句。"
"1260","Sung then got upon his horse and bidding him farewell left.","gōng jì qí nǎi bié ér qù。","公既騎乃別而去。"
"1261","Having arrived at his village, he became conscious as if waking from a dream.","jí dǐ lǐ。 huò ruò mèng wù。","及抵里。豁若夢寤。"
"1262","He had been as dead for three days.","shí zú yǐ sān rì。","時卒已三日。"
"1263","His mother heard groans in the coffin and helped him out. It was some time before he was able to speak.","mǔ wén guān zhōng shēn yín。 fú chū bàn rì shǐ néng yǔ。","母聞棺中呻吟。扶出半日始能語。"
"1264","He asked about Ch'ang-shan, and found there really was a student named Chang who had died that very day.","wèn zhī cháng shān。 guǒ yǒu zhāng shēng。 yú shì rì sǐ yǐ。","問之長山。果有張生。於是日死矣。"
"1265","Nine years later his mother died.","hòu jiǔ nián。 mǔ guǒ zú。","後九年。母果卒。"
"1266","When the funeral was over, Sung performed his washings, went into his room and died.","yíng zàng jì bì。 huàn zhuó rù shì ér mò。","營葬既畢。浣濯入室而沒。"
"1267","His wife's parents lived inside the city near the western gate.","qí yuè jiā jū chéng zhōng xī mén nèi。","其岳家居城中西門內。"
"1268","They suddenly saw Sung followed by many carriages and horses with gay trappings and red tasseled bridles arrive at their place, enter the hall, make his obeisance and go.","hū jiàn gōng lòu yīng zhū zé yú mǎ shèn zhòng。 dēng qí táng。 yī bài ér xíng。","忽見公鏤膺朱幘輿馬甚眾。登其堂。一拜而行。"
"1269","They were all astonished and alarmed, not knowing that he had become a spirit.","xiāng gòng jīng yí。 bù zhī qí wéi shén。","相共驚疑。不知其為神。"
"1270","They hastened to the village and found that he had already died.","bēn xùn xiāng zhōng。 zé yǐ mò yǐ。","奔訊鄉中。則已沒矣。"
"1271","Sung had made his own records of his experiences, but unfortunately in the confusion and disturbances of those days they were lost.","gōng yǒu zì jì xiǎo zhuàn。 xī luàn hòu wú cún。","公有自記小傳。惜亂後無存。"
"1272","This is only a bare sketch of the story.","cǐ qí lüè ěr。","此其略耳。"
"1273","Guardianship is governed by the national law of the ward.","jiān hù yī bèi jiān hù rén zhī běn guó fǎ。","監護依被監護人之本國法。"
"1274","But the guardianship of a foreigner who is domiciled or resident in China and is subject to one of the following conditions is governed by Chinese law.","dàn zài zhōng guó yǒu zhù suǒ huò jū suǒ zhī wài guó rén。 yǒu zuǒ liè qíng zhī yī zhě。 qí jiān hù yī zhōng guó fǎ。","但在中國有住所或居所之外國人。有左列情之一者。其監護依中國法。"
"1275","According to his national law there are reasons necessitating the appointment of a guardian, but there is nobody to exercise the function of guardianship.","yī qí běn guó fǎ yǒu xū zhì jiān hù rén zhī yuán yīn。 ér wú rén xíng jiān hù shì wù zhě。","依其本國法有須置監護人之原因。而無人行監護事務者。"

"1280","The revocation of a will is governed by the national law of the testator existing at the time of revocation.","yí zhǔ zhī chè xiāo。 yī chè xiāo shí yí zhǔ rén zhī běn guó fǎ。","遺囑之撤銷。依撤銷時遺囑人之本國法。"

"1329","During a period of more than twenty years China had not been able to get rid of the malpractices of her judicial system, so that there was no way to start talking about the abolition of extraterritoriality.","èr shí yú nián。 zhōng guó shàng bù néng gǎi gé yí qiè sī fǎ xíng zhèng zhī bì duān。 suǒ yǐ fèi qì zhì wài fǎ quán yì shì。 yì wú cóng tán qǐ。","二十餘年。中國尚不能改革一切司法行政之弊端。所以廢棄治外法權一事。亦無從談起。"
"1330","The Powers, with the object of fulfilling what they had previously promised, in accordance with the provisions of the 5th resolution of the Washington Conference, are going to despatch to China a special commission which shall investigate the present status of the Chinese judicial system and formulate a report.","liè guó wèi shí jiàn qí chū yán qǐ jiàn。 jiāng zūn zhào huá huì dì wǔ yì jué àn zhī guī dìng。 pài qiǎn wěi yuán huì lái huá diào chá zhōng guó sī fǎ xíng zhèng zhī zhuàng kuàng。 kāi jù bào gào。","列國為實踐其初言起見。將遵照華會第五議決案之規定。派遣委員會來華調查中國司法行政之狀況。開具報告。"
"1331","By this the interested countries may be able to decide whether they should proceed with extraterritoriality or not, and what scheme should be followed in proceeding with it or abolishing it.","shǐ yǒu guān xì gè guó zhèng fǔ dé yǐ shěn hé lǐng shì cái pàn quán zhī yīng fǒu jìn xíng。 jí rú hé jìn xíng fàng qì zhī cè。","使有關係各國政府得以審核領事裁判權之應否進行。及如何進行放棄之策。"
"1332","It can be seen from this that the Powers all sympathize with the Chinese demands, and in case China should some day improve her judicial system, it would be a guarantee of the abolition of special rights. The Powers then would be certainly glad to comply with China's demands and would not raise any objection.","yóu cǐ kě jiàn gè yǒu guān xì guó。 jiē yǐ tóng qíng yú zhōng guó zhī yāo qiú yǐ。 shǐ zhōng guó zhī sī fǎ xíng zhèng。 yí dàn gǎi liáng。 kě wéi fàng qì gāi tè quán zhī bǎo zhàng。 bǐ zì lè yú cóng mìng。 jué wú fǎn kàng zhī yì yě。","由此可見各有關係國。皆已同情於中國之要求矣。使中國之司法行政。一旦改良。可為放棄該特權之保障。彼自樂於從命。絕無反抗之意也。"
"1333","Now when the Powers have already spoken in such a sincere and honest way, should China still insist on the immediate abolition of extraterritoriality, it would not be fair from the point of view of both reason and actual conditions.","liè qiáng duì yú zhōng guó jì fā cǐ chéng kěn tǎn shuài zhī yán。 zhōng guó ruò réng gù chí qí lì kè fèi qì zhì wài fǎ quán zhī zhǔ zhāng。 yǐ qíng lǐ xiàn shì liǎng fāng miàn guān zhī。 jūn yǒu suǒ wèi dāng yě。","列強對於中國既發此誠懇坦率之言。中國若仍固持其立刻廢棄治外法權之主張。以情理現勢兩方面觀之。均有所未當也。"
"1334","The Chinese must use all their efforts to reform their judicial system in order to be ready for the coming of the investigating commission, and thus they can complete all preliminary conditions for the abolition of extraterritoriality.","zhōng guó rén yí yòng qí quán lì yǐ gǎi gé qí sī fǎ xíng zhèng。 yǐ dài guó jì diào chá wěi yuán huì zhī fā xiàn。 shù hū kě yǐ chéng jiù yí qiè fèi qì zhì wài fǎ quán zhī xiān xíng tiáo jiàn。","中國人宜用其全力以改革其司法行政。以待國際調查委員會之發現。庶乎可以成就一切廢棄治外法權之先行條件。"
"1335","As for the position of international equality, it would come then of itself.","guó jì jiān dì wèi zhī píng děng。 dāng yǒu bù qī rán ér rán zhī shì yǐ。","國際間地位之平等。當有不期然而然之勢矣。"

"1356","The meaning of the expression, ""In order to govern the state rightly it is necessary first to regulate the family"" is this: it is not possible for one to teach others, when he cannot teach his own family.","suǒ wèi zhì guó。 bì xiān qí qí jiā zhě。 qí jiā bù kě jiào。 ér néng jiào rén zhě。 wú zhī","所謂治國。必先齊其家者。其家不可教。而能教人者。無之"

"1359","This land belongs to my father.","cǐ dì wéi wú fù suǒ yǒu","此地為吾父所有"
"1360","Influenced by his wife.","wèi fù rén suǒ huò","為婦人所惑"
"1361","This was fixed by Heaven's decree.","cǐ wéi tiān mìng suǒ dìng","此為天命所定"
"1362","There lived at Chao-ch'eng an old woman more than seventy years of age. She had an only son.","zhào chéng ōu。 nián qī shí yú。 zhǐ yī zǐ。","趙城嫗。年七十餘。止一子。"
"1363","One day he went up into the mountains and was devoured by a tiger.","yī rì rù shān。 wèi hǔ suǒ shì。","一日入山。為虎所噬。"
"1364","The sorrow of his mother was so great that she almost wished to die.","ōu bēi tòng。 jī bù yù huó。","嫗悲痛。幾不欲活。"
"1365","Weeping and wailing she went to the local district magistrate and brought an accusation against the tiger.","háo tí ér sù yú zǎi。","號啼而訴於宰。"
"1366","The magistrate laughed and said, ""How could a tiger be controlled by the law!","zǎi xiào yuē。 hǔ hé kě yǐ guān fǎ zhì zhī hū。","宰笑曰。虎何可以官法制之乎。"
"1367","But the old woman went on weeping, and it was impossible to stop her lamentations.","ōu yù háo tiào bù néng zhì zhǐ。","嫗愈號跳不能制止。"
"1368","The magistrate hooted at her, but she did not pay any attention.","zǎi chì zhī。 yì bù wèi jù。","宰叱之。亦不畏懼。"
"1369","Then the magistrate in compassion for her great age did not wish to intimidate her and promised her to have the tiger arrested.","yòu lián qí lǎo。 bù rěn jiā wēi nù。 suì nuò wèi zhuō hǔ。","又憐其老。不忍加威怒。遂諾為捉虎。"
"1370","However the old woman still lay prostrate on the ground and would not go until the warrant had been issued.","ōu fú bù qù。 bì dài jù dié chū。 nǎi kěn xíng。","嫗伏不去。必待句牒出。乃肯行。"
"1371","The magistrate, having no other choice, asked his attendants which of them would take up the job.","zǎi wú nài zhī。 jí wèn zhū yì。 shuí néng wǎng zhě。","宰無奈之。即問諸役。誰能往者。"
"1372","One of them, named Li-Neng, who happened to be drunk, stepped up to his seat and declared that he could do it.","yī lì míng lǐ néng。 xūn zuì。 yì zuò xià。 zì yán néng zhī。","一隸名李能。醺醉。詣坐下。自言能之。"
"1373","Whereupon the warrant was issued, and then the old woman went away.","chí dié xià。 ōu shǐ qù。","持牒下。嫗始去。"
"1374","When Li-Neng got sober he was sorry for what had happened.","lì xǐng ér huǐ zhī。","隸醒而悔之。"
"1375","But reflecting that it was a mere trick the magistrate had played on the old woman in order to get rid of her, he did not care much about it and handed back the warrant to the magistrate.","yóu wèi zǎi zhī wěi jú。 gū yǐ jiě yōu rǎo ěr。 yīn yì bù shèn wéi yì。 chí dié bào jiǎo。","猶謂宰之偽局。姑以解嫗擾耳。因亦不甚為意。持牒報繳。"
"1376","The latter cried angrily, ""You said you could do this, and now I will not allow you to return the warrant.","zǎi nù yuē。 gù yán néng zhī。 hé róng fù huǐ。","宰怒曰。固言能之。何容復悔。"
"1377","Li-Neng very much annoyed asked him for a warrant ordering to impress the local huntsmen.","lì jiǒng shèn。 qǐng dié jū liè hù。","隸窘甚。請牒拘獵戶。"
"1378","The magistrate granted it to him, and Li-Neng, having collected the huntsmen, lay in ambush day and night in the hills hoping to catch a tiger and thus to make an appearance of having performed his duty.","zǎi cóng zhī。 lì jí zhū liè rén rì yè fú shān gǔ。 jì dé yī hǔ。 shù kě sāi zé。","宰從之。隸集諸獵人日夜伏山谷。冀得一虎。庶可塞責。"
"1379","Real rights are governed by the law of the place where the things are situated; provided that real rights relating to ships are governed by the national law of the ship.","guān yú wù quán。 yī wù zhī suǒ zài dì fǎ。 dàn guān yú chuán bó zhī wù quán。 yī qí chuán jí guó zhī fǎ lǜ。","關於物權。依物之所在地法。但關於船舶之物權。依其船籍國之法律。"
"1380","The acquisition and extinction of real rights, excepting those relating to ships, are governed by the law of the place where the things are situate at the time of the completion of the causal facts.","wù quán zhī dé sàng。 chú guān yú chuán bó wài。 yī qí yuán yīn shì shí wán chéng shí。 wù zhī suǒ zài dì fǎ。","物權之得喪。除關於船舶外。依其原因事實完成時。物之所在地法。"
"1381","The form of a will relating to real rights may follow the provision of the first clause of section 1 of Article 26.","guān yú wù quán zhī yí zhǔ fāng shì。 dé yī dì èr shí liù tiáo dì yī xiàng qián duàn zhī guī dìng。","關於物權之遺囑方式。得依第二十六條第一項前段之規定。"
"1382","The proper law governing the essentials and effect of juridical acts giving rise to obligations is determined by the intention of the parties.","fǎ lǜ xíng wéi fā shēng zhài quán zhě。 qí chéng lì yào jiàn jí xiào lì。 yī dāng shì rén yì sī dìng qí yīng shì yòng zhī fǎ lǜ。","法律行為發生債權者。其成立要件及效力。依當事人意思定其應適用之法律。"
"1383","When the intention of the parties is uncertain, their national law governs if they are of the same nationality; but the law of the place of transaction governs if they are of different nationalities.","dāng shì rén yì sī bù míng shí。 tóng guó jí zhě。 yī qí běn guó fǎ。 guó jí bù tóng zhě。 yī xíng wéi dì fǎ。","當事人意思不明時。同國籍者。依其本國法。國籍不同者。依行為地法。"
"1384","When there are different places of transaction, the place where communication is made is the place of transaction.","xíng wéi dì bù tóng zhě。 yǐ fā tōng zhī zhī dì。 wéi xíng wéi dì。","行為地不同者。以發通知之地。為行為地。"
"1385","When the offer and acceptance of a contract are made in different places, as regards the formation and the effect of the contract, the place where the offer is communicated is the place of transaction; but if the offeree does not know, at the time of acceptance, the place from which the offer was despatched, the domicile of the offeror is regarded as the place of transaction.","qì yuē yào yuē dì yǔ chéng nuò dì bù tóng zhě。 qí qì yuē zhī chéng lì jí xiào lì。 yǐ fā yào yuē tōng zhī dì。 wéi xíng wéi dì。 ruò shòu yào yuē rén yú chéng nuò shí。 bù zhī qí fā xìn dì zhě。 yǐ yào yuē rén zhī zhù suǒ dì。 shì wéi xíng wéi dì。","契約要約地與承諾地不同者。其契約之成立及效力。以發要約通知地。為行為地。若受要約人於承諾時。不知其發信地者。以要約人之住所地。視為行為地。"
"1386","Is there any difference between killing a man with a stick and with a sword?","shā rén yǐ tǐng yǔ rèn。 yǒu yǐ yì hū","殺人以梃與刃。有以異乎"
"1387","A prince should employ his ministers according to the rules of propriety; ministers should serve their prince with faithfulness.","jūn shǐ chén yǐ lǐ。 chén shì jūn yǐ zhōng","君使臣以禮。臣事君以忠"
"1388","The Master said, ""The superior man considers righteousness to be essential. He performs it according to the rules of propriety. He brings it forth in humility. "1441","More than one month passed away during which time Li-Neng received several hundred blows with the bamboo. Overwhelmed with wrongs and having nobody to whom to make complaint, he went to the temple of Tung-Yüeh in the eastern suburb, and there he kneeled down, prayed, and wept bitterly.","yuè yú。 shòu zhàng shù bǎi。 yuān kǔ wǎng kòng。 suì yì dōng guō yuè miào。 guì ér zhù zhī。 kū shī shēng。","月餘。受杖數百。冤苦罔控。遂詣東郭嶽廟。跪而祝之。哭失聲。"
"1442","Suddenly a tiger appeared from outside.","wú hé。 yī hǔ zì wài lái。","無何。一虎自外來。"
"1443","Li-Neng was terrified thinking that the tiger was going to devour him.","lì cuò è。 kǒng bèi xī shì。","隸錯愕。恐被咥噬。"
"1444","The tiger entered and paying no attention to anything squatted down in the doorway.","hǔ rù。 shū bù tā gù。 dūn lì mén zhōng。","虎入。殊不他顧。蹲立門中。"
"1445","Then Li-Neng addressed the tiger with a prayer. ""If it is you,"" he said, ""who killed that old woman's son, suffer me to tie you up.","lì zhù yuē。 rú shā mǒu zi ěr yě。 qí fǔ tīng wú fù。","隸祝曰。如殺某子爾也。其俯聽吾縛。"
"1446","Whereupon, drawing out a cord, he threw it over the tiger's neck. The latter drooped its ears and allowed itself to be bound.","suì chū léi suǒ zhí hǔ jǐng。 hǔ tiē ěr shòu fù。","遂出縲索縶虎頸。虎帖耳受縛。"
"1447","When the tiger was led into the office of the district magistrate, the latter asked it, ""Was that young man devoured by you?","qiān dá xiàn shǔ。 zǎi wèn hǔ yuē。 mǒu zi ěr shì zhī yé。","牽達縣署。宰問虎曰。某子爾噬之耶。"
"1448","The tiger nodded its head in assent.","hǔ hàn zhī。","虎頷之。"
"1449","Murderers should suffer death,"" continued the magistrate, ""such is the law from the ancient times. Moreover the old woman had only one son whom you killed; and now, in her declining years, what support can she get? If you can be as a son to her I shall pardon you.","zǎi yuē。 shā rén zhě sǐ。 gǔ zhī dìng lǜ。 qiě yù zhǐ yī zi。 ér ěr shā zhī。 bǐ cán nián chuí jǐn。 hé yǐ shēng huó。 tǎng ěr néng wéi ruò zi yě。 wǒ jiāng shè zhī。","宰曰。殺人者死。古之定律。且嫗止一子。而爾殺之。彼殘年垂盡。何以生活。倘爾能為若子也。我將赦之。"
"1450","The tiger again nodded its head.","hǔ yòu hàn zhī。","虎又頷之。"
"1451","Whereupon the tiger was set free and ordered to go.","nǎi shì fú lìng qù。","乃釋縛令去。"
"1452","The old woman was very much dissatisfied with the magistrate because he did not order someone to kill the tiger to revenge the death of her son.","yù fāng yuàn zǎi bù shā hǔ yǐ cháng zǐ yě。","嫗方怨宰不殺虎以償子也。"
"1453","Next morning, when the old woman opened the door, there was a dead deer lying before it.","chí dàn。 qǐ fēi。 zé yǒu sǐ lù。","遲旦。啟扉。則有死鹿。"
"1454","She sold its flesh and skin, and thus was able to make a living.","yù huò qí ròu gé。 yòng yǐ zī dù。","嫗貨其肉革。用以資度。"
"1455","From that day it became a common thing.","zì shì yǐ wéi cháng。","自是以為常。"
"1456","Sometimes the tiger would bring valuables and throw them in the court-yard, so that the old woman became very well-off and better cared for than by her own son.","shí xián jīn bó zhì tíng zhōng。 yù yóu cǐ zhì fēng yù。 fèng yǎng guò yú qí zǐ。","時銜金帛擲庭中。嫗由此致豐裕。奉養過於其子。"
"1457","She felt very grateful to the tiger who, when he came, slept a whole day under the eaves.","xīn qiè dé hǔ。 hǔ lái shí wò yán xià。 jìng rì bù qù。","心竊德虎。虎來時臥簷下。竟日不去。"
"1458","Men and animals, seeing it, remained quiet and were not afraid of it.","rén chù xiāng ān。 gè wú cāi jì。","人畜相安。各無猜忌。"
"1459","In a few years the old woman died, and the tiger came and roared in the hall.","shù nián yù sǐ。 hǔ lái hǒu yú táng zhōng。","數年嫗死。虎來吼於堂中。"
"1460","With the money the old woman had saved it was possible to arrange a pompous funeral for her, and she was buried by her relatives.","yù sù suǒ jī。 chuò kě yíng zàng。 zú rén gòng yì zhī。","嫗素所積。綽可營葬。族人共瘞之。"
"1461","The grave-mound had just been completed when a tiger rushed in, and every one who was at the funeral fled away in fear.","fén lěi fāng chéng。 hǔ zhòu bēn lái。 bīn kè jìn táo。","墳壘方成。虎驟奔來。賓客盡逃。"
"1462","The tiger approached the mound, roared like thunder and disappeared.","hǔ zhí fù zhǒng qián。 háo míng léi dòng。 yí shí shǐ qù。","虎直赴冢前。嗥鳴雷動。移時始去。"
"1463","The people of that place built in the eastern suburb a temple in honor of the Faithful Tiger which remains there till this day.","tǔ rén lì yì hǔ cí yú dōng jiāo。 zhì jīn yóu cún。","土人立義虎祠於東郊。至今猶存。"
"1464","Obligations arising out of management of affairs without mandate and unjustified benefits are governed by the law of the place where the acts occurred.","guān yú yīn shì wù guǎn lǐ bù dāng lì dé fā shēng zhī zhài quán。 yī shì shí fā shēng dì fǎ。","關於因事務管理不當利得發生之債權。依事實發生地法。"
"1465","Obligations arising out of delicts are governed by the law of the place where the acts were committed, but this does not apply to acts not considered wrong by Chinese law.","guān yú yīn bù fǎ xíng wéi fā shēng zhī zhài quán。 yī xíng wéi dì fǎ。 dàn yī Zhōng guó fǎ bù rèn wéi bù fǎ zhě。 bù shì yòng zhī。","關於因不法行為發生之債權。依行為地法。但依中國法不認為不法者。不適用之。"
"1466","Applications for compensation of damages and for other dispositions on account of delicts mentioned in the above provision are limited only to those which are allowed by Chinese law.","qián xiàng bù fǎ xíng wéi zhī sǔn hài péi cháng jí qí tā chǔ fèn zhī qǐng qiú。 yǐ Zhōng guó fǎ rèn xǔ zhě wéi xiàn。","前項不法行為之損害賠償及其他處分之請求。以中國法認許者為限。"
"1467","Laws relating to forms of juridical acts.","guān yú fǎ lǜ xíng wéi fāng shì zhī fǎ lǜ。","關於法律行為方式之法律。"
"1468","The forms of juridical acts, unless otherwise provided, are governed by the law of the place where the acts were performed; provided that the forms prescribed by the law governing the effect of such acts are also effective.","fǎ lǜ xíng wéi zhī fāng shì。 chú yǒu tè bié guī dìng wài。 yī xíng wéi dì fǎ。 dàn zūn yòng guī dìng xíng wéi xiào lì zhī fǎ lǜ。 suǒ dìng zhī fāng shì。 yì wéi yǒu xiào。","法律行為之方式。除有特別規定外。依行為地法。但遵用規定行為效力之法律。所定之方式。亦為有效。"
"1469","The proviso of the above section is not applicable to the forms of acts which have for their object the exercise or preservation of rights arising out of negotiable instruments.","yǐ xíng shǐ huò bǎo quán piào jù shàng quán lì wéi mù dì zhī xíng wéi。 qí fāng shì bù shì yòng qián xiàng dàn shū guī dìng。","以行使或保全票據上權利為目的之行為。其方式不適用前項但書規定。"
"1470","These Rules shall come into operation from the day of promulgation.","běn tiáo lì zì gōng bù rì shī xíng。","本條例自公布日施行。"
"1471","The philosopher Kao said, ""Man's nature is like the willow tree, and righteousness is like a cup or a bowl. The molding of benevolence and righteousness out of man's nature is like the making of cups and bowls from the willow tree.","gào zǐ yuē。 xìng yóu qǐ liǔ yě。 yì yóu bēi juān yě。 yǐ rén xìng wéi rén yì。 yóu yǐ qǐ liǔ wéi bēi juān","告子曰。性猶杞柳也。義猶杯棬也。以人性為仁義。猶以杞柳為杯棬"
"1472","If the axes enter the mountain forests at the proper time, there will be more wood than can be used.","fǔ jīn yǐ shí rù shān lín。 cái mù bù kě shèng yòng yě","斧斤以時入山林。材木不可勝用也"
"1473","Those who are called great ministers serve their prince according to righteousness, and when they cannot, they retire.","suǒ wèi dà chén zhě。 yǐ dào shì jūn。 bù kě zé zhǐ","所謂大臣者。以道事君。不可則止"
"1474","With regard to our stations you are sovereign, and I am subject. How can I presume to be on terms of friendship with my sovereign? With regard to virtue you are my disciple. "1489","Gifted with ability, and yet putting questions to those who were not gifted; possessing much, and yet putting questions to those possessing little.","yǐ néng wèn yú bù néng。 yǐ duō wèn yú guǎ","以能問於不能。以多問於寡"
"1490","Jan-tzu returned from the Court. The Master said to him, ""Why are you so late?"" He replied, ""We had government business."" The Master said, ""It must have been family affairs. "1517","On the 4th of September the eight Powers, signatories of the Washington Treaty, viz. England, Belgium, France, Italy, Japan, Holland, Portugal and the United States of America sent a joint note in reply to the communication of the Chinese Government of the 24th of June showing a common attitude toward China's request for the revision of unequal treaties from which it is evident that the Powers are acting in common toward China.","jiǔ yuè sì rì。 qiān dìng huá shèng dùn tiáo yuē zhī bā qiáng guó zhě。 jí yīng bǐ fǎ yì rì hé pú měi děng guó shì yě。 yí zhì bèi wén dá fù zhōng guó liù yuè èr shí sì rì zhī zhào huì。 duì yú zhōng guó suǒ qǐng qiú zhī xiū gǎi bù píng děng tiáo yuē yī céng。 biǎo shì gòng tóng zhī tài dù。 suǒ yǐ shì liè qiáng duì yú zhōng guó cǎi yòng tóng yī zhī bù zhòu yě。","九月四日。簽訂華盛頓條約之八強國者。即英比法意日和葡美等國是也。一致備文答覆中國六月二十四日之照會。對於中國所請求之修改不平等條約一層。表示共同之態度。所以示列強對於中國採用同一之步驟也。"

"1569","From the point of view of the Powers, all interested countries consider China's fulfillment of the obligations fixed by those treaties as preliminary conditions for the revision of the unequal treaties.","zì liè qiáng fāng miàn guān zhī。 fán yǔ Zhōng guó yǒu guān xì zhī qiáng guó。 jūn yǐ Zhōng guó xū xiān shí dì lǚ xíng qí tiáo yuē shàng suǒ guī dìng zhī zé rèn。 wéi xiū gǎi bù píng děng tiáo yuē zhī xiān xíng tiáo jiàn。","自列強方面觀之。凡與中國有關係之強國。均以中國須先實地履行其條約上所規定之責任。為修改不平等條約之先行條件。"
"1570","Moreover both the procedure in making this revision and the obligations to be taken up mutually should be in accordance with the provisions of the Washington Conference.","qiě yī qiè xiū gǎi zhī chéng xù。 jí bǐ cǐ suǒ yīng fù zhī zé rèn。 yì yí yǐ Huá Shèng dùn huì yì suǒ guī dìng zhě wéi biāo zhǔn。","且一切修改之程序。及彼此所應負之責任。亦宜以華盛頓會議所規定者為標準。"
"1571","In other words, the Powers wish to put responsibility for not abolishing the unequal treaties upon the Chinese, saying that in international relations China really has the qualifications for enjoying equal treatment with the other nations; but the fact that she is unable to enjoy it rests with China herself.","huàn ér yán zhī。 shí liè qiáng yù yǐ bù néng qǔ xiāo bù píng děng tiáo yuē zhī zé jiā zhū Zhōng guó rén zhī shēn。 wèi Zhōng guó zài guó jì shàng shí yǒu yǔ gè guó xiǎng shòu píng děng dài yù zhī zī gé。 dàn suǒ yǐ bù néng zhě。 gài yóu Zhōng guó zì qǔ zhī yě。","換而言之。實列強欲以不能取消不平等條約之責加諸中國人之身。謂中國在國際上實有與各國享受平等待遇之資格。但所以不能者。蓋由中國自取之也。"
"1572","In connection with the abolition of extraterritoriality there are very reasonable arguments in the reply of the Powers.","guān yú qǔ xiāo zhì wài fǎ quán yī xiàng。 gè guó zhī fù wén。 pō bù wú xiāng dāng zhī lǐ yóu。","關於取消治外法權一項。各國之覆文。頗不無相當之理由。"
"1573","They say that in 1902 and 1903, when the Treaty of Commerce was concluded, they had already expressed their intention to give up extraterritoriality, and if the conditions of the Chinese laws and the procedure of the execution of the judicial decisions had been such as to satisfy them, they would have long ago abandoned consular jurisdiction.","bǐ yún dāng yī jiǔ líng èr yī jiǔ líng sān nián dì jié tōng shāng tiáo yuē zhī shí。 bǐ yǐ biǎo shì yuàn fàng qì zhì wài fǎ quán zhī yì。 tǎng Zhōng guó zhī fǎ lǜ zhuàng kuàng。 yǔ zhí xíng bàn fǎ。 jiē kě yǐ shǐ bǐ děng mǎn yì。 zé lǐng shì cái pàn quán。 gù zǎo yǐ fèi qì yǐ。","彼云當一九零二一九零三年締結通商條約之時。彼已表示願放棄治外法權之意。倘中國之法律狀況。與執行辦法。皆可以使彼等滿意。則領事裁判權。固早已廢棄矣。"

"1616","A well known Chinese authority on international law has recently made the following analytical comment in reference to this question:","jìn yǒu zhù míng guó jì fǎ xué zhuān jiā mǒu shì。 duì yú cǐ shì。 céng wéi fēn pōu shì zhī pī píng。 qí tán huà rú xià。","近有著名國際法學專家某氏。對於此事。曾為分剖式之批評。其談話如下。"
"1617","Since China's participation in the Washington Conference in 1922 the Chinese people have come to cherish friendly sentiments toward the United States, mainly for the reason that the Nine-Power Treaty, concluded at the Washington Conference concerning affairs in China, was designed to afford her opportunity to get rid of the shackles of the old treaties in order that she might get freedom for natural development.","zì yī jiǔ èr èr nián wǒ guó cān yǔ huá fǔ huì yì hòu。 guó rén duì yú měi guó。 mò bù biǎo shì hǎo gǎn。 gài yǐ huá huì suǒ dìng jiǔ guó jiān guān yú zhōng guó shì jiàn zhī tiáo yuē。 yì zài yǔ zhōng guó qū chú jiù yuē shù fù zhī jī huì。 bǐ zhōng guó dé yǐ zì yóu fā zhǎn。","自一九二二年我國參與華府會議後。國人對於美國。莫不表示好感。蓋以華會所訂九國間關於中國事件之條約。意在予中國祛除舊約束縛之機會。俾中國得以自由發展。"
"1618","Although this was not sufficient to satisfy our people's expectations completely, it might still lead to the correction of the mistakes made in the old treaties and serve as the preliminary step to the gradual loosening of the fetters.","qí zhōng suī wèi néng jìn mǎn wú rén zhī xī wàng。 ér jiù yuē zhī shī。 jí cǐ xiū zhèng。 wéi jiàn cì jiě chú shù fù zhī chū bù。","其中雖未能盡滿吾人之希望。而舊約之失。藉此修正。為漸次解除束縛之初步。"
"1619","For this stand for justice we greatly respect the American people.","měi rén rú cǐ zhǔ chí zhèng yì。 zhì kě qīn pèi。","美人如此主持正義。至可欽佩。"
"1620","But we are quite surprised at the telegraphic report from Berlin to the effect that the United States has unexpectedly invited Germany to adhere to the Nine-Power Treaty of Washington.","bù wèi bǎi lín diàn xìn chuán lái。 měi guó hū yǒu yāo qǐng dé guó jiā rù huá shèng dùn jiǔ guó tiáo yuē。 shí chū wú rén yì liào zhī wài。","不謂柏林電信傳來。美國忽有邀請德國加入華盛頓九國條約。實出吾人意料之外。"

"1636","With those which have already concluded agreements of reciprocal equality with her it has absolutely nothing to do, and, naturally, those Powers do not belong to that category.","qí yǐ dìng yǒu píng děng tiáo yuē zhě。 běn wú guān xì。 dāng rán bù zài cǐ liè。","其已訂有平等條約者。本無關係。當然不在此列。"
"1637","The invitation by the American Government to Germany to adhere to the Nine-Power Treaty runs, therefore, counter not only to the Sino-German Agreement, but also obviously to the provisions of Article VIII of the said Treaty.","gù měi guó cǐ cì yāo qǐng dé guó jiā rù jiǔ guó tiáo yuē。 bù tè yǔ zhōng dé xié dìng dà yǒu wéi bèi。 qiě yǔ gāi yuē dì bā tiáo zhī guī dìng。 xiǎn yǒu wèi hé。","故美國此次邀請德國加入九國條約。不特與中德協定大有違背。且與該約第八條之規定。顯有未合。"
"1638","It is known that the Chinese Government has already made vigorous protests to the Governments at Berlin and Washington.","wén wǒ zhèng fǔ yǐ xiàng bó lín huá shèng dùn liǎng chù yán zhòng jiāo shè。","聞我政府已向柏林華盛頓兩處嚴重交涉。"
"1639","Whatever may happen, the Chinese people will be opposed to any retrogressive step by those Powers which have already concluded agreements of reciprocal equality with China.","wú lùn rú hé。 zhōng guó rén mín duì yú píng děng xiāng hù tiáo yuē zhī guó jiā。 yù wéi tuì yī bù zhī xíng dòng。 jué nán chéng rèn yě。","無論如何。中國人民對於平等相互條約之國家。欲為退一步之行動。決難承認也。"

"1645","This is particularly the case within recent years as a result of the peaceful nature and friendly feelings of the Chinese people.","jìn nián lái jìn bù yóu sù。 gài huá rén sù hào hé píng。 fù yú qíng gǎn。","近年來進步尤速。蓋華人素好和平。富於情感。"
"1646","Now the invitation by the United States to Germany to adhere to the Nine-Power Treaty is nothing else but a confession that the Washington Conference was but an association of the Powers for division of special rights and interests in China, and that the American policy of helping China was a mere pretense.","jīn měi guó yāo qǐng dé guó jiā rù jiǔ guó tiáo yuē。 wú yì zì rèn huá fǔ huì yì wéi gè guó zài huá quán lì zhī jié hé。 měi guó zhù huá zhèng cè wéi yī zhǒng jiǎ miàn jù。","今美國邀請德國加入九國條約。無異自認華府會議為各國在華權利支配之結合。美國助華政策為一種假面具。"
"1647","The Nine-Power Treaty has only fixed general principles for the liberation of China, while in the Sino-German Agreement China has actually carried out these principles and recovered her complete freedom.","gài jiǔ guó tiáo yuē jǐn guī dìng jiě chú zhōng guó shù fù zhī pǔ tōng yuán zé。 ér zhōng guó zài zhōng dé xié yuē shàng。 yǐ shí xíng cǐ xiàng yuán zé。 qǔ dé wán quán zhī zì yóu。","蓋九國條約僅規定解除中國束縛之普通原則。而中國在中德協約上。已實行此項原則。取得完全之自由。"
"1648","The American invitation to Germany to adhere to the Nine-Power Treaty is therefore nothing less than an indication of America's opposition to China's recovery of her freedom, and an encouragement to Germany to place China in further bondage.","jīn měi yāo dé jiā rù jiǔ guó tiáo yuē。 bù chì biǎo shì fǎn duì zhōng guó zhī wán quán zì yóu。 ér zàn chéng dé guó jiā shù fù yú zhōng guó。","今美邀德加入九國條約。不啻表示反對中國之完全自由。而贊成德國加束縛於中國。"
"1649","This is really difficult to understand.","cǐ bù kě jiě yě。","此不可解也。"
"1650","Such a step on the part of the American Government is tantamount to a complete reversal of the American policy for helping China which was in effect for over ten years, and it places the United States among those Powers which embarrass China's progress and oppose the recovery of her freedom.","měi guó cǐ jǔ wú yì biàn gēng qí shí yú nián lái zhù huá zhī zhèng cè。 ér wéi qiān zhì zhōng guó jìn bù。 fǎn duì zhōng guó huī fù zì yóu zhī guó jiā。","美國此舉無異變更其十餘年來助華之政策。而為牽制中國進步。反對中國恢復自由之國家。"
"1651","It is surely not the thing for a Power known to be exponent of right and justice to do.","zhǔ zhāng rén dào zhèng yì zhě。 suǒ bù wéi yě。","主張人道正義者。所不為也。"

"1722","At present the discussions about the governmental system in China are endless, and there is no agreement as to any course to follow.","mù xià duì yú zhōng guó zhèng tǐ。 yì lùn fēn yún。 mò zhōng yī shì。","目下對於中國政體。議論紛紜。莫衷一是。"
"1723","Still the parliamentary government is the heart of all these discussions.","wéi jūn yǐ guó huì zhèng zhì wéi zhōng xīn。","惟均以國會政治為中心。"
"1724","The parliamentary system is but a product of European governmental systems, and China merely borrowed it to help in the carrying out of her own government.","rán guó huì zhèng zhì。 wéi ōu zhōu zhèng zhì zhī chǎn wù。 zhōng guó bù guò jiǎ zhī yǐ zī zhōng guó zhèng zhì zhī yùn yòng ér yǐ。","然國會政治。為歐洲政治之產物。中國不過假之以資中國政治之運用而已。"
"1725","But can a parliamentary form of government which has grown up in Europe be suitable to the needs of China? That is a big question indeed.","rán ér fā dá yú ōu zhōu zhī guó huì zhèng zhì。 jiū jìng néng fǒu shì yòng yú Zhōng guó。 shàng shǔ yī dà yí wèn。","然而發達於歐洲之國會政治。究竟能否適用於中國。尚屬一大疑問。"
"1726","Inasmuch as the parliamentary system is as yet in the beginning of an experiment, it is not easy to decide whether it will be suitable or not.","cǐ suī shàng zài chū qī zhī shì yàn zhōng。 bù yì duàn qí shì fǒu。","此雖尚在初期之試驗中。不易斷其適否。"
"1727","But what is the condition of this system now in Europe, the land of its origin?","rán guó huì zhèng zhì。 zài běn chǎn dì zhī ōu zhōu。 mù xià guǒ chéng hé zhǒng zhuàng tài hū。","然國會政治。在本產地之歐洲。目下果呈何種狀態乎。"
"1728","This is a very interesting question.","cǐ shí zuì yǒu xìng qù zhī wèn tí。","此實最有興趣之問題。"
"1729","With respect to the parliamentary system let us first study it in its birthplace, England. What is the situation there?","zī guān yú guó huì zhèng zhì。 gū xiān jiū qí zài gāi chū chǎn dì zhī yīng guó。 guǒ zuò hé zhǒng zhuàng tài。","茲關於國會政治。姑先究其在該出產地之英國。果作何種狀態。"
"1730","Since the European war, the enemies of European governmental systems have been radicalism and socialism which have both sprung up from the common people, together with a form of class struggle adopted by both these systems.","gài zì ōu zhàn yǐ hòu。 ōu zhōu zhèng zhì zhī dí。 cún yú yóu mín zhòng ér lái zhī guò jī zhǔ yì。 shè huì zhǔ yì yǔ qí qǔ jiē jí zhàn zhēng zhī xíng shì。","蓋自歐戰以後。歐洲政治之敵。存於由民眾而來之過激主義。社會主義與其取階級戰爭之形式。"
"1731","I have not seen you for a long time.","jiǔ wéi yǎ fàn","久違雅範"
"1732","you have got a son.","gé xià dé zhàn nòng zhāng zhī xǐ","閣下得占弄璋之喜"
"1733","Most of the continental countries of Europe have taken to socialism.","ōu zhōu dà lù zhū guó。 duō yǐ wéi shè huì zhǔ yì suǒ gū lǐng。","歐洲大陸諸國。多已為社會主義所估領。"
"1734","Only England has been able to preserve completely her pre-war representative government.","wéi yīng guó shàng néng wán quán wéi chí qí zhàn qián zhī dài yì zhèng zhì。","唯英國尚能完全維持其戰前之代議政治。"
"1735","But the present great strike is a challenge to revolution under pretense of question of wages.","rán cǐ cì zhī dà bà gōng。 quán wéi shè huì zhǔ yì zhě jiǎ gōng qián wèn tí ér wéi gé mìng de tiǎo zhàn。","然此次之大罷工。全為社會主義者假工錢問題而為革命的挑戰。"
"1736","The parliamentary government of England was a stronghold of the European representative form of government. Now it is in danger of being overthrown by socialism.","jí yīng guó zhī dài yì zhèng zhì。 běn wéi zhī chí ōu zhōu dài yì zhèng tǐ zhī jīn chéng tāng chí。 jīn yì jiāng wéi shè huì zhǔ yì suǒ xiàn luò。","即英國之代議政治。本為支持歐洲代議政體之金城湯池。今亦將為社會主議所陷落。"
"1737","In that case the European representative government would be seriously affected.","ōu zhōu zhī dài yì zhèng zhì。 bù néng bù dà shòu dǎ jī。","歐洲之代議政治。不能不大受打擊。"
"1738","Some people say that radicalism in England is but a false alarm.","huò yún guò jī zhǔ yì。 zài yīng guó bù guò wéi yī zhǒng qǐ yōu ér yǐ。","或云過激主義。在英國不過為一種杞憂而已。"
"1739","Why so? It is because the English labor party will never allow the communists within their ranks to resort to violence.","hé zé。 gài yīng guó láo dòng dǎng。 jué bù zhì shǐ gòng chǎn zhǔ yì zhě。 zài dǎng nèi bá hù gù yě。","何則。蓋英國勞動黨。決不至使共產主義者。在黨內跋扈故也。"
"1740","Nevertheless it is still a question whether the conservative party will be able to restrain the reactionary elements of the party so that they do not run to fascism.","suī rán。 bǎo shǒu dǎng duì yú pán jū dǎng nèi zhī wán mí pài。 néng fǒu yì zhì zhī shǐ bù zhì qū yú fǎ xī sī qǐ zhǔ yì。 bù néng bù wéi yī yí wèn yě。","雖然。保守黨對於蟠居黨內之頑迷派。能否抑制之使不至趨於法西斯起主義。不能不為一疑問也。"
"1741","Therefore, although England may have no anxiety concerning communistic rule of the country, still there is for her danger of a dictatorial government like that of Italy.","gù yīng guó suī wú wéi gòng chǎn zhǔ yì zhèng zhì zhī yōu。 rán kǒng yǒu wéi yì guó de dú cái zhèng zhì zhī yú。","故英國雖無為共產主義政治之憂。然恐有為義國的獨裁政治之虞。"
"1742","It would also affect the parliamentary system.","qí yǐng xiǎng yú guó huì zhèng zhì zé yī yě。","其影響於國會政治則一也。"

"1746","Let us look next at Italy. Why has dictator Mussolini appeared there?","cì guān chá yì guó。 dú cái shǒu xiàng mò suǒ lì ní hé gù chū xiàn hū。","次觀察義國。獨裁首相莫索利尼何故出現乎。"
"1747","It was for no other reason than that the Italian people, seeing their parliamentary government lifeless and broken down, came to lose all hope in it.","shì wú tā。 gài yì guó guó mín。 jiàn qí guó huì zhèng zhì zhī má bì wěi dùn。 suì zhì shī wàng。","是無他。蓋義國國民。見其國會政治之痲痺萎頓。遂至失望。"
"1748","The king, the political parties and officials were all perplexed and completely at a loss, and the country was for a moment on the point of falling into a state of anarchy.","guó wáng zhèng dǎng guān liáo。 jūn máng rán zì shī。 yī shí jiāng xiàn yú wú zhèng fǔ zhuàng tài。","國王政黨官僚。均茫然自失。一時將陷於無政府狀態。"
"1749","Just at this time, since the people did not desire bolshevism, they accordingly welcomed the dictatorship of Mussolini.","wéi cǐ jì yīn guó mín bù xǐ chì huà。 suì zhì huān yíng mò suǒ lì ní zhī dú cái zhèng zhì。","惟此際因國民不喜赤化。遂至歡迎莫索利尼之獨裁政治。"
"1750","Now Germany and France certainly do not prefer dictatorship, and only on account of the failure of the parliamentary government would they accept dictatorship in order to avoid anarchy.","jí dé guó yǔ fǎ guó。 yì jué fēi zé dú cái zhèng zhì zhě。 bù guò yīn huò yú guó huì zhèng zhì zhī shī bài。 yù jí dú cái zhèng zhì yǐ miǎn wú zhèng fǔ zhuàng tài yǐ ěr。","即德國與法國。亦決非擇獨裁政治者。不過因惑於國會政治之失敗。欲藉獨裁政治以免無政府狀態已耳。"
"1751","Again let us look at France. Within two years her cabinet has been changed four times.","zài guān chá fǎ guó。 qí nèi gé zài èr nián jiān。 céng sì dù gēng yì。","再觀察法國。其內閣在二年間。曾四度更迭。"
"1752","As the foundation of the government is being constantly shaken, and small political parties are very numerous, the government policy is unable to be stabilized.","jī chǔ cháng dòng yáo。 xiǎo dǎng lín lì。 zhèng cè bù dé ān dìng。","基礎常動搖。小黨林立。政策不得安定。"
"1753","The financial policy is particularly the cause of this constant instability of the cabinet.","qí cái zhèng zhèng cè。 yóu wéi nèi gé dòng yáo zhī dà yuán yīn。","其財政政策。尤為內閣動搖之大原因。"
"1754","From the point of view of economics France is in a flourishing condition, not like England and Germany where the problem of unemployment has arisen.","gài fǎ guó yóu jīng jì shàng yán zhī。 pō wéi fán róng。 bù sì yīng dé liǎng guó fā shēng shī yè wèn tí。","蓋法國由經濟上言之。頗為繁榮。不似英德兩國發生失業問題。"
"1755","It is only because the government is powerless, that the financial difficulties are not easily settled.","wéi zhèng zhì jī guān má bì。 bù yì jiě jué cái zhèng shàng zhī kùn nán。","惟政治機關痲痺。不易解決財政上之困難。"
"1756","For this reason the French people are daily losing faith in their government machinery.","yīn cǐ guó mín rì yì bù xìn rèn qí zhèng zhì jī guān。","因此國民日益不信任其政治機關。"
"1757","Of all European countries the French know most thoroughly the harm of dictatorship.","zài ōu zhōu zhū guó zhōng。 yǐ fǎ rén mín wéi zuì shú zhī dú cái zhèng zhì zhī hài。","在歐洲諸國中。以法人民為最熟知獨裁政治之害。"
"1758","However they are obliged to admit that a dictatorship is better than a weak and powerless government.","rán yì bù dé bù tòng gǎn dú cái zhèng zhì。 yōu yú ruǎn ruò wú néng zhī zhèng zhì。","然亦不得不痛感獨裁政治。優於軟弱無能之政治。"
"1759","One hears that among the French there are also many admirers of Mussolini, and that is not without reason.","wén fǎ rén zhōng yì yǒu jǐng mù mò suǒ lì ní zhě。 cǐ fēi wú gù yě。","聞法人中亦有景慕莫索利尼者。此非無故也。"

"1771","From the account given above one may say that the representative form of government of the European Powers is now in a very dangerous position.","yī yǐ shàng suǒ lüè shù。 kě wèi ōu zhōu liè qiáng zhī dài yì zhèng zhì。 jīn yǐ pín yú wēi jī。","依以上所略述。可謂歐洲列強之代議政治。今已頻於危機。"

"1779","The Master said, 'If the scholar be not grave, he will not call forth any veneration, and his learning will not be solid.","zǐ yuē， jūn zǐ bù zhòng， zé bù wēi， xué zé bù gù。","子曰，君子不重，則不威，學則不固。"

"1782","The Master said, 'With one like Ts'ze, I can begin to talk about the odes. I told him one point, and he knew its proper sequence.","zǐ yuē， cì yě， shǐ kě yǔ yán shī yǐ yǐ， gào zhū wǎng ér zhī lái zhě。","子曰，賜也，始可與言詩已矣，告諸往而知來者。"
"1783","The Master said, 'I will not be afflicted at men's not knowing me; I will be afflicted that I do not know men.","zǐ yuē， bù huàn rén zhī bù jǐ zhī， huàn bù zhī rén yě。 wéi zhèng dì èr","子曰，不患人之不己知，患不知人也。為政第二"
"1784","The Master said, 'In the Book of Poetry are three hundred pieces, but the design of them all may be embraced in one sentence - ""Having no depraved thoughts.","zǐ yuē， shī sān bǎi， yī yán yǐ bì zhī， yuē， sī wú xié。","子曰，詩三百，一言以蔽之，曰，思無邪。"
"1785","If they be led by virtue, and uniformity sought to be given them by the rules of propriety, they will have the sense of shame, and moreover will become good.","dào zhī yǐ dé， qí zhī yǐ lǐ， yǒu chǐ qiě gé。","道之以德，齊之以禮，有恥且格。"
