"1787","Soon after, as Fan Ch'ih was driving him, the Master told him, saying, 'Mang-sun asked me what filial piety was, and I answered him, - ""not being disobedient.","fán chí yù， zǐ gào zhī yuē， mèng sūn wèn xiào yú wǒ， wǒ duì yuē， wú wéi。","樊遲御，子告之曰，孟孫問孝於我，我對曰，無違。"
"1788","Mang Wu asked what filial piety was. The Master said, 'Parents are anxious lest their children should be sick.","mèng wǔ bó wèn xiào， zǐ yuē， fù mǔ wéi qí jí zhī yōu。","孟武伯問孝，子曰，父母唯其疾之憂。"
"1789","The Master said, 'If a man keeps cherishing his old knowledge, so as continually to be acquiring new, he may be a teacher of others.","zǐ yuē， wēn gù ér zhī xīn， kě yǐ wéi shī yǐ。","子曰，溫故而知新，可以為師矣。"
"1790","The Master said, 'The accomplished scholar is not a utensil.","zǐ yuē， jūn zǐ bù qì。","子曰，君子不器。"
"1791","Tsze-kung asked what constituted the superior man. The Master said, 'He acts before he speaks, and afterwards speaks according to his actions.","zǐ gòng wèn jūn zǐ， zǐ yuē， xiān xíng qí yán， ér hòu cóng zhī。","子貢問君子，子曰，先行其言，而後從之。"
"1792","The Master said, 'The superior man is catholic and no partisan. The mean man is partisan and not catholic.","zǐ yuē， jūn zǐ zhōu ér bù bǐ， xiǎo rén bǐ ér bù zhōu。","子曰，君子周而不比，小人比而不周。"
"1793","The Master said, 'Learning without thought is labour lost; thought without learning is perilous.","zǐ yuē， xué ér bù sī zé wǎng， sī ér bù xué zé dài。","子曰，學而不思則罔，思而不學則殆。"
"1794","The Master said, 'The study of strange doctrines is injurious indeed!","zǐ yuē， gōng hū yì duān， sī hài yě yǐ。","子曰，攻乎異端，斯害也己。"
"1795","Some one addressed Confucius, saying, 'Sir, why are you not engaged in the government?","huò wèi kǒng zǐ yuē， zǐ xī bù wéi zhèng。","或謂孔子曰，子奚不為政。"
"1796","Tsze-chang asked whether the affairs of ten ages after could be known.","zǐ zhāng wèn shí shì， kě zhī yě。","子張問十世，可知也。"
"1797","The Master said, 'For a man to sacrifice to a spirit which does not belong to him is flattery.","zǐ yuē， fēi qí guǐ ér jì zhī， tiǎn yě。","子曰，非其鬼而祭之，諂也。"
"1798","Lin Fang asked what was the first thing to be attended to in ceremonies.","lín fàng wèn lǐ zhī běn。","林放問禮之本。"
"1799","The Master said, 'A great question indeed!","zǐ yuē， dà zāi wèn。","子曰，大哉問。"
"1800","In festive ceremonies, it is better to be sparing than extravagant. In the ceremonies of mourning, it is better that there be deep sorrow than a minute attention to observances.","lǐ， yǔ qí shē yě， níng jiǎn， sāng， yǔ qí yì yě， níng qī。","禮，與其奢也，寧儉，喪，與其易也，寧戚。"
"1801","The Master said, 'The rude tribes of the east and north have their princes, and are not like the States of our great land which are without them.","zǐ yuē， yí dí zhī yǒu jūn， bù rú zhū xià zhī wáng yě。","子曰，夷狄之有君，不如諸夏之亡也。"
"1802","The Master said, 'At the great sacrifice, after the pouring out of the libation, I have no wish to look on.","zǐ yuē， dì， zì jì guàn ér wǎng zhě， wú bù yù guān zhī yǐ。","子曰，禘，自既灌而往者，吾不欲觀之矣。"
"1803","He sacrificed to the dead, as if they were present. He sacrificed to the spirits, as if the spirits were present.","wáng sūn jiǎ wèn yuē， yǔ qí mèi yú ào， níng mèi yú zào， hé wèi yě。","王孫賈問曰，與其媚於奧，寧媚於(zao4上穴，中土，下黽)，何謂也。"
"1804","The Master said, 'I consider my not being present at the sacrifice, as if I did not sacrifice.","zǐ yuē， bù rán， huò zuì yú tiān， wú suǒ dǎo yě。","子曰，不然，獲罪於天，無所禱也。"
"1805","The Master said, 'Chau had the advantage of viewing the two past dynasties. How complete and elegant are its regulations! I follow Chau.","zǐ yuē， zhōu jiàn yú èr dài， yù yù hū wén zāi， wú cóng zhōu。","子曰，周監於二代，鬱郁乎文哉，吾從周。"
"1806","The Master said, 'In archery it is not going through the leather which is the principal thing; - because people's strength is not equal. This was the old way.","zǐ yuē， shè bù zhǔ pí， wèi lì bù tóng kē， gǔ zhī dào yě。","子曰，射不主皮，為力不同科，古之道也。"
"1807","The Master said, 'The full observance of the rules of propriety in serving one's prince is accounted by people to be flattery.","zǐ gòng yù qù gào shuò zhī xì yáng。 zǐ yuē， cì yě， ěr ài qí yáng， wǒ ài qí lǐ。","子貢欲去告朔之餼羊。子曰，賜也，爾愛其羊，我愛其禮。"
"1808","The Master said, 'The Kwan Tsu is expressive of enjoyment without being licentious, and of grief without being hurtfully excessive.","zǐ yuē， guān suī lè ér bù yín， āi ér bù shāng。","子曰，關睢樂而不淫，哀而不傷。"
"1809","The Master said of the Shao that it was perfectly beautiful and also perfectly good. He said of the Wu that it was perfectly beautiful but not perfectly good.","zǐ wèi sháo， jǐn měi yǐ， yòu jǐn shàn yě， wèi wǔ， jǐn měi yǐ， wèi jǐn shàn yě。","子謂韶，盡美矣，又盡善也，謂武，盡美矣，未盡善也。"
"1810","The Master said, 'High station filled without indulgent generosity; ceremonies performed without reverence; mourning conducted without sorrow; - wherewith should I contemplate such ways?","zǐ yuē， jū shàng bù kuān， wéi lǐ bù jìng， lín sāng bù āi， wú hé yǐ guān zhī zāi。 lǐ rén dì sì","子曰，居上不寬，為禮不敬，臨喪不哀，吾何以觀之哉。里仁第四"
"1811","The Master said, 'It is only the (truly) virtuous man, who can love, or who can hate, others.","zǐ yuē， wéi rén zhě， néng hào rén， néng wù rén。","子曰，惟仁者，能好人，能惡人。"
"1812","The Master said, 'If the will be set on virtue, there will be no practice of wickedness.","zǐ yuē， gǒu zhì yú rén yǐ， wú è yě。","子曰，苟志於仁矣，無惡也。"
"1813","Is any one able for one day to apply his strength to virtue? I have not seen the case in which his strength would be insufficient.","yǒu néng yī rì yòng qí lì yú rén yǐ hū， wǒ wèi jiàn lì bù zú zhě。","有能一日用其力於仁矣乎，我未見力不足者。"
"1814","Should there possibly be any such case, I have not seen it.","gài yǒu zhī yǐ， wǒ wèi zhī jiàn yě。","蓋有之矣，我未之見也。"
"1815","The Master said, 'The faults of men are characteristic of the class to which they belong. By observing a man's faults, it may be known that he is virtuous.","zǐ yuē， rén zhī guò yě， gè yú qí dǎng， guān guò， sī zhī rén yǐ。","子曰，人之過也，各於其黨，觀過，斯知仁矣。"
"1816","The Master said, 'If a man in the morning hear the right way, he may die in the evening without regret.","zǐ yuē， shì zhì yú dào， ér chǐ wù yī wù shí zhě， wèi zú yǔ yì yě。","子曰，士志於道，而恥惡衣惡食者，未足與議也。"
"1817","The Master said, 'The superior man, in the world, does not set his mind either for anything, or against anything; what is right he will follow.","zǐ yuē， jūn zǐ zhī yú tiān xià yě， wú shì yě， wú mò yě， yì zhī yǔ bǐ。","子曰，君子之於天下也，無適也，無莫也，義之與比。"
"1818","The Master said: 'He who acts with a constant view to his own advantage will be much murmured against.","zǐ yuē， fàng yú lì ér xíng， duō yuàn。","子曰，放於利而行，多怨。"
"1819","The Master said, 'Shan, my doctrine is that of an all-pervading unity.' The disciple Tsang replied, 'Yes.","zǐ yuē， cān hū， wú dào yī yǐ guàn zhī。 zēng zǐ yuē， wéi。","子曰，參乎，吾道一以貫之。曾子曰，唯。"
"1820","The Master said, 'The mind of the superior man is conversant with righteousness; the mind of the mean man is conversant with gain.","zǐ yuē， jūn zǐ yù yú yì， xiǎo rén yù yú lì。","子曰，君子喻於義，小人喻於利。"
"1821","The Master said, 'When we see men of worth, we should think of equalling them; when we see men of a contrary character, we should turn inwards and examine ourselves.","zǐ yuē， jiàn xián sī qí yān， jiàn bù xián ér nèi zì xǐng yě。","子曰，見賢思齊焉，見不賢而內自省也。"
"1822","The Master said, 'If the son for three years does not alter from the way of his father, he may be called filial.","zǐ yuē， sān nián wú gǎi yú fù zhī dào， kě wèi xiào yǐ。","子曰，三年無改於父之道，可謂孝矣。"
"1823","The Master said, 'The years of parents may by no means not be kept in the memory, as an occasion at once for joy and for fear.","zǐ yuē， fù mǔ zhī nián， bù kě bù zhī yě， yī zé yǐ xǐ， yī zé yǐ jù。","子曰，父母之年，不可不知也，一則以喜，一則以懼。"
"1824","The Master said, 'The reason why the ancients did not readily give utterance to their words, was that they feared lest their actions should not come up to them.","zǐ yuē， gǔ zhě yán zhī bù chū， chǐ gōng zhī bù dài yě。","子曰，古者言之不出，恥躬之不逮也。"
"1825","The Master said, 'The cautious seldom err.","zǐ yuē， yǐ yuē， shī zhī zhě xiǎn yǐ。","子曰，以約，失之者鮮矣。"
"1826","The Master said, 'The superior man wishes to be slow in his speech and earnest in his conduct.","zǐ yuē， jūn zǐ yù nè yú yán， ér mǐn yú xíng。","子曰，君子欲訥於言，而敏於行。"
"1827","The Master said, 'Virtue is not left to stand alone. He who practises it will have neighbors.","zǐ yuē， dé bù gū， bì yǒu lín。","子曰，德不孤，必有鄰。"
"1828","Tsze-yu said, 'In serving a prince, frequent remonstrances lead to disgrace. Between friends, frequent reproofs make the friendship distant.","zǐ yóu yuē， shì jūn shuò， sī rǔ yǐ， péng yǒu shuò， sī shū yǐ。 gōng yě zhǎng dì wǔ","子游曰，事君數，斯辱矣，朋友數，斯疏矣。公冶長第五"
"1829","The Master said of Tsze-chien, 'Of superior virtue indeed is such a man! If there were not virtuous men in Lu, how could this man have acquired this character?","zǐ wèi zǐ jiàn， jūn zǐ zāi ruò rén， lǔ wú jūn zǐ zhě， sī yān qǔ sī。","子謂子賤，君子哉若人，魯無君子者，斯焉取斯。"
"1830","Tsze-kung asked, 'What do you say of me, Ts'ze? The Master said, 'You are a utensil.' 'What utensil?' 'A gemmed sacrificial utensil.","zǐ gòng wèn yuē， cì yě hé rú。 zǐ yuē， rǔ qì yě。 yuē， hé qì yě。 yuē， hú liǎn yě。","子貢問曰，賜也何如。子曰，女器也。曰，何器也。曰，瑚璉也。"
"1831","Some one said, 'Yung is truly virtuous, but he is not ready with his tongue.","huò yuē， yōng yě rén， ér bù nìng。","或曰，雍也仁，而不佞。"
"1832","The Master was wishing Ch'i-tiao K'ai to enter on official employment. He replied, 'I am not yet able to rest in the assurance of THIS.' The Master was pleased.","zǐ shǐ qī diāo kāi shì。 duì yuē， wú sī zhī wèi néng xìn。 zǐ yuè。","子使漆雕開仕。對曰，吾斯之未能信。子說。"
"1833","Mang Wu asked about Tsze-lu, whether he was perfectly virtuous. The Master said, 'I do not know.","mèng wǔ bó wèn zǐ lù rén hū。 zǐ yuē， bù zhī yě。","孟武伯問子路仁乎。子曰，不知也。"
"1834","The Master said to Tsze-kung, 'Which do you consider superior, yourself or Hui?","zǐ wèi zǐ gòng yuē， rǔ yǔ huí yě， shú yù。","子謂子貢曰，女與回也，孰愈。"
"1835","Tsze-kung replied, 'How dare I compare myself with Hui? Hui hears one point and knows all about a subject; I hear one point, and know a second.","duì yuē， cì yě， hé gǎn wàng huí， huí yě， wén yī yǐ zhī shí， cì yě， wén yī yǐ zhī èr。","對曰，賜也，何敢望回，回也，聞一以知十，賜也，聞一以知二。"
"1836","The Master said, 'You are not equal to him. I grant you, you are not equal to him.","zǐ yuē， fú rú yě， wú yǔ rǔ， fú rú yě。","子曰，弗如也，吾與女，弗如也。"
"1837","Tsze-kung said, 'What I do not wish men to do to me, I also wish not to do to men.' The Master said, 'Ts'ze, you have not attained to that.","zǐ gòng yuē， wǒ bù yù rén zhī jiā zhū wǒ yě， wú yì yù wú jiā zhū rén。 zǐ yuē， cì yě， fēi ěr suǒ jí yě。","子貢曰，我不欲人之加諸我也，吾亦欲無加諸人。子曰，賜也，非爾所及也。"
"1838","Tsze-kung said, 'The Master's personal displays of his principles and ordinary descriptions of them may be heard. His discourses about man's nature, and the way of Heaven, cannot be heard.","zǐ gòng yuē， fū zǐ zhī wén zhāng， kě dé ér wén yě， fū zǐ zhī yán xìng yǔ tiān dào， bù kě dé ér wén yě。","子貢曰，夫子之文章，可得而聞也，夫子之言性與天道，不可得而聞也。"
"1839","When Tsze-lu heard anything, if he had not yet succeeded in carrying it into practice, he was only afraid lest he should hear something else.","zǐ lù yǒu wén， wèi zhī néng xíng， wéi kǒng yǒu wén。","子路有聞，未之能行，唯恐有聞。"
"1840","The Master said, 'Yen P'ing knew well how to maintain friendly intercourse. The acquaintance might be long, but he showed the same respect as at first.","zǐ yuē， yàn píng zhòng shàn yǔ rén jiāo， jiǔ ér jìng zhī。","子曰，晏平仲善與人交，久而敬之。"
"1841","Chi Wan thought thrice, and then acted. When the Master was informed of it, he said, 'Twice may do.","jì wén zǐ sān sī ér hòu xíng。 zǐ wén zhī yuē， zài， sī kě yǐ。","季文子三思而後行。子聞之曰，再，斯可矣。"
"1842","The Master said, 'Po-i and Shu-ch'i did not keep the former wickednesses of men in mind, and hence the resentments directed towards them were few.","zǐ yuē， bó yí shū qí， bù niàn jiù è， yuàn shì yòng xī。","子曰，伯夷叔齊，不念舊惡，怨是用希。"
"1843","The Master said, 'It is all over! I have not yet seen one who could perceive his faults, and inwardly accuse himself.","zǐ yuē， yǐ yǐ hū， wú wèi jiàn néng jiàn qí guò， ér nèi zì sòng zhě yě。","子曰，已矣乎，吾未見能見其過，而內自訟者也。"
"1844","The Master said, 'In a hamlet of ten families, there may be found one honourable and sincere as I am, but not so fond of learning.","zǐ yuē， shí shì zhī yì， bì yǒu zhōng xìn， rú qiū zhě yān， bù rú qiū zhī hào xué yě。 yōng yě dì liù","子曰，十室之邑，必有忠信，如丘者焉，不如丘之好學也。雍也第六"
"1845","The Master said, 'There is Yung! - He might occupy the place of a prince.","zǐ yuē， yōng yě kě shǐ nán miàn。","子曰，雍也可使南面。"
"1846","Chung-kung asked about Tsze-sang Po-tsze. The Master said, 'He may pass. He does not mind small matters.","zhòng gōng wèn zǐ sāng bó zǐ。 zǐ yuē， kě yě， jiǎn。","仲弓問子桑伯子。子曰，可也，簡。"
"1847","The Master said, 'Yung's words are right.","zǐ yuē， yōng zhī yán rán。","子曰，雍之言然。"
"1848","Yuan Sze being made governor of his town by the Master, he gave him nine hundred measures of grain, but Sze declined them.","yuán sī wéi zhī zǎi， yǔ zhī sù jiǔ bǎi， cí。","原思為之宰，與之粟九百，辭。"
"1849","The Master said, 'Do not decline them. May you not give them away in the neighborhoods, hamlets, towns, and villages?","zǐ yuē， wú， yǐ yǔ ěr lín lǐ xiāng dǎng hū。","子曰，毋，以與爾鄰裡鄉黨乎。"
"1850","The Master said to Tsze-hsia, 'Do you be a scholar after the style of the superior man, and not after that of the mean man.","zǐ wèi zǐ xià yuē， rǔ wéi jūn zǐ rú， wú wéi xiǎo rén rú。","子謂子夏曰，女為君子儒，無為小人儒。"
"1851","The Master said, 'Without the specious speech of the litanist T'o and the beauty of the prince Chao of Sung, it is difficult to escape in the present age.","zǐ yuē， bù yǒu zhù tuó zhī nìng， ér yǒu sòng zhāo zhī měi， nán hū miǎn yú jīn zhī shì yǐ。","子曰，不有祝鮀之佞，而有宋朝之美，難乎免於今之世矣。"
"1852","The Master said, 'Who can go out but by the door? How is it that men will not walk according to these ways?","zǐ yuē， shuí néng chū bù yóu hù？ hé mò yóu sī dào yě。","子曰，誰能出不由戶？何莫由斯道也。"
"1853","The Master said, 'Man is born for uprightness. If a man lose his uprightness, and yet live, his escape from death is the effect of mere good fortune.","zǐ yuē， rén zhī shēng yě zhí， wǎng zhī shēng yě， xìng ér miǎn。","子曰，人之生也直，罔之生也，幸而免。"
"1854","The Master said, 'They who know the truth are not equal to those who love it, and they who love it are not equal to those who delight in it.","zǐ yuē， zhī zhī zhě， bù rú hào zhī zhě， hào zhī zhě， bù rú lè zhī zhě。","子曰，知之者，不如好之者，好之者，不如樂之者。"
"1855","The Master said, 'To those whose talents are above mediocrity, the highest subjects may be announced. To those who are below mediocrity, the highest subjects may not be announced.","zǐ yuē， zhōng rén yǐ shàng， kě yǐ yù shàng yě， zhōng rén yǐ xià， bù kě yǐ yù shàng yě。","子曰，中人以上，可以語上也，中人以下，不可以語上也。"
"1856","The Master said, 'Ch'i, by one change, would come to the State of Lu. Lu, by one change, would come to a State where true principles predominated.","zǐ yuē， qí yī biàn， zhì yú lǔ， lǔ yī biàn， zhì yú dào。","子曰，齊一變，至於魯，魯一變，至於道。"
"1857","The Master said, 'A cornered vessel without corners. - A strange cornered vessel! A strange cornered vessel!","zǐ yuē， gū bù gū， gū zāi gū zāi。","子曰，觚不觚，觚哉觚哉。"
"1858","The Master said, 'The superior man, extensively studying all learning, and keeping himself under the restraint of the rules of propriety, may thus likewise not overstep what is right.","zǐ yuē， jūn zǐ bó xué yú wén， yuē zhī yǐ lǐ， yì kě yǐ fú pàn yǐ fú。","子曰，君子博學於文，約之以禮，亦可以弗畔矣夫。"
"1859","The Master having visited Nan-tsze, Tsze-lu was displeased, on which the Master swore, saying, 'Wherein I have done improperly, may Heaven reject me, may Heaven reject me!","zǐ jiàn nán zǐ， zǐ lù bù yuè， fū zǐ shǐ zhī， yuē， yǔ suǒ fǒu zhě， tiān yàn zhī， tiān yàn zhī。","子見南子，子路不說，夫子矢之，曰，予所否者，天厭之，天厭之。"
"1860","The Master said, 'Perfect is the virtue which is according to the Constant Mean! Rare for a long time has been its practise among the people.","zǐ yuē，zhōng yōng zhī wéi dé yě，qí zhì yǐ hū，mín xiǎn jiǔ yǐ。","子曰，中庸之為德也，其至矣乎，民鮮久矣。"
"1861","Now the man of perfect virtue, wishing to be established himself, seeks also to establish others; wishing to be enlarged himself, he seeks also to enlarge others.","fú rén zhě，jǐ yù lì ér lì rén，jǐ yù dá ér dá rén。","夫仁者，己欲立而立人，己欲達而達人。"
"1862","To be able to judge of others by what is nigh in ourselves; - this may be called the art of virtue.","néng jìn qǔ pì，kě wèi rén zhī fāng yě yǐ。 shù ér dì qī","能近取譬，可謂仁之方也已。述而第七"
"1863","The Master said, 'A transmitter and not a maker, believing in and loving the ancients, I venture to compare myself with our old P'ang.","zǐ yuē， shù ér bù zuò， xìn ér hào gǔ， qiè bǐ yú wǒ lǎo péng。","子曰，述而不作，信而好古，竊比於我老彭。"
"1864","The Master said, 'The silent treasuring up of knowledge; learning without satiety; and instructing others without being wearied: - which one of these things belongs to me?","zǐ yuē， mò ér zhì zhī， xué ér bù yàn， huì rén bù juàn， hé yǒu yú wǒ zāi。","子曰，默而識之，學而不厭，誨人不倦，何有於我哉。"
"1865","When the Master was unoccupied with business, his manner was easy, and he looked pleased.","zǐ zhī yàn jū， shēn shēn rú yě， yāo yāo rú yě。","子之燕居，申申如也，夭夭如也。"
"1866","The Master said, 'Extreme is my decay. For a long time, I have not dreamed, as I was wont to do, that I saw the duke of Chau.","zǐ yuē， shèn yǐ wú shuāi yě， jiǔ yǐ， wú bù fù mèng jiàn zhōu gōng。","子曰，甚矣吾衰也，久矣，吾不復夢見周公。"
"1867","The Master said, 'Let the will be set on the path of duty.","zǐ yuē， zhì yú dào。","子曰，志於道。"
"1868","Let every attainment in what is good be firmly grasped.","jù yú dé。","據於德。"
"1869","Let perfect virtue be accorded with.","yī yú rén。","依於仁。"
"1870","Let relaxation and enjoyment be found in the polite arts.","yóu yú yì。","遊於藝。"
"1871","The Master said, 'From the man bringing his bundle of dried flesh for my teaching upwards, I have never refused instruction to any one.","zǐ yuē， zì xíng sù xiū yǐ shàng， wú wèi cháng wú huì yān。","子曰，自行束脩以上，吾未嘗無誨焉。"
"1872","When the Master was eating by the side of a mourner, he never ate to the full.","zǐ shí yú yǒu sāng zhě zhī cè， wèi cháng bǎo yě。","子食於有喪者之側，未嘗飽也。"
"1873","He did not sing on the same day in which he had been weeping.","zǐ yú shì rì kū， zé bù gē。","子於是日哭，則不歌。"
"1874","Tsze-lu said, 'If you had the conduct of the armies of a great State, whom would you have to act with you?","zǐ lù yuē，zǐ xíng sān jūn zé shuí yǔ。","子路曰，子行三軍則誰與。"
"1875","The things in reference to which the Master exercised the greatest caution were - fasting, war, and sickness.","zǐ zhī suǒ shèn，qí，zhàn，jí。","子之所慎，齊，戰，疾。"
"1876","Yen Yu said, 'Is our Master for the ruler of Wei?' Tsze-kung said, 'Oh! I will ask him.","rǎn yǒu yuē，fū zǐ wèi wèi jūn hū。 zǐ gòng yuē，nuò，wú jiāng wèn zhī。","冉有曰，夫子為衛君乎。子貢曰，諾，吾將問之。"
"1877","The Duke of Sheh asked Tsze-lu about Confucius, and Tsze-lu did not answer him.","yè gōng wèn kǒng zǐ yú zǐ lù，zǐ lù bù duì。","葉公問孔子於子路，子路不對。"
"1878","The Master said, 'I am not one who was born in the possession of knowledge; I am one who is fond of antiquity, and earnest in seeking it there.","zǐ yuē，wǒ fēi shēng ér zhī zhě，hào gǔ，mǐn yǐ qiú zhī zhě yě。","子曰，我非生而知之者，好古，敏以求之者也。"
"1879","The subjects on which the Master did not talk, were - extraordinary things, feats of strength, disorder, and spiritual beings.","zǐ bù yù，guài，lì，luàn，shén。","子不語，怪，力，亂，神。"
"1880","The Master said, 'Heaven produced the virtue that is in me. Hwan T'ui - what can he do to me?","zǐ yuē，tiān shēng dé yú yǔ，huán tuī qí rú yǔ hé。","子曰，天生德於予，桓魋其如予何。"
"1881","There were four things which the Master taught, - letters, ethics, devotion of soul, and truthfulness.","zǐ yǐ sì jiào，wén，xíng，zhōng，xìn。","子以四教，文，行，忠，信。"
"1882","The Master said, 'A sage it is not mine to see; could I see a man of real talent and virtue, that would satisfy me.","zǐ yuē，shèng rén wú bù dé ér jiàn zhī yǐ，dé jiàn jūn zǐ zhě，sī kě yǐ。","子曰，聖人吾不得而見之矣，得見君子者，斯可矣。"
"1883","The Master said, 'A good man it is not mine to see; could I see a man possessed of constancy, that would satisfy me.","zǐ yuē，shàn rén wú bù dé ér jiàn zhī yǐ，dé jiàn yǒu héng zhě，sī kě yǐ。","子曰，善人吾不得而見之矣，得見有恆者，斯可矣。"
"1884","The Master angled, - but did not use a net. He shot, - but not at birds perching.","zǐ diào ér bù gāng，yì bù shè sù。","子釣而不綱，弋不射宿。"
"1885","It was difficult to talk (profitably and reputably) with the people of Hu-hsiang, and a lad of that place having had an interview with the Master, the disciples doubted.","hù xiāng nán yǔ yán，tóng zǐ jiàn，mén rén huò。","互鄉難與言，童子見，門人惑。"
"1886","The Master said, 'Extravagance leads to insubordination, and parsimony to meanness. It is better to be mean than to be insubordinate.","zǐ yuē，shē zé bù xùn，jiǎn zé gù，yǔ qí bù xùn yě，níng gù。","子曰，奢則不孫，儉則固，與其不孫也，寧固。"
"1887","The Master said, 'The superior man is satisfied and composed; the mean man is always full of distress.","zǐ yuē，jūn zǐ tǎn dàng dàng，xiǎo rén cháng qī qī。","子曰，君子坦蕩蕩，小人長慼慼。"
"1888","The Master was mild, and yet dignified; majestic, and yet not fierce; respectful, and yet easy.","zǐ wēn ér lì，wēi ér bù měng，gōng ér ān。 tài bó dì bā","子溫而厲，威而不猛，恭而安。泰伯第八"
"1889","The philosopher Tsang said, 'The officer may not be without breadth of mind and vigorous endurance. His burden is heavy and his course is long.","zēng zǐ yuē， shì， bù kě yǐ bù hóng yì， rèn zhòng ér dào yuǎn。","曾子曰，士，不可以不弘毅，任重而道遠。"
"1890","Perfect virtue is the burden which he considers it is his to sustain; - is it not heavy? Only with death does his course stop; - is it not long?","rén yǐ wéi jǐ rèn，bù yì zhòng hū，sǐ ér hòu yǐ，bù yì yuǎn hū。","仁以為己任，不亦重乎，死而後已，不亦遠乎。"
"1891","The Master said, 'It is by the Odes that the mind is aroused.","zǐ yuē，xìng yú shī。","子曰，興於詩。"
"1892","It is by the Rules of Propriety that the character is established.","lì yú lǐ。","立於禮。"
"1893","It is from Music that the finish is received.","chéng yú yuè。","成於樂。"
"1894","The Master said, 'The people may be made to follow a path of action, but they may not be made to understand it.","zǐ yuē，mín kě shǐ yóu zhī，bù kě shǐ zhī zhī。","子曰，民可使由之，不可使知之。"
"1895","The Master said, 'It is not easy to find a man who has learned for three years without coming to be good.","zǐ yuē，sān nián xué，bù zhì yú gǔ，bù yì dé yě。","子曰，三年學，不至於穀，不易得也。"
"1896","The Master said, 'With sincere faith he unites the love of learning; holding firm to death, he is perfecting the excellence of his course.","zǐ yuē，dǔ xìn hào xué，shǒu sǐ shàn dào。","子曰，篤信好學，守死善道。"
"1897","The Master said, 'He who is not in any particular office, has nothing to do with plans for the administration of its duties.","zǐ yuē，bù zài qí wèi，bù móu qí zhèng。","子曰，不在其位，不謀其政。"
"1898","The Master said, 'When the music master Chih first entered on his office, the finish of the Kwan Tsu was magnificent; - how it filled the ears!","zǐ yuē，shī zhì zhī shǐ，guān suī zhī luàn，yáng yáng hū yíng ěr zāi。","子曰，師摯之始，關睢之亂，洋洋乎盈耳哉。"
"1899","The Master said, 'Ardent and yet not upright; stupid and yet not attentive; simple and yet not sincere: - such persons I do not understand.","zǐ yuē，kuáng ér bù zhí，tōng ér bù yuàn，kōng kōng ér bù xìn，wú bù zhī zhī yǐ。","子曰，狂而不直，侗而不願，悾悾而不信，吾不知之矣。"
"1900","The Master said, 'Learn as if you could not reach your object, and were always fearing also lest you should lose it.","zǐ yuē，xué rú bù jí，yóu kǒng shī zhī。","子曰，學如不及，猶恐失之。"
"1901","The Master said, 'How majestic was the manner in which Shun and Yu held possession of the empire, as if it were nothing to them!","zǐ yuē，wēi wēi hū，shùn yǔ zhī yǒu tiān xià yě，ér bù yǔ yān。","子曰，巍巍乎，舜禹之有天下也，而不與焉。"
"1902","How majestic was he in the works which he accomplished! How glorious in the elegant regulations which he instituted!","wēi wēi hū， qí yǒu chéng gōng yě， huàn hū， qí yǒu wén zhāng。","巍巍乎，其有成功也，煥乎，其有文章。"
"1903","Shun had five ministers, and the empire was well-governed.","shùn yǒu chén wǔ rén， ér tiān xià zhì。","舜有臣五人，而天下治。"
"1904","King Wu said, 'I have ten able ministers.","wǔ wáng yuē， yǔ yǒu luàn chén shí rén。","武王曰，予有亂臣十人。"
"1905","The subjects of which the Master seldom spoke were - profitableness, and also the appointments of Heaven, and perfect virtue.","zǐ hǎn yán，lì，yǔ mìng，yǔ rén。","子罕言，利，與命，與仁。"
"1906","The Master heard the observation, and said to his disciples, 'What shall I practise? Shall I practise charioteering, or shall I practise archery? I will practise charioteering.","zǐ wén zhī，wèi mén dì zǐ yuē，wú hé zhí，zhí yù hū，zhí shè hū，wú zhí yù yǐ。","子聞之，謂門弟子曰，吾何執，執御乎，執射乎，吾執御矣。"
"1907","The Master said, 'The linen cap is that prescribed by the rules of ceremony, but now a silk one is worn. It is economical, and I follow the common practice.","zǐ yuē，má miǎn，lǐ yě，jīn yě chún，jiǎn，wú cóng zhòng。","子曰，麻冕，禮也，今也純，儉，吾從眾。"
"1908","There were four things from which the Master was entirely free. He had no foregone conclusions, no arbitrary predeterminations, no obstinacy, and no egoism.","zǐ jué sì，wú yì，wú bì，wú gù，wú wǒ。","子絕四，毋意，毋必，毋固，毋我。"
"1909","The Master was put in fear in K'wang.","zǐ wèi yú kuāng。","子畏於匡。"
"1910","He said, 'After the death of King Wan, was not the cause of truth lodged here in me?","yuē，wén wáng jì mò，wén bù zài zī hū。","曰，文王既沒，文不在茲乎。"
"1911","A high officer asked Tsze-kung, saying, 'May we not say that your Master is a sage? How various is his ability!","dà zǎi wèn yú zǐ gòng，yuē，fū zǐ shèng zhě yú，hé qí duō néng yě。","大宰問於子貢，曰，夫子聖者與，何其多能也。"
"1912","Tsze-kung said, 'Certainly Heaven has endowed him unlimitedly. He is about a sage. And, moreover, his ability is various.","zǐ gòng yuē，gù tiān zòng zhī jiāng shèng，yòu duō néng yě。","子貢曰，固天縱之將聖，又多能也。"
"1913","Lao said, 'The Master said, ""Having no official employment, I acquired many arts.","láo yuē，zǐ yún，wú bù shì，gù yì。","牢曰，子云，吾不試，故藝。"
"1914","The Master said, 'The FANG bird does not come; the river sends forth no map: - it is all over with me!","zǐ yuē，fèng niǎo bù zhì，hé bù chū tú，wú yǐ yǐ hū。","子曰，鳳鳥不至，河不出圖，吾已矣乎。"
"1915","The Master being very ill, Tsze-lu wished the disciples to act as ministers to him.","zǐ jí bìng， zǐ lù shǐ mén rén wéi chén。","子疾病，子路使門人為臣。"
"1916","The Master was wishing to go and live among the nine wild tribes of the east.","zǐ yù jū jiǔ yí。","子欲居九夷。"
"1917","Some one said, 'They are rude. How can you do such a thing?' The Master said, 'If a superior man dwelt among them, what rudeness would there be?","huò yuē， lòu， rú zhī hé。 zǐ yuē， jūn zǐ jū zhī， hé lòu zhī yǒu。","或曰，陋，如之何。子曰，君子居之，何陋之有。"
"1918","The Master said, 'I returned from Wei to Lu, and then the music was reformed, and the pieces in the Royal songs and Praise songs all found their proper places.","zǐ yuē， wú zì wèi fǎn lǔ， rán hòu yuè zhèng， yǎ sòng gè dé qí suǒ。","子曰，吾自衛反魯，然後樂正，雅頌各得其所。"
"1919","The Master standing by a stream, said, 'It passes on just like this, not ceasing day or night!","zǐ zài chuān shàng yuē， shì zhě rú sī fú， bù shě zhòu yè。","子在川上曰，逝者如斯夫，不捨晝夜。"
"1920","The Master said, 'I have not seen one who loves virtue as he loves beauty.","zǐ yuē， wú wèi jiàn hào dé， rú hào sè zhě yě。","子曰，吾未見好德，如好色者也。"
"1921","The Master said, 'Never flagging when I set forth anything to him; - ah! that is Hui.","zǐ yuē， yù zhī ér bù duò zhě， qí huí yě yú。","子曰，語之而不惰者，其回也與。"
"1922","The Master said of Yen Yuan, 'Alas! I saw his constant advance. I never saw him stop in his progress.","zǐ wèi yán yuān yuē， xī hū， wú jiàn qí jìn yě， wèi jiàn qí zhǐ yě。","子謂顏淵曰，惜乎，吾見其進也，未見其止也。"
"1923","The Master said, 'Hold faithfulness and sincerity as first principles. Have no friends not equal to yourself. When you have faults, do not fear to abandon them.","zǐ yuē， zhǔ zhōng xìn， wú yǒu bù rú jǐ zhě， guò zé wù dàn gǎi。","子曰，主忠信，毋友不如己者，過則勿憚改。"
"1924","The Master said, 'The commander of the forces of a large state may be carried off, but the will of even a common man cannot be taken from him.","zǐ yuē， sān jūn kě duó shī yě， pǐ fū bù kě duó zhì yě。","子曰，三軍可奪師也，匹夫不可奪志也。"
"1925","He dislikes none, he covets nothing; - what can he do but what is good!","bù zhì bù qiú， hé yòng bù zāng。","不忮不求，何用不臧。"
"1926","Tsze-lu kept continually repeating these words of the ode, when the Master said, 'Those things are by no means sufficient to constitute (perfect) excellence.","zǐ lù zhōng shēn sòng zhī， zǐ yuē， shì dào yě， hé zú yǐ zāng。","子路終身誦之，子曰，是道也，何足以臧。"
"1927","The Master said, 'When the year becomes cold, then we know how the pine and the cypress are the last to lose their leaves.","zǐ yuē， suì hán， rán hòu zhī sōng bǎi zhī hòu diāo yě。","子曰，歲寒，然後知松柏之後彫也。"
"1928","The Master said, 'The wise are free from perplexities; the virtuous from anxiety; and the bold from fear.","zǐ yuē， zhī zhě bù huò， rén zhě bù yōu， yǒng zhě bù jù。","子曰，知者不惑，仁者不憂，勇者不懼。"
"1929","How the flowers of the aspen-plum flutter and turn! Do I not think of you? But your house is distant.","táng dì zhī huā， piān qí fǎn ér， qǐ bù ěr sī， shì shì yuǎn ér。","唐棣之華，偏其反而，豈不爾思，室是遠而。"
"1930","The Master said, 'It is the want of thought about it. How is it distant?","zǐ yuē，wèi zhī sī yě，wèi hé yuǎn zhī yǒu。xiāng dǎng dì shí。","子曰，未之思也，未何遠之有。鄉黨第十"
"1931","Confucius, in his village, looked simple and sincere, and as if he were not able to speak.","kǒng zǐ yú xiāng dǎng，xún xún rú yě，sì bù néng yán zhě。","孔子於鄉黨，恂恂如也，似不能言者。"
"1932","When the ruler was present, his manner displayed respectful uneasiness; it was grave, but self-possessed.","qí zài zōng miào cháo tíng，biàn biàn rán，wéi jǐn ěr。","其在宗廟朝廷，便便然，唯謹爾。"
"1933","When the prince called him to employ him in the reception of a visitor, his countenance appeared to change, and his legs to move forward with difficulty.","jūn zhào shǐ bìn，sè bó rú yě，zú jué rú yě。","君召使擯，色勃如也，足躩如也。"
"1934","He hastened forward, with his arms like the wings of a bird.","qū jìn，yì rú yě。","趨進，翼如也。"
"1935","When the guest had retired, he would report to the prince, 'The visitor is not turning round any more.","bīn tuì，bì fù mìng yuē，bīn bù gù yǐ。","賓退，必復命曰，賓不顧矣。"
"1936","When he entered the palace gate, he seemed to bend his body, as if it were not sufficient to admit him.","rù gōng mén，jū gōng rú yě，rú bù róng。","入公門，鞠躬如也，如不容。"
"1937","When he was standing, he did not occupy the middle of the gate-way; when he passed in or out, he did not tread upon the threshold.","lì bù zhōng mén，xíng bù lǚ yù。","立不中門，行不履閾。"
"1938","He ascended the reception hall, holding up his robe with both his hands, and his body bent; holding in his breath also, as if he dared not breathe.","shè qí shēng táng，jū gōng rú yě，bǐng qì sì bù xī zhě。","攝齊升堂，鞠躬如也，屏氣似不息者。"
"1939","In presenting the presents with which he was charged, he wore a placid appearance.","xiǎng lǐ，yǒu róng sè。","享禮，有容色。"
"1940","At his private audience, he looked highly pleased.","sī dí， yú yú rú yě","私覿，愉愉如也"
"1941","The superior man did not use a deep purple, or a puce colour, in the ornaments of his dress.","jūn zǐ bù yǐ gàn zōu shì。","君子不以紺緅飾。"
"1942","Even in his undress, he did not wear anything of a red or reddish colour.","hóng zǐ bù yǐ wéi xiè fú。","紅紫不以為褻服。"
"1943","In warm weather, he had a single garment either of coarse or fine texture, but he wore it displayed over an inner garment.","dāng shǔ zhěn chī xì， bì biǎo ér chū zhī。","當暑袗絺綌，必表而出之。"
"1944","Over lamb's fur he wore a garment of black; over fawn's fur one of white; and over fox's fur one of yellow.","zī yì gāo qiú， sù yì ní qiú， huáng yì hú qiú。","緇衣羔裘，素衣麑裘，黃衣狐裘。"
"1945","The fur robe of his undress was long, with the right sleeve short.","xiè qiú cháng， duǎn yòu mèi。","褻裘長，短右袂。"
"1946","He required his sleeping dress to be half as long again as his body.","bì yǒu qǐn yī， cháng yī shēn yǒu bàn。","必有寢衣，長一身有半。"
"1947","When staying at home, he used thick furs of the fox or the badger.","hú mò zhī hòu yǐ jū。","狐貉之厚以居。"
"1948","When he put off mourning, he wore all the appendages of the girdle.","qù sāng， wú suǒ bù pèi。","去喪，無所不佩。"
"1949","His under-garment, except when it was required to be of the curtain shape, was made of silk cut narrow above and wide below.","fēi wéi shang， bì shā zhī。","非帷裳，必殺之。"
"1950","He did not wear lamb's fur or a black cap, on a visit of condolence.","gāo qiú xuán guān bù yǐ diào。","羔裘玄冠不以弔。"
"1951","On the first day of the month he put on his court robes, and presented himself at court.","jí yuè， bì cháo fú ér cháo。","吉月，必朝服而朝。"
"1952","When fasting, he thought it necessary to have his clothes brightly clean and made of linen cloth.","zhāi， bì yǒu míng yī， bù。","齊，必有明衣，布。"
"1953","When fasting, he thought it necessary to change his food, and also to change the place where he commonly sat in the apartment.","zhāi bì biàn shí， jū bì qiān zuò。","齊必變食，居必遷坐。"
"1954","He did not dislike to have his rice finely cleaned, nor to have his minced meat cut quite small.","shí bù yàn jīng， kuài bù yàn xì。","食不厭精，膾不厭細。"
"1955","He did not eat meat which was not cut properly, nor what was served without its proper sauce.","gē bù zhèng bù shí， bù dé qí jiàng bù shí。","割不正不食，不得其醬不食。"
"1956","He did not partake of wine and dried meat bought in the market.","gū jiǔ shì fǔ bù shí。","沽酒市脯不食。"
"1957","He was never without ginger when he ate.","bù chè jiāng shí。","不撤薑食。"
"1958","He did not eat much.","bù duō shí。","不多食。"
"1959","When eating, he did not converse. When in bed, he did not speak.","shí bù yǔ， qǐn bù yán。","食不語，寢不言。"
"1960","Although his food might be coarse rice and vegetable soup, he would offer a little of it in sacrifice with a grave, respectful air.","suī shū shí cài gēng， guā jì， bì zhāi rú yě。","雖疏食菜羹，瓜祭，必齊如也。"
"1961","If his mat was not straight, he did not sit on it.","xí bù zhèng bù zuò。","席不正不坐。"
"1962","When the villagers were drinking together, on those who carried staffs going out, he went out immediately after.","xiāng rén yǐn jiǔ， zhàng zhě chū， sī chū yǐ。","鄉人飲酒，杖者出，斯出矣。"
"1963","When the villagers were going through their ceremonies to drive away pestilential influences, he put on his court robes and stood on the eastern steps.","xiāng rén nuó， cháo fú ér lì yú zuò jiē。","鄉人儺，朝服而立於阼階。"
"1964","When he was sending complimentary inquiries to any one in another State, he bowed twice as he escorted the messenger away.","wèn rén yú tā bāng， zài bài ér sòng zhī。","問人於他邦，再拜而送之。"
"1965","Chi K'ang having sent him a present of physic, he bowed and received it, saying, 'I do not know it. I dare not taste it.","kāng zǐ kuì yào， bài ér shòu zhī， yuē， qiū wèi dá， bù gǎn cháng。","康子饋藥，拜而受之，曰，丘未達，不敢嘗。"
"1966","The stable being burned down, when he was at court, on his return he said, 'Has any man been hurt?' He did not ask about the horses.","jiù fén， zǐ tuì cháo， yuē， shāng rén hū， bù wèn mǎ。","廄焚，子退朝，曰，傷人乎，不問馬。"
"1967","When he was in attendance on the prince and joining in the entertainment, the prince only sacrificed. He first tasted everything.","shì shí yú jūn， jūn jì， xiān fàn。","侍食於君，君祭，先飯。"
"1968","When the prince's order called him, without waiting for his carriage to be yoked, he went at once.","jūn mìng zhào， bù sì jià xíng yǐ。","君命召，不俟駕行矣。"
"1969","When he entered the ancestral temple of the State, he asked about everything.","rù dà miào měi shì wèn。","入大廟每事問。"
"1970","In bed, he did not lie like a corpse. At home, he did not put on any formal deportment.","qǐn bù shī， jū bù róng。","寢不屍，居不容。"
"1971","To any person in mourning he bowed forward to the crossbar of his carriage; he bowed in the same way to any one bearing the tables of population.","xiōng fú zhě shì zhī， shì fù bǎn zhě。","凶服者式之，式負版者。"
"1972","When he was at an entertainment where there was an abundance of provisions set before him, he would change countenance and rise up.","yǒu shèng zhuàn， bì biàn sè ér zuò。","有盛饌，必變色而作。"
"1973","On a sudden clap of thunder, or a violent wind, he would change countenance.","xùn léi， fēng liè， bì biàn。","迅雷，風烈，必變。"
"1974","When he was about to mount his carriage, he would stand straight, holding the cord.","shēng chē， bì zhèng lì， zhí suí。","升車，必正立，執綏。"
"1975","When he was in the carriage, he did not turn his head quite round, he did not talk hastily, he did not point with his hands.","chē zhōng， bù nèi gù， bù jí yán， bù qīn zhǐ。","車中，不內顧，不疾言，不親指。"
"1976","Seeing the countenance, it instantly rises. It flies round, and by and by settles.","sè sī jǔ yǐ， xiáng ér hòu jí。","色斯舉矣，翔而後集。"
"1977","The Master said, 'There is the hen-pheasant on the hill bridge. At its season! At its season!' Tsze-lu made a motion to it. Thrice it smelt him and then rose.","yuē， shān liáng cí zhì， shí zāi shí zāi。 zǐ lù gòng zhī， sān xiù ér zuò。 xiān jìn dì shí yī","曰，山樑雌雉，時哉時哉。子路共之，三嗅而作。先進第十一"
"1978","If I have occasion to use those things, I follow the men of former times.","rú yòng zhī， zé wú cóng xiān jìn。","如用之，則吾從先進。"
"1979","The Master said, 'Of those who were with me in Ch'an and Ts'ai, there are none to be found to enter my door.","zǐ yuē， cóng wǒ yú chén cài zhě， jiē bù jí mén yě。","子曰，從我於陳蔡者，皆不及門也。"
"1980","The Master said, 'Hui gives me no assistance. There is nothing that I say in which he does not delight.","zǐ yuē， huí yě， fēi zhù wǒ zhě yě， yú wú yán， wú suǒ bù yuè。","子曰，回也，非助我者也，於吾言，無所不說。"
"1981","The Master said, 'Filial indeed is Min Tsze- ch'ien! Other people say nothing of him different from the report of his parents and brothers.","zǐ yuē， xiào zāi mǐn zǐ qiān， rén bù jiàn yú qí fù mǔ kūn dì zhī yán。","子曰，孝哉閔子騫，人不間於其父母昆弟之言。"
"1982","Nan Yung was frequently repeating the lines about a white scepter stone. Confucius gave him the daughter of his elder brother to wife.","nán róng sān fù bái guī， kǒng zǐ yǐ qí xiōng zhī zǐ qì zhī。","南容三復白圭，孔子以其兄之子妻之。"
"1983","When Yen Yuan died, Yen Lu begged the carriage of the Master to sell and get an outer shell for his son's coffin.","yán yuān sǐ， yán lù qǐng zǐ zhī chē， yǐ wéi zhī (guo3 mù+xiǎng， yǔ guǒ tóng)。","顏淵死，顏路請子之車，以為之(guo3木+享，與槨同)。"
"1984","When Yen Yuan died, the Master said, 'Alas! Heaven is destroying me! Heaven is destroying me!","yán yuān sǐ， zǐ yuē， yī， tiān sàng yǔ， tiān sàng yǔ。","顏淵死，子曰，噫，天喪予，天喪予。"
"1985","When Yen Yuan died, the Master bewailed him exceedingly, and the disciples who were with him said, 'Master, your grief is excessive?","yán yuān sǐ， zǐ kū zhī tòng， cóng zhě yuē， zǐ tòng yǐ。","顏淵死，子哭之慟，從者曰，子慟矣。"
"1986","Is it excessive?' said he.","yuē， yǒu tòng hū。","曰，有慟乎。"
"1987","If I am not to mourn bitterly for this man, for whom should I mourn?","fēi fú rén zhī wèi tòng ér shuí wèi。","非夫人之為慟而誰為。"
"1988","When Yen Yuan died, the disciples wished to give him a great funeral, and the Master said, 'You may not do so.","yán yuān sǐ， mén rén yù hòu zàng zhī。 zǐ yuē， bù kě。","顏淵死，門人慾厚葬之。子曰，不可。"
"1989","The disciples did bury him in great style.","mén rén hòu zàng zhī。","門人厚葬之。"
"1990","He said, 'Yu, there! - he will not die a natural death.","ruò yóu yě， bù dé qí sǐ rán。","若由也，不得其死然。"
"1991","Some parties in Lu were going to take down and rebuild the Long Treasury.","lǔ rén wéi cháng fǔ。","魯人為長府。"
"1992","Min Tsze-ch'ien said, 'Suppose it were to be repaired after its old style; - why must it be altered and made anew?","mǐn zǐ qiān yuē， réng jiù guàn， rú zhī hé， hé bì gǎi zuò。","閔子騫曰，仍舊貫，如之何，何必改作。"
"1993","The Master said, 'This man seldom speaks; when he does, he is sure to hit the point.","zǐ yuē， fú rén bù yán， yán bì yǒu zhòng。","子曰，夫人不言，言必有中。"
"1994","The Master said, 'What has the lute of Yu to do in my door?","zǐ yuē， yóu zhī sè， xī wéi yú qiū zhī mén。","子曰，由之瑟，奚為於丘之門。"
"1995","The other disciples began not to respect Tsze-lu. The Master said, 'Yu has ascended to the hall, though he has not yet passed into the inner apartments.","mén rén bù jìng zǐ lù。 zǐ yuē， yóu yě， shēng táng yǐ， wèi rù yú shì yě。","門人不敬子路。子曰，由也，升堂矣，未入於室也。"
"1996","Tsze-kung asked which of the two, Shih or Shang, was the superior. The Master said, 'Shih goes beyond the due mean, and Shang does not come up to it.","zǐ gòng wèn shī yǔ shāng yě shú xián。 zǐ yuē， shī yě guò， shāng yě bù jí。","子貢問師與商也孰賢。子曰，師也過，商也不及。"
"1997","Then,' said Tsze-kung, 'the superiority is with Shih, I suppose.","yuē， rán zé shī yù yú。","曰，然則師愈與。"
"1998","The Master said, 'To go beyond is as wrong as to fall short.","zǐ yuē， guò yóu bù jí。","子曰，過猶不及。"
"1999","The head of the Chi family was richer than the duke of Chau had been, and yet Ch'iu collected his imposts for him, and increased his wealth.","lǐ shì fù yú zhōu gōng， ér qiú yě wèi zhī jù liǎn ér fù yì zhī。","李氏富於周公，而求也為之聚斂而附益之。"
"2000","The Master said, 'He is no disciple of mine. My little children, beat the drum and assail him.","zǐ yuē， fēi wú tú yě， xiǎo zǐ， míng gǔ ér gōng zhī kě yě。","子曰，非吾徒也，小子，鳴鼓而攻之可也。"
"2001","Ch'ai is simple.","chái yě yú。","柴也愚。"
"2002","Shan is dull.","cān yě lǔ。","參也魯。"
"2003","Shih is specious.","shī yě pì。","師也闢。"
"2004","Yu is coarse.","yóu yě yàn。","由也喭。"
"2005","The Master said, 'There is Hui! He has nearly attained to perfect virtue. He is often in want.","zǐ yuē， huí yě qí shù hū lǚ kōng。","子曰，回也奇庶乎屢空。"
"2006","Ts'ze does not acquiesce in the appointments of Heaven, and his goods are increased by him. Yet his judgments are often correct.","cì bù shòu mìng， ér huò zhí yān， yì zé lǚ zhòng。","賜不受命，而貨殖焉，億則屢中。"
"2007","Chi Tsze-zan asked whether Chung Yu and Zan Ch'iu could be called great ministers.","lǐ zǐ rán wèn zhòng yóu rǎn qiú， kě wèi dà chén yú。","李子然問仲由冉求，可謂大臣與。"
"2008","The Master said, 'I thought you would ask about some extraordinary individuals, and you only ask about Yu and Ch'iu!","zǐ yuē， wú yǐ zǐ wéi yì zhī wèn， zēng yóu yǔ qiú zhī wèn。","子曰，吾以子為異之問，曾由與求之問。"
"2009","What is called a great minister, is one who serves his prince according to what is right, and when he finds he cannot do so, retires.","suǒ wèi dà chén zhě， yǐ dào shì jūn， bù kě zé zhǐ。","所謂大臣者，以道事君，不可則止。"
"2010","Now, as to Yu and Ch'iu, they may be called ordinary ministers.","jīn yóu yǔ qiú yě， kě wèi jù chén yǐ。","今由與求也，可謂具臣矣。"
"2011","Tsze-zan said, 'Then they will always follow their chief; - will they?","yuē， rán zé cóng zhī zhě yú。","曰，然則從之者與。"
"2012","The Master said, 'In an act of parricide or regicide, they would not follow him.","zǐ yuē， shì fù yǔ jūn， yì bù cóng yě。","子曰，弒父與君，亦不從也。"
"2013","Tsze-lu got Tsze-kao appointed governor of Pi.","zǐ lù shǐ zǐ gāo wéi fèi zǎi。","子路使子羔為費宰。"
"2014","The Master said, 'You are injuring a man's son.","zǐ yuē， zéi fū rén zhī zǐ。","子曰，賊夫人之子。"
"2015","The Master said, 'It is on this account that I hate your glib-tongued people.","zǐ yuē， shì gù wù fú nìng zhě。","子曰，是故惡夫佞者。"
"2016","Tsze-lu, Tsang Hsi, Zan Yu, and Kung-hsi Hwa were sitting by the Master.","zǐ lù， zēng shāng xī xià rì， yǔ xī tóng， rǎn yǒu， gōng xī huá， shì zuò。","子路，曾(上析，下日，與晰同)，冉有，公西華，侍坐。"
"2017","He said to them, 'Though I am a day or so older than you, do not think of that.","zǐ yuē， yǐ wú yí rì zhǎng hū ěr， wú wú yǐ yě。","子曰，以吾一日長乎爾，毋吾以也。"
"2018","From day to day you are saying, ""We are not known."" If some ruler were to know you, what would you like to do?","jū zé yuē， bù wú zhī yě， rú huò zhī ěr， zé hé yǐ zāi。","居則曰，不吾知也，如或知爾，則何以哉。"
"2019","Hsi pursued, 'Master, why did you smile at Yu?","yuē， fū zǐ hé shěn yóu yě。","曰，夫子何哂由也。"
"2020","He was answered, 'The management of a State demands the rules of propriety. His words were not humble; therefore I smiled at him.","yuē， wéi guó yǐ lǐ， qí yán bù ràng， shì gù shěn zhī。","曰，為國以禮，其言不讓，是故哂之。"
"2021","Sze-ma Niu asked about perfect virtue.","sī mǎ niú wèn rén。","司馬牛問仁。"
"2022","The Master said, 'The man of perfect virtue is cautious and slow in his speech.","zǐ yuē， rén zhě qí yán yě rèn。","子曰，仁者其言也訒。"
"2023","Sze-ma Niu asked about the superior man. The Master said, 'The superior man has neither anxiety nor fear.","sī mǎ niú wèn jūn zǐ。 zǐ yuē， jūn zǐ bù yōu bù jù。","司馬牛問君子。子曰，君子不憂不懼。"
"2024","Being without anxiety or fear!' said Nui; - 'does this constitute what we call the superior man?","yuē， bù yōu bù jù， sī wèi zhī jūn zǐ yǐ hū。","曰，不憂不懼，斯謂之君子矣乎。"
"2025","The Master said, 'When internal examination discovers nothing wrong, what is there to be anxious about, what is there to fear?","zǐ yuē， nèi xǐng bù jiù， fū hé yōu hé jù。","子曰，內省不疚，夫何憂何懼。"
"2026","Sze-ma Niu, full of anxiety, said, 'Other men all have their brothers, I only have not.","sī mǎ niú yōu yuē， rén jiē yǒu xiōng dì， wǒ dú wáng。","司馬牛憂曰，人皆有兄弟，我獨亡。"
"2027","Tsze-hsia said to him, 'There is the following saying which I have heard: -","zǐ xià yuē， shāng wén zhī yǐ。","子夏曰，商聞之矣。"
"2028","Death and life have their determined appointment; riches and honours depend upon Heaven.","sǐ shēng yǒu mìng， fù guì zài tiān。","死生有命，富貴在天。"
"2029","Chi Tsze-ch'ang said, 'In a superior man it is only the substantial qualities which are wanted; - why should we seek for ornamental accomplishments?","jí zǐ chéng yuē， jūn zǐ zhì ér yǐ yǐ， hé yǐ wén wéi。","棘子成曰，君子質而已矣，何以文為。"
"2030","Tsze-kung said, 'Alas! Your words, sir, show you to be a superior man, but four horses cannot overtake the tongue.","zǐ gòng yuē， xī hū fū zǐ zhī shuō， jūn zǐ yě， sì bù jí shé。","子貢曰，惜乎夫子之說，君子也，駟不及舌。"
"2031","The Duke Ai inquired of Yu Zo, saying, 'The year is one of scarcity, and the returns for expenditure are not sufficient; - what is to be done?","āi gōng wèn yú yǒu ruò yuē nián jī， yòng bù zú， rú zhī hé。","哀公問於有若曰年饑，用不足，如之何。"
"2032","Yu Zo replied to him, 'Why not simply tithe the people?","yǒu ruò duì yuē， hé chè hū。","有若對曰，盍徹乎。"
"2033","With two tenths, said the duke, 'I find it not enough; - how could I do with that system of one tenth?","yuē， èr， wú yóu bù zú， rú zhī hé qí chè yě。","曰，二，吾猶不足，如之何其徹也。"
"2034","Yu Zo answered, 'If the people have plenty, their prince will not be left to want alone. If the people are in want, their prince cannot enjoy plenty alone.","duì yuē， bǎi xìng zú， jūn shú yǔ bù zú， bǎi xìng bù zú， jūn shú yǔ zú。","對曰，百姓足，君孰與不足，百姓不足，君孰與足。"
"2035","The Duke Ching, of Ch'i, asked Confucius about government.","qí jǐng gōng wèn zhèng yú kǒng zǐ。","齊景公問政於孔子。"
"2036","Confucius replied, 'There is government, when the prince is prince, and the minister is minister; when the father is father, and the son is son.","kǒng zǐ duì yuē， jūn jūn， chén chén， fù fù， zǐ zǐ。","孔子對曰，君君，臣臣，父父，子子。"
"2037","The Master said, 'Ah! it is Yu, who could with half a word settle litigations!","zǐ yuē， piàn yán kě yǐ zhé yù zhě， qí yóu yě yú。","子曰，片言可以折獄者，其由也與。"
"2038","Tsze-lu never slept over a promise.","zǐ lù wú sù nuò。","子路無宿諾。"
"2039","The Master said, 'In hearing litigations, I am like any other body. What is necessary, however, is to cause the people to have no litigations.","zǐ yuē， tīng sòng， wú yóu rén yě， bì yě， shǐ wú sòng hū。","子曰，聽訟，吾猶人也，必也，使無訟乎。"
"2040","Tsze-chang asked about government. The Master said, 'The art of governing is to keep its affairs before the mind without weariness, and to practise them with undeviating consistency.","zǐ zhāng wèn zhèng。 zǐ yuē， jū zhī wú juàn， xíng zhī yǐ zhōng。","子張問政。子曰，居之無倦，行之以忠。"
"2041","The Master said, 'By extensively studying all learning, and keeping himself under the restraint of the rules of propriety, one may thus likewise not err from what is right.","zǐ yuē， bó xué yú wén， yuē zhī yǐ lǐ， yì kě yǐ fú pàn yǐ fú。","子曰，博學於文，約之以禮，亦可以弗畔矣夫。"
"2042","Chi K'ang asked Confucius about government. Confucius replied, 'To govern means to rectify. If you lead on the people with correctness, who will dare not to be correct?","lǐ kāng zǐ wèn zhèng yú kǒng zǐ。 kǒng zǐ duì yuē， zhèng zhě zhèng yě， zǐ shuài yǐ zhèng， shú gǎn bù zhèng。 lǐ kāng zǐ huàn dào， wèn yú kǒng zǐ。 kǒng zǐ duì yuē， gǒu zǐ zhī bù yù， suī shǎng zhī bù qiè。","李康子問政於孔子。孔子對曰，政者正也，子帥以正，孰敢不正。李康子患盜，問於孔子。孔子對曰，苟子之不欲，雖賞之不竊。"
"2043","Tsze-chang asked, 'What must the officer be, who may be said to be distinguished?","zǐ zhāng wèn shì hé rú， sī kě wèi zhī dá yǐ。","子張問士何如，斯可謂之達矣。"
"2044","The Master said, 'What is it you call being distinguished?","zǐ yuē， hé zāi， ěr suǒ wèi dá zhě。","子曰，何哉，爾所謂達者。"
"2045","Tsze-chang replied, 'It is to be heard of through the State, to be heard of throughout his clan.","zǐ zhāng duì yuē， zài bāng bì wén， zài jiā bì wén。","子張對曰，在邦必聞，在家必聞。"
"2046","The Master said, 'That is notoriety, not distinction.","zǐ yuē， shì wén yě， fēi dá yě。","子曰，是聞也，非達也。"
"2047","Fan Ch'ih asked about benevolence. The Master said, 'It is to love all men.' He asked about knowledge. The Master said, 'It is to know all men.","fán chí wèn rén。 zǐ yuē， ài rén。 wèn zhì。 zǐ yuē， zhī rén。","樊遲問仁。子曰，愛人。問知。子曰，知人。"
"2048","Fan Ch'ih did not immediately understand these answers.","fán chí wèi dá。","樊遲未達。"
"2049","The Master said, 'Employ the upright and put aside all the crooked; - in this way the crooked can be made to be upright.","zǐ yuē， jǔ zhí cuò zhū wǎng， néng shǐ wǎng zhě zhí。","子曰，舉直錯諸枉，能使枉者直。"
"2050","Tsze-hsia said, 'Truly rich is his saying!","zǐ xià yuē， fù zāi yán hū。","子夏曰，富哉言乎。"
"2051","Tsze-kung asked about friendship. The Master said, 'Faithfully admonish your friend, and skillfully lead him on. If you find him impracticable, stop. Do not disgrace yourself.","zǐ gòng wèn yǒu。 zǐ yuē， zhōng gào ér shàn dǎo zhī， bù kě zé zhǐ， wú zì rǔ yān。","子貢問友。子曰，忠告而善道之，不可則止，毋自辱焉。"
"2052","The philosopher Tsang said, 'The superior man on grounds of culture meets with his friends, and by their friendship helps his virtue.","zēng zǐ yuē， jūn zǐ yǐ wén huì yǒu， yǐ yǒu fǔ rén。 zǐ lù dì shí sān","曾子曰，君子以文會友，以友輔仁。子路第十三"
"2053","Tsze-lu asked about government. The Master said, 'Go before the people with your example, and be laborious in their affairs.","zǐ lù wèn zhèng。 zǐ yuē， xiān zhī， láo zhī。","子路問政。子曰，先之，勞之。"
"2054","He requested further instruction, and was answered, 'Be not weary (in these things).","qǐng yì。 yuē， wú juàn。","請益。曰，無倦。"
"2055","Tsze-lu said, 'The ruler of Wei has been waiting for you, in order with you to administer the government. What will you consider the first thing to be done?","zǐ lù yuē， wèi jūn dài zǐ ér wéi zhèng， zǐ jiāng xī xiān。","子路曰，衛君待子而為政，子將奚先。"
"2056","The Master replied, 'What is necessary is to rectify names.","zǐ yuē， bì yě， zhèng míng hū。","子曰，必也，正名乎。"
"2057","So, indeed!' said Tsze-lu. 'You are wide of the mark! Why must there be such rectification?","zǐ lù yuē， yǒu shì zāi， zǐ zhī yū yě， xī qí zhèng。","子路曰，有是哉，子之迂也，奚其正。"
"2058","The Master said, 'How uncultivated you are, Yu! A superior man, in regard to what he does not know, shows a cautious reserve.","zǐ yuē， yě zāi， yóu yě， jūn zǐ yú qí suǒ bù zhī， gài quē rú yě。","子曰，野哉，由也，君子於其所不知，蓋闕如也。"
"2059","Fan Ch'ih having gone out, the Master said, 'A small man, indeed, is Fan Hsu!","fán chí chū。 zǐ yuē， xiǎo rén zāi， fán xū yě。","樊遲出。子曰，小人哉，樊須也。"
"2060","The Master said, 'The governments of Lu and Wei are brothers.","zǐ yuē， lǔ wèi zhī zhèng， xiōng dì yě。","子曰，魯衛之政，兄弟也。"
"2061","When the Master went to Wei, Zan Yu acted as driver of his carriage.","zǐ shì wèi， rǎn yǒu pú。","子適衛，冉有僕。"
"2062","The Master observed, 'How numerous are the people!","zǐ yuē， shù yǐ zāi。","子曰，庶矣哉。"
"2063","Yu said, 'Since they are thus numerous, what more shall be done for them?' 'Enrich them,' was the reply.","rǎn yǒu yuē， jì shù yǐ， yòu hé jiā yān。 yuē， fù zhī。","冉有曰，既庶矣，又何加焉。曰，富之。"
"2064","And when they have been enriched, what more shall be done?' The Master said, 'Teach them.","yuē， jì fù yǐ， yòu hé jiā yān。 yuē， jiào zhī。","曰，既富矣，又何加焉。曰，教之。"
"2065","The Master said, 'If a truly royal ruler were to arise, it would still require a generation, and then virtue would prevail.","zǐ yuē， rú yǒu wàng zhě， bì shì ér hòu rén。","子曰，如有王者，必世而後仁。"
"2066","The Duke Ting asked whether there was a single sentence which could make a country prosperous. Confucius replied, 'Such an effect cannot be expected from one sentence.","dìng gōng wèn yì yán ér kě yǐ xīng bāng， yǒu zhū。 kǒng zǐ duì yuē， yán bù kě yǐ ruò shì qí jī yě。","定公問一言而可以興邦，有諸。孔子對曰，言不可以若是其幾也。"
"2067","There is a saying, however, which people have - ""To be a prince is difficult; to be a minister is not easy.","rén zhī yán yuē， wéi jūn nán， wéi chén bù yì。","人之言曰，為君難，為臣不易。"
"2068","If a ruler knows this, - the difficulty of being a prince, - may there not be expected from this one sentence the prosperity of his country?","rú zhī wéi jūn zhī nán yě， bù jī hū yì yán ér xīng bāng hū。","如知為君之難也，不幾乎一言而興邦乎。"
"2069","The Duke of Sheh asked about government.","yè gōng wèn zhèng。","葉公問政。"
"2070","The Master said, 'Good government obtains, when those who are near are made happy, and those who are far off are attracted.","zǐ yuē， jìn zhě yuè， yuǎn zhě lái。","子曰，近者說，遠者來。"
"2071","The Master said, 'The people of the south have a saying - ""A man without constancy cannot be either a wizard or a doctor."" Good!","zǐ yuē， nán rén yǒu yán yuē， rén ér wú héng， bù kě yǐ zuò wū yī， shàn fú。","子曰，南人有言曰，人而無恆，不可以作巫醫，善夫。"
"2072","Inconstant in his virtue, he will be visited with disgrace.","bù héng qí dé， huò chéng zhī xiū。","不恆其德，或承之羞。"
"2073","The Master said, 'This arises simply from not attending to the prognostication.","zǐ yuē， bù zhān ér yǐ yǐ。","子曰，不佔而已矣。"
"2074","The Master said, 'The superior man is affable, but not adulatory; the mean man is adulatory, but not affable.","zǐ yuē， jūn zǐ hé ér bù tóng， xiǎo rén tóng ér bù hé。","子曰，君子和而不同，小人同而不和。"
"2075","The Master said, 'The superior man has a dignified ease without pride. The mean man has pride without a dignified ease.","zǐ yuē， jūn zǐ tài ér bù jiāo， xiǎo rén jiāo ér bù tài。","子曰，君子泰而不驕，小人驕而不泰。"
"2076","The Master said, 'The firm, the enduring, the simple, and the modest are near to virtue.","zǐ yuē， gāng， yì， mù， nè， jìn rén。","子曰，剛，毅，木，訥，近仁。"
"2077","The Master said, 'Let a good man teach the people seven years, and they may then likewise be employed in war.","zǐ yuē， shàn rén jiào mín qī nián， yì kě yǐ jí róng yǐ。","子曰，善人教民七年，亦可以即戎矣。"
"2078","The Master said, 'To lead an uninstructed people to war, is to throw them away.","zǐ yuē， yǐ bù jiào mín zhàn， shì wèi qì zhī。 xiàn wèn dì shí sì","子曰，以不教民戰，是謂棄之。憲問第十四"
"2079","When the love of superiority, boasting, resentments, and covetousness are repressed, this may be deemed perfect virtue.","kè， fá， yuàn， yù， bù xíng yān， kě yǐ wéi rén yǐ。","克，伐，怨，欲，不行焉，可以為仁矣。"
"2080","The Master said, 'This may be regarded as the achievement of what is difficult. But I do not know that it is to be deemed perfect virtue.","zǐ yuē， kě yǐ wéi nàn yǐ， rén zé wú bù zhī yě。","子曰，可以為難矣，仁則吾不知也。"
"2081","The Master said, 'The scholar who cherishes the love of comfort is not fit to be deemed a scholar.","zǐ yuē， shì ér huái jū， bù zú yǐ wéi shì yǐ。","子曰，士而懷居，不足以為士矣。"
"2082","The Master said, 'Superior men, and yet not always virtuous, there have been, alas! But there never has been a mean man, and, at the same time, virtuous.","zǐ yuē， jūn zǐ ér bù rén zhě yǒu yǐ fú， wèi yǒu xiǎo rén ér rén zhě yě。","子曰，君子而不仁者有矣夫，未有小人而仁者也。"
"2083","The Master said, 'Can there be love which does not lead to strictness with its object? Can there be loyalty which does not lead to the instruction of its object?","zǐ yuē， ài zhī， néng wù láo hū， zhōng yān， néng wù huì hū。","子曰，愛之，能勿勞乎，忠焉，能勿誨乎。"
"2084","Some one asked about Tsze-ch'an. The Master said, 'He was a kind man.","huò wèn zǐ chǎn。 zǐ yuē， huì rén yě。","或問子產。子曰，惠人也。"
"2085","He asked about Tsze-hsi. The Master said, 'That man! That man!","wèn zǐ xī。 yuē， bǐ zāi bǐ zāi。","問子西。曰，彼哉彼哉。"
"2086","The Master said, 'To be poor without murmuring is difficult. To be rich without being proud is easy.","zǐ yuē， pín ér wú yuàn， nán， fù ér wú jiāo， yì。","子曰，貧而無怨，難，富而無驕，易。"
"2087","The Master asked Kung-ming Chia about Kung-shu Wan, saying, 'Is it true that your master speaks not, laughs not, and takes not?","zǐ wèn gōng shū wén zǐ yú gōng míng jiǎ yuē， xìn hū， fū zǐ bú yán bú xiào， bú qǔ hū。","子問公叔文子於公明賈曰，信乎，夫子不言不笑，不取乎。"
"2088","The Master said, 'The duke Wan of Tsin was crafty and not upright. The duke Hwan of Ch'i was upright and not crafty.","zǐ yuē， jìn wén gōng jué ér bù zhèng， qí huán gōng zhèng ér bù jué。","子曰，晉文公譎而不正，齊桓公正而不譎。"
"2089","Will you require from him the small fidelity of common men and common women, who would commit suicide in a stream or ditch, no one knowing anything about them?","qǐ ruò pǐ fū pǐ fù zhī wéi liàng yě， zì jīng yú gōu dú， ér mò zhī zhī yě。","豈若匹夫匹婦之為諒也，自經於溝瀆，而莫之知也。"
"2090","The great officer, Hsien, who had been family-minister to Kung-shu Wan, ascended to the prince's court in company with Wan.","gōng shū wén zǐ zhī chén， dà fū zhuàn， yǔ wén zǐ tóng shēng zhū gōng。","公叔文子之臣，大夫僎，與文子同升諸公。"
"2091","The Master, having heard of it, said, 'He deserved to be considered WAN (the accomplished).","zǐ wén zhī yuē， kě yǐ wéi yǐ。","子聞之曰，可以為矣。"
"2092","The Master said, 'He who speaks without modesty will find it difficult to make his words good.","zǐ yuē， qí yán zhī bù zuò， zé wéi zhī yě nán。","子曰，其言之不怍，則為之也難。"
"2093","Tsze-lu asked how a ruler should be served. The Master said, 'Do not impose on him, and, moreover, withstand him to his face.","zǐ lù wèn shì jūn。 zǐ yuē， wù qī yě， ér fàn zhī。","子路問事君。子曰，勿欺也，而犯之。"
"2094","The Master said, 'The progress of the superior man is upwards; the progress of the mean man is downwards.","zǐ yuē， jūn zǐ shàng dá， xiǎo rén xià dá。","子曰，君子上達，小人下達。"
"2095","The Master said, 'In ancient times, men learned with a view to their own improvement. Now-a-days, men learn with a view to the approbation of others.","zǐ yuē， gǔ zhī xué zhě wèi jǐ， jīn zhī xué zhě wèi rén。","子曰，古之學者為己，今之學者為人。"
"2096","Chu Po-yu sent a messenger with friendly inquiries to Confucius.","qú bó yù shǐ rén yú kǒng zǐ。","蘧伯玉使人於孔子。"
"2097","The philosopher Tsang said, 'The superior man, in his thoughts, does not go out of his place.","zēng zǐ yuē， jūn zǐ sī bù chū qí wèi。","曾子曰，君子思不出其位。"
"2098","The Master said, 'The superior man is modest in his speech, but exceeds in his actions.","zǐ yuē， jūn zǐ chǐ qí yán ér guò qí xíng。","子曰，君子恥其言而過其行。"
"2099","Tsze-kung said, 'Master, that is what you yourself say.","zǐ gòng yuē， fū zǐ zì dào yě。","子貢曰，夫子自道也。"
"2100","Tsze-kung was in the habit of comparing men together. The Master said, 'Tsze must have reached a high pitch of excellence! Now, I have not leisure for this.","zǐ gòng fāng rén， zǐ yuē， cì yě xián hū zāi， fú wǒ zé bù xiá。","子貢方人，子曰，賜也賢乎哉，夫我則不暇。"
"2101","The Master said, 'I will not be concerned at men's not knowing me; I will be concerned at my own want of ability.","zǐ yuē， bù huàn rén zhī bù jǐ zhī， huàn qí bù néng yě。","子曰，不患人之不己知，患其不能也。"
"2102","Wei-shang Mau said to Confucius, 'Ch'iu, how is it that you keep roosting about? Is it not that you are an insinuating talker?","wēi shēng mǔ wèi kǒng zǐ yuē， qiū hé wéi shì qī qī zhě yú， wú nǎi wéi nìng hū。","微生畝謂孔子曰，丘何為是棲棲者與，無乃為佞乎。"
"2103","Confucius said, 'I do not dare to play the part of such a talker, but I hate obstinacy.","kǒng zǐ yuē， fēi gǎn wéi nìng yě， jí gù yě。","孔子曰，非敢為佞也，疾固也。"
"2104","The Master said, 'A horse is called a ch'i, not because of its strength, but because of its other good qualities.","zǐ yuē， jì， bù chēng qí lì， chēng qí dé yě。","子曰，驥，不稱其力，稱其德也。"
"2105","Some one said, 'What do you say concerning the principle that injury should be recompensed with kindness?","huò yuē， yǐ dé bào yuàn， hé rú。","或曰，以德報怨，何如。"
"2106","The Master said, 'With what then will you recompense kindness?","zǐ yuē， hé yǐ bào dé。","子曰，何以報德。"
"2107","Recompense injury with justice, and recompense kindness with kindness.","yǐ zhí bào yuàn， yǐ dé bào dé。","以直報怨，以德報德。"
"2108","The Master said, 'Alas! there is no one that knows me.","zǐ yuē， mò wǒ zhī yě fú。","子曰，莫我知也夫。"
"2109","The Master said, 'Some men of worth retire from the world.","zǐ yuē， xián zhě pì shì。","子曰，賢者辟世。"
"2110","Some retire from particular states.","qí cì pì dì。","其次辟地。"
"2111","Some retire because of disrespectful looks.","qí cì pì sè。","其次辟色。"
"2112","Some retire because of contradictory language.","qí cì pì yán。","其次辟言。"
"2113","The Master said, 'Those who have done this are seven men.","zǐ yuē， zuò zhě qī rén yǐ。","子曰，作者七人矣。"

"2115","Tsze-chang said, 'What is meant when the Shu says that Kao-tsung, while observing the usual imperial mourning, was for three years without speaking?","zǐ zhāng yuē， shū yún， gāo zōng liàng yīn sān nián bù yán， hé wèi yě。","子張曰，書雲，高宗諒陰三年不言，何謂也。"
"2116","The Master said, 'When rulers love to observe the rules of propriety, the people respond readily to the calls on them for service.","zǐ yuē， shàng hào lǐ， zé mín yì shǐ yě。","子曰，上好禮，則民易使也。"
"2117","When he was in Chan, their provisions were exhausted, and his followers became so ill that they were unable to rise.","zài chén jué liáng， cóng zhě bìng， mò néng xīng。","在陳絕糧，從者病，莫能興。"
"2118","The Master said, 'Ts'ze, you think, I suppose, that I am one who learns many things and keeps them in memory?","zǐ yuē， cì yě， rǔ yǐ yǔ wéi duō xué ér zhì zhī zhě yú。","子曰，賜也，女以予為多學而識之者與。"
"2119","Tsze-kung replied, 'Yes, - but perhaps it is not so?","duì yuē， rán， fēi yú。","對曰，然，非與。"

"2122","The Master said, 'May not Shun be instanced as having governed efficiently without exertion? What did he do? He did nothing but gravely and reverently occupy his royal seat.","zǐ yuē， wú wéi ér zhì zhě， qí shùn yě yú， fú hé wéi zāi， gōng jǐ zhèng nán miàn ér yǐ yǐ。","子曰，無為而治者，其舜也與，夫何為哉，恭己正南面而已矣。"
"2123","Tsze-chang asked how a man should conduct himself, so as to be everywhere appreciated.","zǐ zhāng wèn xíng。","子張問行。"

"2125","The Master said, 'Truly straightforward was the historiographer Yu. When good government prevailed in his State, he was like an arrow. When bad government prevailed, he was like an arrow.","zǐ yuē， zhí zāi shǐ yú， bāng yǒu dào rú shǐ， bāng yǒu dào rú shǐ。","子曰，直哉史魚，邦有道如矢，邦有道如矢。"
"2126","Yen Yuan asked how the government of a country should be administered.","yán yuān wèn wéi bāng。","顏淵問為邦。"
"2127","The Master said, 'Follow the seasons of Hsia.","zǐ yuē， xíng xià zhī shí。","子曰，行夏之時。"
"2128","Ride in the state carriage of Yin.","shèng yīn zhī lù。","乘殷之輅。"
"2129","Wear the ceremonial cap of Chau.","fú zhōu zhī miǎn。","服周之冕。"
"2130","Let the music be the Shao with its pantomimes.","yuè zé sháo wǔ。","樂則韶舞。"
"2131","Banish the songs of Chang, and keep far from specious talkers. The songs of Chang are licentious; specious talkers are dangerous.","fàng zhèng shēng， yuǎn nìng rén， zhèng shēng yín， nìng rén dài。","放鄭聲，遠佞人，鄭聲淫，佞人殆。"
"2132","The Master said, 'If a man take no thought about what is distant, he will find sorrow near at hand.","zǐ yuē， rén wú yuǎn lǜ， bì yǒu jìn yōu。","子曰，人無遠慮，必有近憂。"
"2133","The Master said, 'It is all over! I have not seen one who loves virtue as he loves beauty.","zǐ yuē， yǐ yǐ hū， wú wèi jiàn hào dé rú hào sè zhě yě。","子曰，已矣乎，吾未見好德如好色者也。"
"2134","The Master said, 'He who requires much from himself and little from others, will keep himself from being the object of resentment.","zǐ yuē， gōng zì hòu， ér báo zé yú rén， zé yuǎn yuàn yǐ。","子曰，躬自厚，而薄責於人，則遠怨矣。"
"2135","The Master said, 'The superior man is distressed by his want of ability. He is not distressed by men's not knowing him.","zǐ yuē， jūn zǐ bìng wú néng yān， bù bìng rén zhī bù jǐ zhī yě。","子曰，君子病無能焉，不病人之不己知也。"
"2136","The Master said, 'The superior man dislikes the thought of his name not being mentioned after his death.","zǐ yuē， jūn zǐ jí mò shì， ér míng bù chēng yān。","子曰，君子疾沒世，而名不稱焉。"
"2137","The Master said, 'What the superior man seeks, is in himself. What the mean man seeks, is in others.","zǐ yuē， jūn zǐ qiú zhū jǐ， xiǎo rén qiú zhū rén。","子曰，君子求諸己，小人求諸人。"
"2138","The Master said, 'The superior man is dignified, but does not wrangle. He is sociable, but not a partizan.","zǐ yuē， jūn zǐ jīn ér bù zhēng， qún ér bù dǎng。","子曰，君子矜而不爭，群而不黨。"
"2139","The Master said, 'The superior man does not promote a man simply on account of his words, nor does he put aside good words because of the man.","zǐ yuē， jūn zǐ bù yǐ yán jǔ rén， bù yǐ rén fèi yán。","子曰，君子不以言舉人，不以人廢言。"
"2140","This people supplied the ground why the three dynasties pursued the path of straightforwardness.","sī mín yě， sān dài zhī suǒ yǐ zhí dào ér xíng yě。","斯民也，三代之所以直道而行也。"
"2141","The Master said, 'Specious words confound virtue. Want of forbearance in small matters confounds great plans.","zǐ yuē， qiǎo yán luàn dé， xiǎo bù rěn， zé luàn dà móu。","子曰，巧言亂德，小不忍，則亂大謀。"
"2142","The Master said, 'A man can enlarge the principles which he follows; those principles do not enlarge the man.","zǐ yuē， rén néng hóng dào， fēi dào hóng rén。","子曰，人能弘道，非道弘人。"

"2144","When his knowledge is sufficient to attain, and he has virtue enough to hold fast, if he cannot govern with dignity, the people will not respect him.","zhī jí zhī， rén néng shǒu zhī， bù zhuāng yǐ lì zhī， zé mín bù jìng。","知及之，仁能守之，不莊以蒞之，則民不敬。"

"2146","The Master said, 'The superior man is correctly firm, and not firm merely.","zǐ yuē， jūn zǐ zhēn， ér bù liàng。","子曰，君子貞，而不諒。"
"2147","The Master said, 'A minister, in serving his prince, reverently discharges his duties, and makes his emolument a secondary consideration.","zǐ yuē， shì jūn jìng qí shì， ér hòu qí shí。","子曰，事君敬其事，而後其食。"
"2148","The Master said, 'In teaching there should be no distinction of classes.","zǐ yuē， yǒu jiào， wú lèi。","子曰，有教，無類。"
"2149","The Master said, 'Those whose courses are different cannot lay plans for one another.","zǐ yuē， dào bù tóng， bù xiāng wéi móu。","子曰，道不同，不相為謀。"
"2150","The Master said, 'In language it is simply required that it convey the meaning.","zǐ yuē， cí， dá ér yǐ yǐ。","子曰，辭，達而已矣。"
"2151","The Music-master, Mien, having gone out, Tsze-chang asked, saying. 'Is it the rule to tell those things to the Music- master?","shī miǎn chū， zǐ zhāng wèn yuē， yǔ shī yán zhī dào yú。","師冕出，子張問曰，與師言之道與。"

"2153","The head of the Chi family was going to attack Chwan-yu.","lǐ shì jiāng fá zhuān yú。","李氏將伐顓臾。"
"2154","Zan Yu and Chi-lu had an interview with Confucius, and said, 'Our chief, Chi, is going to commence operations against Chwan-yu.","rǎn yǒu lǐ lù jiàn yú kǒng zǐ yuē， lǐ shì jiāng yǒu shì yú zhuān yú。","冉有李路見於孔子曰，李氏將有事於顓臾。"
"2155","Confucius said, 'Ch'iu, is it not you who are in fault here?","kǒng zǐ yuē， qiú， wú nǎi ěr shì guò yú。","孔子曰，求，無乃爾是過與。"
"2156","Zan Yu said, 'Our master wishes the thing; neither of us two ministers wishes it.","rǎn yǒu yuē， fū zǐ yù zhī， wú èr chén zhě， jiē bù yù yě。","冉有曰，夫子欲之，吾二臣者，皆不欲也。"
"2157","Zan Yu said, 'But at present, Chwan-yu is strong and near to Pi; if our chief do not now take it, it will hereafter be a sorrow to his descendants.","rǎn yǒu yuē， jīn fú zhuān yú， gù ér jìn yú fèi， jīn bù qǔ， hòu shì bì wéi zǐ sūn yōu。","冉有曰，今夫顓臾，固而近於費，今不取，後世必為子孫憂。"

"2159","When right principles prevail in the kingdom, government will not be in the hands of the Great officers.","tiān xià yǒu dào， zé zhèng bù zài dài fū。","天下有道，則政不在大夫。"
"2160","When right principles prevail in the kingdom, there will be no discussions among the common people.","tiān xià yǒu dào， zé shù rén bù yì。","天下有道，則庶人不議。"
"2161","Living in retirement to study their aims, and practising righteousness to carry out their principles: - I have heard these words, but I have not seen such men.","yǐn jū yǐ qiú qí zhì， xíng yì yǐ dá qí dào， wú wén qí yǔ yǐ， wèi jiàn qí rén yě。","隱居以求其志，行義以達其道，吾聞其語矣，未見其人也。"
"2162","Is not that saying illustrated by this?","qí sī zhī wèi yú。","其斯之謂與。"
"2163","Ch'an K'ang asked Po-yu, saying, 'Have you heard any lessons from your father different from what we have all heard?","chén kàng wèn yú bó yú yuē， zǐ yì yǒu yì wén hū。","陳亢問於伯魚曰，子亦有異聞乎。"
"2164","I have heard only these two things from him.","wén sī èr zhě。","聞斯二者。"
"2165","The Master said, 'By nature, men are nearly alike; by practice, they get to be wide apart.","zǐ yuē， xìng xiāng jìn yě， xí xiāng yuǎn yě。","子曰，性相近也，習相遠也。"
"2166","The Master said, 'There are only the wise of the highest class, and the stupid of the lowest class, who cannot be changed.","zǐ yuē， wéi shàng zhī yǔ xià yú bù yí。","子曰，唯上知與下愚不移。"
"2167","The Master, having come to Wu-ch'ang, heard there the sound of stringed instruments and singing.","zǐ zhī wǔ chéng， wén xián gē zhī shēng。","子之武城，聞弦歌之聲。"
"2168","Well pleased and smiling, he said, 'Why use an ox knife to kill a fowl?","fū zǐ wǎn ěr ér xiào yuē， gē jī yān yòng niú dāo。","夫子莞爾而笑曰，割雞焉用牛刀。"
"2169","The Master said, 'My disciples, Yen's words are right. What I said was only in sport.","zǐ yuē， èr sān zǐ， yǎn zhī yán shì yě， qián yán xì zhī ěr。","子曰，二三子，偃之言是也，前言戲之耳。"
"2170","Pi Hsi inviting him to visit him, the Master was inclined to go.","fó xī zhào。 zǐ yù wǎng。","佛肸召。子欲往。"
"2171","Am I a bitter gourd! How can I be hung up out of the way of being eaten?","wú qǐ páo guā yě zāi， yān néng xì ér bù shí。","吾豈匏瓜也哉，焉能繫而不食。"
"2172","The Master said, 'Yu, have you heard the six words to which are attached six becloudings?' Yu replied, 'I have not.","zǐ yuē， yóu yě， rǔ wén liù yán liù bì yǐ hū。 duì yuē， wèi yě。","子曰，由也，女聞六言六蔽矣乎。對曰，未也。"
"2173","Sit down, and I will tell them to you.","jū， wú yù rǔ。","居，吾語女。"
"2174","The Master said, 'My children, why do you not study the Book of Poetry?","zǐ yuē， xiǎo zǐ， hé mò xué fú shī。","子曰，小子，何莫學夫詩。"
"2175","The Odes serve to stimulate the mind.","shī kě yǐ xìng。","詩可以興。"
"2176","They may be used for purposes of self-contemplation.","kě yǐ guān。","可以觀。"
"2177","They teach the art of sociability.","kě yǐ qún。","可以群。"
"2178","They show how to regulate feelings of resentment.","kě yǐ yuàn。","可以怨。"
"2179","From them you learn the more immediate duty of serving one's father, and the remoter one of serving one's prince.","ěr zhī shì fù， yuǎn zhī shì jūn。","邇之事父，遠之事君。"
"2180","From them we become largely acquainted with the names of birds, beasts, and plants.","duō zhì yú niǎo shòu cǎo mù zhī míng。","多識於鳥獸草木之名。"
"2181","The Master said, 'Your good, careful people of the villages are the thieves of virtue.","zǐ yuē， xiāng yuán， dé zhī zéi yě。","子曰，鄉原，德之賊也。"
"2182","The Master said, 'To tell, as we go along, what we have heard on the way, is to cast away our virtue.","zǐ yuē， dào tīng ér tú shuō， dé zhī qì yě。","子曰，道聽而塗說，德之棄也。"
"2183","The Master said, 'There are those mean creatures! How impossible it is along with them to serve one's prince!","zǐ yuē， bǐ fū， kě yǔ shì jūn yě yú zāi。","子曰，鄙夫，可與事君也與哉。"
"2184","While they have not got their aims, their anxiety is how to get them. When they have got them, their anxiety is lest they should lose them.","qí wèi dé zhī yě， huàn dé zhī， jì dé zhī， huàn shī zhī。","其未得之也，患得之，既得之，患失之。"
"2185","When they are anxious lest such things should be lost, there is nothing to which they will not proceed.","gǒu huàn shī zhī， wú suǒ bù zhì yǐ。","苟患失之，無所不至矣。"
"2186","The Master said, 'Anciently, men had three failings, which now perhaps are not to be found.","zǐ yuē， gǔ zhě， mín yǒu sān jí， jīn yě huò shì zhī wáng yě。","子曰，古者，民有三疾，今也或是之亡也。"
"2187","The Master said, 'Fine words and an insinuating appearance are seldom associated with virtue.","zǐ yuē， kǎo yán lìng sè xiǎn yǐ rén。","子曰，攷言令色鮮矣仁。"
"2188","Tsai Wo asked about the three years' mourning for parents, saying that one year was long enough.","zǎi wǒ wèn， sān nián zhī sāng qī yǐ jiǔ yǐ。","宰我問，三年之喪期已久矣。"
"2189","The Master said, 'If you were, after a year, to eat good rice, and wear embroidered clothes, would you feel at ease?' 'I should,' replied Wo.","zǐ yuē， shí fú dào， yì fú jǐn， yú rǔ ān hū。 yuē， ān。","子曰，食夫稻，衣夫錦，於女安乎。曰，安。"
"2190","The Master said, 'When a man at forty is the object of dislike, he will always continue what he is.","zǐ yuē， nián sì shí ér jiàn wù yān， qí zhōng yě yǐ。 wēi zǐ dì shí bā","子曰，年四十而見惡焉，其終也已。微子第十八"
"2191","The Viscount of Wei withdrew from the court. The Viscount of Chi became a slave to Chau. Pi-kan remonstrated with him and died.","wēi zǐ qù zhī， jī zǐ wéi zhī nú， bǐ gān jiàn ér sǐ。","微子去之，箕子為之奴，比干諫而死。"
"2192","Confucius said, 'The Yin dynasty possessed these three men of virtue.","kǒng zǐ yuē， yīn yǒu sān rén yān。","孔子曰，殷有三仁焉。"
"2193","The people of Ch'i sent to Lu a present of female musicians, which Chi Hwan received, and for three days no court was held. Confucius took his departure.","qí rén guī nǚ yuè。 lǐ huán zǐ shòu zhī， sān rì bù cháo， kǒng zǐ xíng。","齊人歸女樂。李桓子受之，三日不朝，孔子行。"
"2194","Confucius alighted and wished to converse with him, but Chieh-yu hastened away, so that he could not talk with him.","kǒng zǐ xià， yù yǔ zhī yán。 qū ér bì zhī， bù dé yǔ zhī yán。","孔子下，欲與之言。趨而闢之，不得與之言。"
"2195","Ch'ang-tsu and Chieh-ni were at work in the field together, when Confucius passed by them, and sent Tsze- lu to inquire for the ford.","cháng jù jié nì ǒu ér gēng。 kǒng zǐ guò zhī， shǐ zǐ lù wèn jīn yān。","長沮桀溺耦而耕。孔子過之，使子路問津焉。"
"2196","Tsze-lu joined his hands across his breast, and stood before him.","zǐ lù gǒng ér lì。","子路拱而立。"
"2197","The old man kept Tsze-lu to pass the night in his house, killed a fowl, prepared millet, and feasted him. He also introduced to him his two sons.","zhǐ zǐ lù sù， shā jī wéi shǔ ér sì zhī， xiàn qí èr zǐ yān。","止子路宿，殺雞為黍而食之，見其二子焉。"
"2198","The men who have retired to privacy from the world have been Po-i, Shu-ch'i, Yu-chung, I-yi, Chu-chang, Hui of Liu-hsia, and Shao-lien.","yì mín， bó yí， shū qí， yú zhòng， yí yì， zhū zhāng， liǔ xià huì， shào lián。","逸民，伯夷，叔齊，虞仲，夷逸，朱張，柳下惠，少連。"
"2199","The Master said, 'Refusing to surrender their wills, or to submit to any taint in their persons; - such, I think, were Po-i and Shu-ch'i.","zǐ yuē， bù jiàng qí zhì， bù rǔ qí shēn， bó yí shū qí yú。","子曰，不降其志，不辱其身，伯夷叔齊與。"
"2200","I am different from all these. I have no course for which I am predetermined, and no course against which I am predetermined.","wǒ zé yì yú shì， wú kě wú bù kě。","我則異於是，無可無不可。"
"2201","To Chau belonged the eight officers, Po-ta, Po-kwo, Chung-tu, Chung-hwu, Shu-ya, Shu-hsia, Chi-sui, and Chi-kwa.","zhōu yǒu bā shì， bó dá， bó shì， zhòng tū， zhòng hū， shū yè， shū xià， lǐ suí， lǐ guā。 zǐ zhāng dì shí jiǔ","周有八士，伯達，伯適，仲突，仲忽，叔夜，叔夏，李隨，李騧。子張第十九"
"2202","Tsze-hsia said, 'There are learning extensively, and having a firm and sincere aim; inquiring with earnestness, and reflecting with self-application: - virtue is in such a course.","zǐ xià yuē， bó xué ér dǔ zhì， qiè wèn ér jìn sī， rén zài qí zhōng yǐ。","子夏曰，博學而篤志，切問而近思，仁在其中矣。"
"2203","Tsze-hsia said, 'Mechanics have their shops to dwell in, in order to accomplish their works. The superior man learns, in order to reach to the utmost of his principles.","zǐ xià yuē， bǎi gōng jū sì， yǐ chéng qí shì， jūn zǐ xué yǐ zhì qí dào。","子夏曰，百工居肆，以成其事，君子學以致其道。"
"2204","Tsze-hsia said, 'The mean man is sure to gloss his faults.","zǐ xià yuē， xiǎo rén zhī guò yě， bì wén。","子夏曰，小人之過也，必文。"
"2205","Tsze-hsia said, 'When a person does not transgress the boundary line in the great virtues, he may pass and repass it in the small virtues.","zǐ xià yuē， dà dé bù yú xián， xiǎo dé chū rù kě yě。","子夏曰，大德不踰閑，小德出入可也。"
"2206","Tsze-hsia said, 'The officer, having discharged all his duties, should devote his leisure to learning. The student, having completed his learning, should apply himself to be an officer.","zǐ xià yuē， shì ér yōu zé xué， xué ér yōu zé shì。","子夏曰，仕而優則學，學而優則仕。"
"2207","Tsze-hsia said, 'Mourning, having been carried to the utmost degree of grief, should stop with that.","zǐ yóu yuē， sāng zhì hū āi ér zhǐ。","子游曰，喪致乎哀而止。"
"2208","Tsze-hsia said, 'My friend Chang can do things which are hard to be done, but yet he is not perfectly virtuous.","zǐ yóu yuē， wú yǒu zhāng yě， wéi nán néng yě， rán ér wèi rén。","子游曰，吾友張也，為難能也，然而未仁。"
"2209","The philosopher Tsang said, 'How imposing is the manner of Chang! It is difficult along with him to practise virtue.","zēng zǐ yuē， táng táng hū zhāng yě， nán yǔ bìng wéi rén yǐ。","曾子曰，堂堂乎張也，難與並為仁矣。"
"2210","Kung-sun Ch'ao of Wei asked Tsze-kung, saying, 'From whom did Chung-ni get his learning?","wèi gōng sūn cháo wèn yú zǐ gòng yuē， zhòng ní yān xué。","衛公孫朝問於子貢曰，仲尼焉學。"
"2211","Shu-sun Wu-shu observed to the great officers in the court, saying, 'Tsze-kung is superior to Chung-ni.","shū sūn wǔ shū yù dài fū yú cháo yuē， zǐ gòng xián yú zhòng ní。","叔孫武叔語大夫於朝曰，子貢賢於仲尼。"
"2212","But I may assume that they are few who find the door. Was not the observation of the chief only what might have been expected?","dé qí mén zhě huò guǎ yǐ， fū zǐ zhī yún， bù yì yí hū。","得其門者或寡矣，夫子之雲，不亦宜乎。"
"2213","Ch'an Tsze-ch'in, addressing Tsze-kung, said, 'You are too modest. How can Chung-ni be said to be superior to you?","chén zǐ qín wèi zǐ gòng yuē， zǐ wéi gōng yě， zhòng ní qǐ xián yú zǐ hū。","陳子禽謂子貢曰，子為恭也，仲尼豈賢於子乎。"
"2214","Shun also used the same language in giving charge to Yu.","shùn yì yǐ mìng yǔ。","舜亦以命禹。"
"2215","Chau conferred great gifts, and the good were enriched.","zhōu yǒu dà lài， shàn rén shì fù。","周有大賚，善人是富。"
"2216","Although he has his near relatives, they are not equal to my virtuous men. The people are throwing blame upon me, the One man.","suī yǒu zhōu qīn， bù rú rén rén， bǎi xìng yǒu guò， zài yú yī rén。","雖有周親，不如仁人，百姓有過，在予一人。"
"2217","He carefully attended to the weights and measures, examined the body of the laws, restored the discarded officers, and the good government of the kingdom took its course.","jǐn quán liàng， shěn fǎ dù， xiū fèi guān， sì fāng zhī zhèng xíng yān。","謹權量，審法度，修廢官，四方之政行焉。"
"2218","What he attached chief importance to, were the food of the people, the duties of mourning, and sacrifices.","suǒ zhòng mín， shí， sàng， jì。","所重民，食，喪，祭。"
"2219","Without an acquaintance with the rules of Propriety, it is impossible for the character to be established.","zǐ zhāng yuē， hé wèi huì ér bù fèi。 zǐ yuē， yīn mín zhī suǒ lì ér lì zhī， sī bù yì huì ér bù fèi hū， zé kě láo ér láo zhī， yòu shuí yuàn， yù rén ér dé rén， yòu yān tān， jūn zǐ wú zhòng guǎ， wú xiǎo dà， wú gǎn màn， sī bù yì tài ér bù jiāo hū， jūn zǐ zhèng qí yī guān， zūn qí zhān shì， yǎn rán rén wàng ér wèi zhī， sī bù yì wēi ér bù měng hū。","子張曰，何謂惠而不費。子曰，因民之所利而利之，斯不亦惠而不費乎，擇可勞而勞之，又誰怨，欲仁而得仁，又焉貪，君子無眾寡，無小大，無敢慢，斯不亦泰而不驕乎，君子正其衣冠，尊其瞻視，儼然人望而畏之，斯不亦威而不猛乎。"
